## ד (id: BDB01975)

ד, דּ, *Dāleth*, fourth letter; in modern Heb. numeral 4; Dâ̌leth (with diaresis) \= 4000; no evidence of this usage in OT times.



## דָּאֵב (id: BDB01976)

\[Str1669] † \[דָּאֵב] **vb.** become faint, languish (NH *id.* Hiph.; cf. Ar. ገሰሰ፡ *toil, weary oneself;* v. also דְּאָבָה, דְּאָבוֺן \& cf. דוב)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. דָּֽאֲבָה [Psalm 88:10](https://ref.ly/Ps88:10) (subj. עַיִן); *Inf.* לְדַאֲבָה [Je 31:12](https://ref.ly/Jer31:12) ´לאֹ יוֺסִיפוּ לְד (subj. ransomed Israel);—[Je 31:25](https://ref.ly/Jer31:25) has נֶפֶשׁ דָּֽאֲבָה, of hunger (‖נֶפֶשׁ עֲיֵפָה), where adj. or pt. seems needed; Hi Gf דְּאֵבָה or דֹּאֲבָה (the punctuators had, acc. to them, the Aram. participle in mind); Thes. makes relative clause.

> \[Str1670] † דְּאָבָה **n.f.** faintness, failure of mental energy, dismay [Jb 41:14](https://ref.ly/Job41:14) וּלְפָנָיו תָּדוּץ דְּאָבָה *and before him* (i.e. the crocodile) *danceth dismay*.

> \[Str1671] † \[דְּאָבוֺן] **n.\[m.]** faintness, languishing, cstr. וְכִלְיוֺן עֵינַיִם וְדַֽאֲבוֺן ָ\+נפֶשׁ [Dt 28:65](https://ref.ly/Deut28:65) (cf. [Lv 26:16](https://ref.ly/Lev26:16) \& Syr. ገሸሸ፡ v. דוב).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 88:10; Jeremiah 31:12; Jeremiah 31:25; Job 41:14; Deuteronomy 28:65; Leviticus 26:16
* **Associated Articles:** דּוּב (ID: `BDB02047@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## דָּאַג (id: BDB01979)

\[Str1672] † דָּאַג **vb.** be anxious, concerned, fear (Talm. דְּאֵיג, דְּאִג *id.*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְדָאַג consec. [1 S 9:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:5), [10:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:2); 2 fs. דָּאַגְתְּ [Is 57:11](https://ref.ly/Isa57:11); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִדְאָג [Je 17:8](https://ref.ly/Jer17:8); אֶדְאַג [Psalm 38:19](https://ref.ly/Ps38:19); *Pt.* דֹּאֵג [Je 38:19](https://ref.ly/Jer38:19), דֹּאֲגִים [Je 42:16](https://ref.ly/Jer42:16); —**1\.** *be anxious, concerned*, with reference to, in behalf of, c. לְ [1 S 9:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:5), [10:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:2); *id.* c. מִן [Je 42:16](https://ref.ly/Jer42:16) (famine personif.); sq. מֵחַטָּאתִי [Psalm 38:19](https://ref.ly/Ps38:19); **be anxious**, abs. [Je 17:8](https://ref.ly/Jer17:8) (‖יָרֵא). **2\.** fear, dread, sq. acc. of Pers. feared [Is 57:11](https://ref.ly/Isa57:11) (‖יָרֵא) [Je 38:19](https://ref.ly/Jer38:19) (where also sq. cl. with פֶּן).

> \[Str1673] † דֹּאֵג **n.pr.m.** an Edomite, servant of Saul [1 S 21:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:8), [22:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:9), [22:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:18), [22:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:18), [22:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:22) (v:[18](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:18), v:[18](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:18), v:[22](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:22) Kt דויג cf. Dr) דּוֺאֵג [Psalm 52:2](https://ref.ly/Ps52:2) (title).

> \[Str1674] † דְּאָגָה **n.f.**anxiety, anxious care—דְּאָגָה [Jos 22:24](https://ref.ly/Josh22:24) \+ 5 t.; *anxiety* for \= for fear of, c. מִן [Jos 22:24](https://ref.ly/Josh22:24); *anxiety* [Pr 12:25](https://ref.ly/Prov12:25) (where c. verb. masc. cf. Now), [Je 49:23](https://ref.ly/Jer49:23) ´בַּיָּם ד; *anxious care* Ez [4:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:16), [12:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:19) (in both ‖שִׁמָּמוֺן), [12:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:18) (‖רַעַשׁ and רָגְזָה).

> \[Str1709] דָּאג *fish*, cf. דָּג sub דגה. p. 185

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 12:19; Jeremiah 42:16; 1 Samuel 22:22; 1 Samuel 21:8; Proverbs 12:25; 1 Samuel 22:9; Ezekiel 12:18; Psalms 52:2; Jeremiah 38:19; Jeremiah 17:8; Isaiah 57:11; 1 Samuel 9:5; 1 Samuel 22:18; Psalms 38:19; Ezekiel 4:16; Jeremiah 49:23; Joshua 22:24; 1 Samuel 10:2


## דָּאָה (id: BDB01983)

\[Str1675] † \[דָּאָה] **vb.** fly swiftly, dart through the air (cf. perhaps Ar. دَأْدَأ *run vehemently* (of camel))—**Qal** *Impf.* יִדְאֶה [Dt 28:49](https://ref.ly/Deut28:49) \+ 2 t.; וַיֵּ֫דֶא [Psalm 18:11](https://ref.ly/Ps18:11)(\> ‖ [2 S 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:11) וַיֵּרָא); *fly swiftly, dart*, of eagle [Dt 28:49](https://ref.ly/Deut28:49), in simile of swift army; of Chaldaeans camp. with eagle, in judgment against Moab \& Edom [Je 48:40](https://ref.ly/Jer48:40), [49:22](https://ref.ly/Jer49:22) (in both ‖פרש כנפיו); of ´י in theoph. [Psalm 18:11](https://ref.ly/Ps18:11) (cf. [2 S 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:11)) וַיֵּדֶא על־כנפי רוח (‖וַיִּרְכַּב עַל־כְּרוּב וַיָּעֹף).

> \[Str1676] † דָּאָה **n.f.** a bird of prey, possibly kite (Vulgate Saad. BoHieroz. ii. 191 Di [Lv 11:14](https://ref.ly/Lev11:14); Aram. דַּיְתָא, ܕܰܝܬܳܐ; NH דַּיָּה of diff. birds of prey; name prob. fr. flying, swooping), [Lv 11:14](https://ref.ly/Lev11:14) forbidden as food; cf. also [Dt 14:13](https://ref.ly/Deut14:13) וְהָרָאָה ואת־הָאַיָּה והַדַּיָּה, where for הראה rd. הַדָּאָה \& del. הַדַּיָּה; so Sam LXX; cf. Di *l. c.*

> \[Str1772] † \[דַּיָּה] **n.f.** id.—frequenting ruins, cf. Di *l. c.;* דַּיּוֺת [Is 34:15](https://ref.ly/Isa34:15) (on הַדַּיָּה [Dt 14:13](https://ref.ly/Deut14:13) cf. foregoing).

> \[Str1756] דּאֹר **n.pr.** v. sub II. דור. p. 190

> \[Str1677] דֹּב, דּוֺב v. דבב. below

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 34:15; Deuteronomy 28:49; Psalms 18:11; Jeremiah 49:22; 2 Samuel 22:11; Leviticus 11:14; Deuteronomy 14:13; Jeremiah 48:40
* **Associated Articles:** דָּבַב (ID: `BDB01990@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## דבא (id: BDB01988)

דבא (? √ of foll.; existence \& mng. dub.)

> \[Str1679] † \[דֹּ֫בֶא] **n.\[m.]** perh. rest, but sense very doubtful (? Ar. بَدَأَ *rest*, Ḳamus; LXX SyrVer Targum Onk *strength*, reading perh. רבאך cf. Sam. רביך, vid. Di) :וּכְיָמֶיךָ דָּבְאֶֽךָ [Dt 33:25](https://ref.ly/Deut33:25).

* **Associated Passages:** Deuteronomy 33:25


## דָּבַב (id: BDB01990)

\[Str1680] † \[דָּבַב] **vb.** move gently, glide, glide over (NH דָּבַב *flow slowly, drop;* Ar. دَبَّ *walk leisurely, gently*, دَابَّةٌ *any animal that walks* or *creeps*, cf. דֹּב; As. *dabâbu, plot, plan*, COTGloss, cf. דִּבָּה)—only **Qal** *Pt.* דּוֺבֵב שִפְתֵי יְשֵׁנִים Ct [7:10](https://ref.ly/Song7:10), of wine *gliding over the lips of sleepers;* (שְפָתַי וְשִׁנָּ\+י ´ד i.e. *my lips and teeth* LXX SyrVer Aq GeiUrschrift 405\).

> \[Str1677] † דֹּב **n.m.**:[Pr 28:15](https://ref.ly/Prov28:15), **f.**:[2 K 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:24) (seld.) bear (from soft or gliding motion, NH *id.*, Eth. ድብ፡ As. *dabû* (?) DlS 55; Aram. דּוּבָּא, ܕܶܒܳܐ; Ar. دُبٌّ, دُبَّةٌ, is however a loan\-word cf. HomNS 301 f.);—abs. דֹּב [Am 5:19](https://ref.ly/Amos5:19) \+ 5 t.; דּוֺב [1 S 17:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:34) \+ 2 t.; pl. דֻּבִּים [2 K 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:24), [Is 59:11](https://ref.ly/Isa59:11); —*bear*, female [2 K 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:24), [2 S 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:8), [Pr 17:12](https://ref.ly/Prov17:12), [Ho 13:8](https://ref.ly/Hos13:8) cf. [Is 11:7](https://ref.ly/Isa11:7); undetermined [1 S 17:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:34), [17:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:36), [17:37](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:37), [Am 5:19](https://ref.ly/Amos5:19), [Pr 28:15](https://ref.ly/Prov28:15), [Is 59:11](https://ref.ly/Isa59:11), [La 3:10](https://ref.ly/Lam3:10), [La 3:10](https://ref.ly/Lam3:10) (on art. [1 S 17:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:34), [Am 5:19](https://ref.ly/Amos5:19) cf. RS Sem, i. 119 n.)

> \[Str1681] † דִּבָּה **n.f.** whispering, defamation, evil report (? as that which glides stealthily)—abs. ´ד [Nu 14:36](https://ref.ly/Num14:36), [Pr 10:18](https://ref.ly/Prov10:18); cstr. דִּבַּת [Je 20:10](https://ref.ly/Jer20:10) \+ 4 t.; sf. דִּבָּֽתְךָ [Pr 25:10](https://ref.ly/Prov25:10) דִּבָּתָם [Gn 37:2](https://ref.ly/Gen37:2); —**1\.** *whispering* [Psalm 31:14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14), [Je 20:10](https://ref.ly/Jer20:10) (Hi Hup De Gf VB; yet cf. Che Psalm). **2\.** *defamation* [Pr 10:18](https://ref.ly/Prov10:18) (c. הוציא). **3\.** *evil report*, specif. a (true) report of evil doing [Gn 37:2](https://ref.ly/Gen37:2) (P) וַיָּבֵא אֶת־דִּבָּתָם רָעָה; cf. also [Pr 25:10](https://ref.ly/Prov25:10) \& Ez [36:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:3) וַתֵּֽעֲלוּ עַל־שְפַת לָשׁוֺן וְדִבַּת־עָם, unfavourable report of spies [Nu 13:32](https://ref.ly/Num13:32), [14:36](https://ref.ly/Num14:36), [14:37](https://ref.ly/Num14:37) (all P \& all c. הוֺצִיא; adj. רעה only [14:37](https://ref.ly/Num14:37)).

> \[Str1686] † דִּבְיוֺנִים **n.\[m.]** so Qr; \= דב יונים *dove’s dung* (?) [2 K 6:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:25) for Kt חרייונים; LXX Κόπρου περιστερῶν (Klo gives conject. emend.)

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:11; Hosea 13:8; 1 Samuel 17:34; 2 Kings 6:25; Ezekiel 36:3; Proverbs 28:15; Numbers 14:37; 2 Samuel 17:8; Proverbs 25:10; Lamentations 3:10; Psalms 31:14; Isaiah 11:7; Numbers 13:32; Genesis 37:2; 1 Samuel 17:37; Song of Songs 7:10; 2 Kings 2:24; Proverbs 10:18; 1 Samuel 17:36; Numbers 14:36; Proverbs 17:12; Amos 5:19; Jeremiah 20:10


## דבל (id: BDB01994)

דבל (Ar. دَبَلَ *collect*, also *make into lumps, gobbets* (Lane); Eth. \& cogn. in deriv.)

> \[Str1690] † דְּבֵלָה **n.f.** lump of pressed figs, pressed (fig\-) cake (NH דְּבֵילָה, Aram *id.*, ܕܒܶܠܬܳܐ, \= Greek παλάθη; Ar. دُبْلَةٌ *lump, large gobbet or mouthful;* cf. As. *dublu, foundation*, \& Heb. synon. אֲשִׁישָׁה DlHA 58\)—דְּבֵלָה [1 S 30:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:12); cstr. דְּבֶלֶת [2 K 20:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:7) \= [Is 38:21](https://ref.ly/Isa38:21); pl. דְּבֵלִים [1 S 25:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:18), [1 Ch 12:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:40); —used as food [1 S 25:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:18), [30:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:12), [1 Ch 12:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:40); דְּבֶלֶת תְּאֵנִים, as application to boil, or eruption [2 K 20:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:7) \= [Is 38:21](https://ref.ly/Isa38:21).

> \[Str1689] † \[דִּבְלָה] **n.pr.loc.** only c. ה loc. דִּבְלָ֫תָה Ez [6:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:14) but rd. רִבְלָתָה JDMich Hi Sm Co Da.

> \[Str1691] † \[דִּבְלַ֫יִם] **n.pr.m.** father of Gomer wife of Hosea דִּבְלָיִם [Ho 1:3](https://ref.ly/Hos1:3).

> \[Str1015] דִּבְלָתַ֫יִם in **n.pr.loc.** v. ´בית ד, ´עלמן ד (possibly fr. √ דבל in sense of *collect, assemble*, Eth. ተድኣበለ፡ III. 3 *se colligere*, መድበል፡ *coetus, chorus, conventus, concilium*)—[Je 48:22](https://ref.ly/Jer48:22); דִּבְלָתָיְמָה [Nu 33:46](https://ref.ly/Num33:46), [33:47](https://ref.ly/Num33:47).

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 48:22; 1 Chronicles 12:40; 2 Kings 20:7; Isaiah 38:21; 1 Samuel 30:12; 1 Samuel 25:18; Numbers 33:46; Hosea 1:3; Numbers 33:47; Ezekiel 6:14


## דָּבַק (id: BDB01999)

\[Str1692] † דָּבַק, דָּבֵק **vb.** cling, cleave, keep close (NH *id.*, Ar. دَبِقَ Aram. דְּבֵק, דְּבַק ܕܒܶܩ, ܕܒܰܩ)—**Qal** *Pf.* דָּבַק [1 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:2) \+ 2 t.; וְדָבַק consec. [Gn 2:24](https://ref.ly/Gen2:24); דָּבֵָק [2 K 3:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:3); 3 fs. דָּֽבְקָה [Ru 1:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:14) \+ 5 t.; דָּבֵָהקָה [Job 29:10](https://ref.ly/Job29:10); 1 S. דָּבַקְתִּי [Psalm 119:31](https://ref.ly/Ps119:31); 3 pl. דָּֽבְקוּ [2 S 20:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:2); וְדָֽבְקוּ consec. [Dt 28:60](https://ref.ly/Deut28:60); דָּבֵָקוּ [Jb 41:15](https://ref.ly/Job41:15); וּדְבַקְתֶּם consec. [Jos 23:12](https://ref.ly/Josh23:12); *Impf.* יִדְבַּק [Dt 13:18](https://ref.ly/Deut13:18) \+ 3 t.; 3 fs. תִּדְבַּק [2 K 5:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:27), [Psalm 137:6](https://ref.ly/Ps137:6); וַתִּדְבַּק [Gn 34:3](https://ref.ly/Gen34:3) \+ 2 t.; sf. תִּדְבָּקַנִי [Gn 19:19](https://ref.ly/Gen19:19); 2 ms. תִּדְבָּק [Dt 10:20](https://ref.ly/Deut10:20), Ez [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4) (del. B Co); 2 fs. תִּדְבָּקִין [Ru 2:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:8), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:21); 3 pl. יִדְבְּקוּ [Nu 36:7](https://ref.ly/Num36:7), [36:9](https://ref.ly/Num36:9); 2 mpl. תִּדְבָּקוּ [Jos 23:8](https://ref.ly/Josh23:8); תִּדְבָּק\+וּן [Dt 13:5](https://ref.ly/Deut13:5); *Inf. cstr.* וּלְדָבְקָה־בּוֺ [Dt 11:22](https://ref.ly/Deut11:22) \+ 2 t.;—in Hexateuch only JD, except [Nu 36:7](https://ref.ly/Num36:7), [36:9](https://ref.ly/Num36:9) (P);—**1\.** *cling, cleave to*, **a.** lit. sq. בְּ [Jb 19:20](https://ref.ly/Job19:20) (bone to skin), so sq. לְ [Psalm 102:6](https://ref.ly/Ps102:6); sq. אֶל [2 S 23:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:10) (hand to sword; accidentally om. with other words [1 Ch 11:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:13) cf. Dr [2 S 23:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:11)), [Je 13:11](https://ref.ly/Jer13:11) (girdle to loins), [La 4:4](https://ref.ly/Lam4:4) (tongue to roof of mouth, in thirst), so sq. לְ [Jb 29:10](https://ref.ly/Job29:10), [Psalm 137:6](https://ref.ly/Ps137:6) (as a judgment); so also in metaph. [Psalm 44:26](https://ref.ly/Ps44:26) דָּֽבְקָה לָאָרֶץ בִּטְנֵנוּ *our belly cleaveth to the earth* (‖שָׁחָה לֶעָפָר נַפְשֵׁנוּ), [119:25](https://ref.ly/Ps119:25) דָּֽבְקָה לֶעָפָר נַפְשִׁי; fish to scales of crocodile (fig. of Pharaoh), sq. בְּ Ez [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4) (but cf. supr.); abs. (recipr.) of folds of crocodile's belly [Jb 41:15](https://ref.ly/Job41:15); further of the חֵרֶם remaining in (*sticking to*) the hand sq. בְּ [Dt 13:18](https://ref.ly/Deut13:18); so of spot, stain [Jb 31:7](https://ref.ly/Job31:7); **b.** so also of abiding on the land of one's tribe [Nu 36:7](https://ref.ly/Num36:7), [36:9](https://ref.ly/Num36:9) (sq. בְּ). **c.** \= *remain with, close to* sq. עִם [Ru 2:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:8), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:21) sq. בְּ v:[23](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:23). **2\.** *cling, cleave to*, **a.** fig. of loyalty, affection etc., sts. with idea of physical proximity retained, sq. בְּ [Gn 2:24](https://ref.ly/Gen2:24) (J; man to wife) cf. [34:3](https://ref.ly/Gen34:3) (J) [1 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:2), [Jos 23:12](https://ref.ly/Josh23:12) (D); further [Ru 1:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:14) (Ruth to Naomi); [2 S 20:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:2) (people to king); esp. (sq. בְּ) of cleaving to ´י [Dt 11:22](https://ref.ly/Deut11:22) (‖אָהֵב, הָלַח בְּכָל־דְּרָכָיו) [30:20](https://ref.ly/Deut30:20) (‖אָהֵב, שָׁמַע בְּקֹלוֺ), [Jos 22:5](https://ref.ly/Josh22:5) (‖אָהֵב, ´הָלַח וגו, שָׁמַר מִצְוֺתָיו); cf. further [Dt 10:20](https://ref.ly/Deut10:20), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Deut13:5), [Jos 23:8](https://ref.ly/Josh23:8) (all D), [2 K 18:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:6); [Psalm 63:9](https://ref.ly/Ps63:9) (sq. אַחֲרֵי) \& [Psalm 119:31](https://ref.ly/Ps119:31) דָּבַקְתּי בְעֵֽדְוֺתֶָיךָ; so **b.** of the opposite דָּבֵָק…בְּחַטּאֹות יָֽרָבְעָם [2 K 3:37](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:37), **c.** subj. disease, calamity, sq. בְּ Pers. [1 K 22:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:34) \= [2 Ch 18:33](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:33) ובין הַשִּׁרְיָן ´בֵּין הַדּ. (The Be al. cf. VB: the jointed *attachment* or *appendage* to the rigid breast\-armour, which covered the abdomen; \> Klo prop. appendage of helmet; Thes suggests armpits, lit. *joints* (cf. מַדְבְּקֵי יָד [Je 38:12](https://ref.ly/Jer38:12) Targum); Ew the soft muscles etc. *connecting* the chest with the bottom of the back.)

## דָּבַר (id: BDB02002)

\[Str1696] \[דָּבַר]:1142 **vb.** speak (original mng. dub.; *range in order* Thes is conjectural and not comprehensive enough; *treiben* MV does not explain Ar. or Heb. usage, but only Aram. A mng. *go away*, sustained by Ar. دَبَرَ بِهِ *go away with it*, would best explain the four branches of usage:—(1\) Ar. دَبَرَ *depart, perish*, IV. *retreat*, fig. *retrograde, decline;* دَبْرٌ *passing away, death;* As. *dabâru*, Pi. *drive away*, BezOrient. Diplom, Vocab., *Dibbara*, pest\-god, Hpt. in KAT2. 500, דֶּבֶר *pestilence.* (2\) Ar. ܕܒܰܪ *follow behind*, in time, place, or station; ܕܳܒܽܘܪܳܐ *part behind*, ܕܽܘܒܳܪܳܐ *back*, דְּבִיר *hindmost chamber of temple*. (3\) Syr. ܕܒܪ *lead, guide*, cattle, sheep, government, *take* a wife, \= Aram. דְּבַר; Syr. ܕܒܘܪܐ, Aram. דַּבָּר *leader;* Syr. ܕܘܒܪܐ, Aram. דַּבָּרָא *guidance;* Syr. ܕܰܒܪܳܐ, Aram. דַּבְרָא, Heb. דֹּבֶר; Syr. ܡܰܕܒܰܪ, Aram. מַדְבְּרָא, Heb. מִדְבָּר *pasture, wilderness;* Ar. دَبَرَ, Syr. ܕܶܒܘܽܪܻܝܬܳܐ, Aram. דַּבַּרְתָא, Heb. דְּבוֺרָה *swarm of bees*, may be in this line, as led by their queen, so Thes. (4\) Ar. دَبْرٌ *consider the end* or *issue* (of an affair), *relate* (a story or tradition); دَبَّرَ فِى *consider*, دَبَّرَ عَلَى *plan against;* Ph. דבר *speak;* Heb. דִּבֶּר etc. Syn. of אָמַר *say*, as Aram. מַלֵּל with אֲמַר; Gk. λαλεῖν, λέγειν; Lat *loqui, dicere;* Germ. *reden, sagen*)—† **Qal**:41 only inf. (once ?) \& pt.: *Inf.* sf. בְּדָבְרֶָךָ (by attraction to בְּשָׁפְטֶָךָ for usual Piel) [Psalm 51:6](https://ref.ly/Ps51:6); *Pt.* דֹּבֵר [Ex 6:29](https://ref.ly/Exod6:29) \+ 30 t.; pl. דֹּבְרִים [Nu 36:5](https://ref.ly/Num36:5), [Psalm 109:20](https://ref.ly/Ps109:20); cstr. דֹּבְרֵי [Psalm 5:7](https://ref.ly/Ps5:7) \+ 3 t.; f. דֹּבְרוֺת [Nu 27:7](https://ref.ly/Num27:7), [Psalm 31:19](https://ref.ly/Ps31:19); pass. דָּבֻר [Pr 25:11](https://ref.ly/Prov25:11); —*speak*, abs.  *Note.*—וַתָּקָם וַתְּדַבֵּר אֶת־כָּל־זֶרַע הַמַּמְלָכָה [2 Ch 22:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:10) LXX ἀπώλεσε, Vulgate *interfecit,* scribal error for וַתְּאַבֵּד [2 K 11:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:1). Other explanations: *plot against, waylay* (Ar. دُبُرٌ) Thes MV; *pronounce sentence,* ellipt. וַתְּדַבֵּר מִשְׁפָּטִים GesHdw Rob; but cf. VB.—† **Pu.** *Impf.* בַּיּוֺם שֶׁיְּדֻבַּר־בָּהּ *in the day when she may be spoken for* Ct [8:8](https://ref.ly/Song8:8) (vid. **Pi. 4 c**); *Pt.* נִכְבָּדוֺת מְדֻבָּר בָּח *glorious things are being spoken in thee* (RV Pe MV *of thee*) [Psalm 87:3](https://ref.ly/Ps87:3). † **Hithp.** *Pt.* מִדַּבֵּר \= מִתְדַּבֵּר with acc. מִדַּבֵּר הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה *speaking this word* [2 S 14:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:13); ´מִדַּבֵּר אֶל (אֵת הַקּוֺל)שָׁמַע *heard* (*the voice,* or) *one speaking unto* [Nu 7:89](https://ref.ly/Num7:89) (P) Ez [2:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:2), [43:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:6) (Di [Nu 7:89](https://ref.ly/Num7:89) would rd. מְדַבֵּר after LXX; the meaning not appreciably different from Piel; the Hithp. difficult to reconcile with אֶל). † **Hiph.** *Impf.* וַיַּדְבֵּר עַמִּים תַּחְתָּי [Psalm 18:48](https://ref.ly/Ps18:48) ([2 S 22:48](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:48) has וּמֹרִיד for rare וַיַּדְבֵּר), יַדְבֵּר עַמִּים תַּחְתֵּינוּ [47:4](https://ref.ly/Ps47:4); either (cf. Aram. דַּבַּר) *leads* subject (so Ges *coegit,* De Now), or (cf. Ar. أَدْبَرَ *turn the back, retreat puts to flight,* fig. for *subdues* (so Hi). 

> \[Str1697] דָּבָר:1439 **n.m.** speech, word—[Gn 18:14](https://ref.ly/Gen18:14) \+ 446 t.; cstr. דְּבַר [Gn 12:17](https://ref.ly/Gen12:17) \+ 361 t.; sf. דְּבָרִי [Nu 11:23](https://ref.ly/Num11:23) \+ (sfs. 66 t.); sg. in all 875 t.; pl. דְּבָרִים [Ex 4:10](https://ref.ly/Exod4:10) \+ 182 t.; cstr. דִּבְרֵי [Gn 24:30](https://ref.ly/Gen24:30) \+ 253 t.; sf. דְּבָרָיו [Gn 37:8](https://ref.ly/Gen37:8) (sfs. 127 t.); pl. in all 564 t.;—**1\.** sg. *speech, discourse, saying, word,* as the sum of that which is spoken: **1\.** *of men,* **a.** נְבוֺן דָּבָר *discreet in speech* [1 S 16:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:18); שְפָתַיִם ´ד *speech of lips* [Psalm 59:13](https://ref.ly/Ps59:13), *mere talk* [Is 36:5](https://ref.ly/Isa36:5) (\= [2 K 18:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:20)) [Pr 14:23](https://ref.ly/Prov14:23) (cf. [13:12](https://ref.ly/Jer13:12), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Jer14:17) (of God), [1 S 8:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:10) (´דִּבְרֵי י). **b.** *word of command,* מֶּלֶח(הַ) ´ד [1 Ch 21:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:4), [21:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:6), [Est 1:12](https://ref.ly/Esth1:12) \+ 6 t. Est, [Ec 8:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:4) cf. [2 Ch 30:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:5), [31:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:5), [Dn 9:23](https://ref.ly/Dan9:23), [9:25](https://ref.ly/Dan9:25); † מַלְכוּת ´ד *royal edict* [Est 1:19](https://ref.ly/Esth1:19); כדבר עשה *do according to the command of* [Gn 44:2](https://ref.ly/Gen44:2), [47:30](https://ref.ly/Gen47:30), [Ex 8:9](https://ref.ly/Exod8:9), [8:27](https://ref.ly/Exod8:27), [12:35](https://ref.ly/Exod12:35), [32:28](https://ref.ly/Exod32:28) (J) [Lv 10:7](https://ref.ly/Lev10:7) (P) Ju [11:10](https://ref.ly/Judg11:10), [Ezr 10:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:5), [Ne 5:12](https://ref.ly/Neh5:12), [5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13); cf. [2 S 17:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:6) (word of counsel), [1 K 3:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:12) (request), [1 K 17:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:13) (proposal; on these mngs. v. infr.) **c.** *message, report, tidings,* וַיִּשְׁמַע הָעָם אֶת־הַדָּבָר הָרָע הַזֶּה *and the people heard this evil report* [Ex 33:4](https://ref.ly/Exod33:4) (JE) cf. [1 K 20:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:12); הדבר (היה)אמת *the report was true* [1 K 10:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:6) \= [2 Ch 9:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:5); עַד בּוֺא דָבָר מֵעִמָּכֶם *until word come from you* [2 S 15:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:28); ויגע הדבר אל מלך נינוה [Jon 3:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:6); † הֵשִׁיב דָּבָר *return* or *bring word, report* [Gn 37:14](https://ref.ly/Gen37:14), [Nu 13:26](https://ref.ly/Num13:26), [22:8](https://ref.ly/Num22:8) (J) [Dt 1:22](https://ref.ly/Deut1:22), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Deut1:25), [Jos 14:7](https://ref.ly/Josh14:7), [22:32](https://ref.ly/Josh22:32) (D) [1 K 2:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:30), [20:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:9), [2 K 22:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:9), [22:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:20) (\= [2 Ch 34:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:16), [34:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:28)), but *answer* [1 S 17:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:30), [2 S 3:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:11), [24:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:13) (\= [1 Ch 21:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:12)), [1 K 12:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:6), [12:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:9) (\= [2 Ch 10:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:6), [10:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:9)), [12:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:16), [Ne 2:20](https://ref.ly/Neh2:20), [Pr 18:13](https://ref.ly/Prov18:13), [27:11](https://ref.ly/Prov27:11), [Is 41:28](https://ref.ly/Isa41:28), Ez [9:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:11) (DrSm, p, 190 explains the phrase as *turn back with a word*); cf. ענה (לא)דבר *answer something* or *nothing* [1 K 18:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:21), [2 K 18:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:36), [Psalm 119:42](https://ref.ly/Ps119:42), [Is 36:21](https://ref.ly/Isa36:21), [Je 44:20](https://ref.ly/Jer44:20) vid. **IV. 6;** דברים השׁיב *return words* [Ex 19:8](https://ref.ly/Exod19:8) (E) [Pr 24:26](https://ref.ly/Prov24:26) vid. **III. 1\. d.** *advice, counsel,* בדבר בלעם *by advice of Balaam* [Nu 31:16](https://ref.ly/Num31:16) (P), cf. Ju [20:7](https://ref.ly/Judg20:7), [2 S 19:44](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:44), [Est 5:5](https://ref.ly/Esth5:5). **e.** *request,* אמתו *of his maid,* עבדו *of his servant* [2 S 14:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:15), [14:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:22). **f.** *promise,* לא יחל דברו *he shall not break his word* [Nu 30:3](https://ref.ly/Num30:3) (P), cf. הקים דבר *perform a promise* [Ne 5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13), vid. also **2 b. g.** *charge, complaint,* אם אמת היה הדבר הזה *if this charge be true* [Dt 22:20](https://ref.ly/Deut22:20) cf. [13:15](https://ref.ly/Deut13:15), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Deut17:4), שים דבר בְּ [1 S 22:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:15), שים עלילות דברים לְ [Dt 22:14](https://ref.ly/Deut22:14), [22:17](https://ref.ly/Deut22:17). **h.** *decision, sentence,* דְּבַר הַמִּשְׁפָּט *the sentence of judgment* [Dt 17:9](https://ref.ly/Deut17:9) cf. v:[10](https://ref.ly/Deut17:10), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Deut17:11). **i.** *theme, story,* רָחַשׁ לִבִּי דָּבָר טוֺב my heart swells with a *good theme* [Psalm 45:2](https://ref.ly/Ps45:2); דְּבַרגְּבוּרוֺת *the story of his great might* [Jb 41:4](https://ref.ly/Job41:4). **2\.** *word of God,* as a divine communication in the form of commandments, prophecy, and words of help to his people, used 394 times. This word is communicated in several ways: **a.** אל ´ויהי דבר י *then the word of Yahweh came unto* [1 S 15:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:10), [2 S 7:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:4) (cf. אלהים [1 Ch 17:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:3)), [1 K 6:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:11), [13:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:20), [16:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:1), [17:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:2), [17:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:8), [21:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:17), [21:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:28), [2 Ch 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:2) (cf. האלהים [1 K 12:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:22)): cf. [1 Ch 22:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:8) (על by confusion), [Is 38:4](https://ref.ly/Isa38:4), [Je 1:4](https://ref.ly/Jer1:4) \+ 20 t. Je, Ez [3:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:16) \+ 41 t. Ez. Ez, [Dn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Dan9:2), [Zc 1:1](https://ref.ly/Zech1:1), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Zech1:7), [7:1](https://ref.ly/Zech7:1); † אל ´י ´הנה ד [Gn 15:4](https://ref.ly/Gen15:4), [1 K 19:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:9); † היה אל ´י ´ד [2 S 24:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:11), [1 K 16:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:7), [18:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:1), [2 K 20:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:4), Ez [24:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:20); † ביד ´י ´היה ד [Hg 1:1](https://ref.ly/Hag1:1), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Hag2:1), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Hag2:10); † ביד ´י ´ויהי ד [Hg 1:3](https://ref.ly/Hag1:3); † אשׁר ´י ´היה אל ד [Ho 1:1](https://ref.ly/Hos1:1), [Jo 1:1](https://ref.ly/Joel1:1), Mi [Zp 1:1](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:1); † ´י ´אליו אשׁר היה ד [1 K 18:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:31) [Je 1:2](https://ref.ly/Jer1:2); † ´אל יר ´י ´אשׁר היה ד [Je 14:1](https://ref.ly/Jer14:1), [46:1](https://ref.ly/Jer46:1), [47:1](https://ref.ly/Jer47:1), [49:34](https://ref.ly/Jer49:34) (vid. אשׁר **6 a**); הדבר אשׁר היה אל [Je 7:1](https://ref.ly/Jer7:1) \+ 11 t. Je; † ´י ´את ד [2 K 3:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:12), [Je 23:28](https://ref.ly/Jer23:28), [27:18](https://ref.ly/Jer27:18); cf. אֵלַי דָּבָר יְגֻנָּב *unto me a word was brought by stealth* [Jb 4:12](https://ref.ly/Job4:12), cf. ´וְטֶרֶם יִגָּלֶה אֵלָיו דְּבַר י *and the word of Yahweh was not yet revealed unto him* [1 S 3:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:7). Yahweh also sends his word שׁלח [Psalm 107:20](https://ref.ly/Ps107:20), [147:18](https://ref.ly/Ps147:18), [Is 9:7](https://ref.ly/Isa9:7), [Je 42:5](https://ref.ly/Jer42:5) (cf. pl. [Zc 7:12](https://ref.ly/Zech7:12)) and makes it an object of vision ראה [Je 2:31](https://ref.ly/Jer2:31), [38:21](https://ref.ly/Jer38:21) (cf. pl. Ez [11:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:25)), חזה [Is 2:1](https://ref.ly/Isa2:1); it is also commanded צוה [Ex 16:16](https://ref.ly/Exod16:16), [16:32](https://ref.ly/Exod16:32), [35:4](https://ref.ly/Exod35:4), [Lv 8:5](https://ref.ly/Lev8:5), [9:6](https://ref.ly/Lev9:6), [17:2](https://ref.ly/Lev17:2), [Nu 30:2](https://ref.ly/Num30:2), [36:6](https://ref.ly/Num36:6) (P) [Dt 4:2](https://ref.ly/Deut4:2), [13:1](https://ref.ly/Deut13:1), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Deut15:15), [Jos 1:13](https://ref.ly/Josh1:13), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Josh4:10) (D) [1 Ch 16:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:15) (\= [Psalm 105:8](https://ref.ly/Ps105:8)) [Ne 1:8](https://ref.ly/Neh1:8), [Je 7:23](https://ref.ly/Jer7:23), cf. הדברים אשׁר צוה [Ex 35:1](https://ref.ly/Exod35:1), [Lv 8:36](https://ref.ly/Lev8:36) (P) [Ex 19:7](https://ref.ly/Exod19:7) (E) [Dt 6:6](https://ref.ly/Deut6:6). **b.** Yahweh confirms his word of promise [Dt 9:5](https://ref.ly/Deut9:5), [1 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:23) (where read דְּבָרֵח *thy word* acc. to LXX SyrVer \& Dr), [1 K 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:4), [6:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:12), [8:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:20), [Je 29:10](https://ref.ly/Jer29:10), [33:14](https://ref.ly/Jer33:14), and his word of warning [1 K 12:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:15), [Dn 9:12](https://ref.ly/Dan9:12); his word stands for ever [Is 40:8](https://ref.ly/Isa40:8); it is settled for ever in heaven [Psalm 119:89](https://ref.ly/Ps119:89); he remembers his *holy word* דבר קדשׁו [Psalm 105:42](https://ref.ly/Ps105:42) (cf. דִּבְרֵי קָדְשׁוֺ [Je 23:9](https://ref.ly/Jer23:9)); he himself [Jo 2:11](https://ref.ly/Joel2:11), the angels [Psalm 103:20](https://ref.ly/Ps103:20), and forces of nature [Psalm 148:8](https://ref.ly/Ps148:8) עשה דברו do his word of command; by his word the heavens were made [Psalm 33:6](https://ref.ly/Ps33:6); it is near his people, in their mouth and heart [Dt 30:14](https://ref.ly/Deut30:14); a lamp to their feet [Psalm 119:105](https://ref.ly/Ps119:105). **II.** *saying, utterance, sentence,* as a section of a discourse:—**1\.** *of men,* **a.** דָּבָר בְּעִתּוֺ *a word in due season* [Pr 15:23](https://ref.ly/Prov15:23) cf. [Jb 4:2](https://ref.ly/Job4:2), [Pr 12:25](https://ref.ly/Prov12:25); commonly in plural, † (ה)שִּׁירָ(הַ)דִּבְרֵי *words of the song* (i.e. its lines of poetry) [Dt 31:30](https://ref.ly/Deut31:30), [32:44](https://ref.ly/Deut32:44), [Psalm 18:1](https://ref.ly/Ps18:1) (\= [2 S 22:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:1)) [137:3](https://ref.ly/Ps137:3); הלל בדברי דויד *to sing praises with the words of David* (his psalms) [2 Ch 29:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:30); דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים *sentences of the wise* [Pr 1:6](https://ref.ly/Prov1:6), [22:17](https://ref.ly/Prov22:17), [Ec 9:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:17), [12:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:11); these sayings are commonly *written*, cf. דברי האגרת *words of the letter*[Est 9:26](https://ref.ly/Esth9:26); הדברים עתיקים *the records are ancient* [1 Ch 4:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:22); דִּבְרֵי סֵפֶר(ה) [Is 29:11](https://ref.ly/Isa29:11), [29:18](https://ref.ly/Isa29:18), [Je 29:1](https://ref.ly/Jer29:1). **b.** *title of writings:* דִּבְרֵי קֹהֶלֶת [Ec 1:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:1), אגור ´ד [Pr 30:1](https://ref.ly/Prov30:1), למואל ´ד [Pr 31:1](https://ref.ly/Prov31:1), ירמיהו ´ד [Je 1:1](https://ref.ly/Jer1:1), so [Am 1:1](https://ref.ly/Amos1:1), [Ne 1:1](https://ref.ly/Neh1:1); cf. in appended phrase or clause, [Je 51:64](https://ref.ly/Jer51:64), איוב ´ד [Jb 31:40](https://ref.ly/Job31:40). **c.** in Ch *name of a work:* החזים ´ד [2 Ch 33:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:18), [33:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:19), שׁמעיה ´בד [2 Ch 12:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:15), יהוא ´בד [2 Ch 20:34](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:34), ´שׁמואל על ד [1 Ch 29:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:29); נתן ´על ד [1 Ch 29:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:29), [2 Ch 9:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:29), גד על ´ד [1 Ch 29:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:29). (It is not certain whether writings of prophets or histories about them are referred to; in the latter case they are *acts*, **IV. 2,** vid. DrIntr. 497\.) **2\.** *of God:* עֲשֶרֶת הַדְּבָרִים *the ten words* (sentences in the tables) [Ex 34:28](https://ref.ly/Exod34:28) (JE) [Dt 4:13](https://ref.ly/Deut4:13), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Deut10:4), referred to as הדברים [Ex 34:1](https://ref.ly/Exod34:1) (JE) [Dt 4:10](https://ref.ly/Deut4:10), [4:36](https://ref.ly/Deut4:36), [5:19](https://ref.ly/Deut5:19), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Deut9:10), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Deut10:2); of commands of covenant code [Ex 24:3](https://ref.ly/Exod24:3), [24:4](https://ref.ly/Exod24:4), [24:8](https://ref.ly/Exod24:8) cf. [34:27](https://ref.ly/Exod34:27) (JE); of Deuteronomic code [Dt 17:19](https://ref.ly/Deut17:19) (\+ 16 t. D) [2 K 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:11), [23:3–24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:3-2Kgs23:24) (cf. 2 Ch [34:19–31](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:19-2Chr34:31)) [Je 11:2](https://ref.ly/Jer11:2) – 8 [34:18](https://ref.ly/Jer34:18); the law\-book of Joshua [Jos 24:26](https://ref.ly/Josh24:26) (E); of the code of Ezra [Ezr 7:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:11), [9:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:4), [Ne 8:9](https://ref.ly/Neh8:9), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Neh8:13); the words of Yahweh written by Jeremiah [Je 25:13](https://ref.ly/Jer25:13), [36:4](https://ref.ly/Jer36:4)–[32](https://ref.ly/Jer36:32) [51:61](https://ref.ly/Jer51:61); the words of the book of Daniel [Dn 12:4](https://ref.ly/Dan12:4), [12:9](https://ref.ly/Dan12:9); commands in general [1 S 15:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:11), [Psalm 50:17](https://ref.ly/Ps50:17), [119:57](https://ref.ly/Ps119:57), [119:130](https://ref.ly/Ps119:130), [119:139](https://ref.ly/Ps119:139); promises [2 S 7:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:28), [Ne 9:8](https://ref.ly/Neh9:8), cf. [1 Ch 25:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:5), [2 Ch 36:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:16). **III.** *a word, words:*—**1\.** *of men:* שָפָה אֶחָת וּדְבָרִים אֲחָדִים *one lip, and one and the same words* [Gn 11:1](https://ref.ly/Gen11:1) (J); אֵין אֹמֶר וְאֵין דְּבָרִים *no speech and no words* [Psalm 19:4](https://ref.ly/Ps19:4); אִישׁ דְּבָרִים *man of words*, ready in speech, [Ex 4:10](https://ref.ly/Exod4:10) (J); רב דברים *multitude of words* [Jb 11:2](https://ref.ly/Job11:2), [Pr 10:19](https://ref.ly/Prov10:19), [Ec 5:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:2); קְחוּ עִמָּכֶם דְּבָרִים *take with you words* [Ho 14:3](https://ref.ly/Hos14:3); שׁקר(ה) (י)דבר *lying word(s)* [Ex 5:9](https://ref.ly/Exod5:9), [23:7](https://ref.ly/Exod23:7) (E) [Pr 13:5](https://ref.ly/Prov13:5), [29:12](https://ref.ly/Prov29:12), [Is 59:13](https://ref.ly/Isa59:13), [Je 7:4](https://ref.ly/Jer7:4), [7:8](https://ref.ly/Jer7:8); דִּבְרֵי רוּחַ *words of wind*, vain words [Jb 16:3](https://ref.ly/Job16:3); וּמַה־שֶּׁמֶץ דָּבָר נִשְׁמַע־בּוֺ *and what a whisper of a word we hear of him!* [Jb 26:14](https://ref.ly/Job26:14). **2\.** *of God.* It is difficult to determine how many of the following should come under **II. 2\.** The pl. for *words of God* is relatively seldom: [Gn 20:8](https://ref.ly/Gen20:8), [Ex 19:6](https://ref.ly/Exod19:6), [Nu 12:6](https://ref.ly/Num12:6), [Jos 3:9](https://ref.ly/Josh3:9) (E) [Ex 4:28](https://ref.ly/Exod4:28), [4:30](https://ref.ly/Exod4:30), [Nu 11:24](https://ref.ly/Num11:24) (J) [Dt 18:19](https://ref.ly/Deut18:19), [Jos 23:14](https://ref.ly/Josh23:14) (D) [1 S 3:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:18), [8:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:10), [15:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:1), [2 Ch 11:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:4), [Jb 42:7](https://ref.ly/Job42:7), [Pr 30:6](https://ref.ly/Prov30:6), [Is 31:2](https://ref.ly/Isa31:2), [Dn 10:11](https://ref.ly/Dan10:11), [Am 8:11](https://ref.ly/Amos8:11), [Mi 2:7](https://ref.ly/Mic2:7), apart from [Je 3:12](https://ref.ly/Jer3:12) \+ 30 t. Je, Ez [2:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:7) \+ 4 t. Ez, [Zc 1:6](https://ref.ly/Zech1:6) \+ 4 t. Zc. **IV.** *matter, affair*, thing about which one speaks:—**1\.** *business, occupation:* דְּבַר הַמֶּלֶח *king's business* [1 S 21:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:9); לְכָל־דְּבַר *for every matter of* [1 Ch 26:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:32), [27:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:1), [2 Ch 19:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:11); דְּבָרֶיךָ לְכָ־ל *for all thine affairs* [1 Ch 28:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:21); דבר המזבח [Nu 18:7](https://ref.ly/Num18:7); דָּבָר אֵין־לָהֶם עִם *they had not dealings with* Ju [18:7](https://ref.ly/Judg18:7), [18:28](https://ref.ly/Judg18:28); † דְּבַר־יוֺם בְּיוֺמוֺ *the task or portion of a day in its day, daily duty or due* [Ex 5:13](https://ref.ly/Exod5:13), [5:19](https://ref.ly/Exod5:19) (E) [16:4](https://ref.ly/Exod16:4) (J) [Lv 23:37](https://ref.ly/Lev23:37) (P) [1 K 8:59](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:59), [2 K 25:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:30), [1 Ch 16:37](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:37), [2 Ch 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:13) (ביום) v:[14](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:14), [31:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:16), [Ezr 3:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:4), [Ne 11:23](https://ref.ly/Neh11:23), [12:47](https://ref.ly/Neh12:47), [Je 52:34](https://ref.ly/Jer52:34), [Dn 1:5](https://ref.ly/Dan1:5); † דבר שׁנה בשׁנה [1 K 10:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:25), [2 Ch 9:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:24). **2\.** pl. *acts* cstr. דִּבְרֵי [1 K 11:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:41), [11:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:41), [1 Ch 29:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:29), [2 Ch 12:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:15), [16:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:11); † דְּבָרָיו *his acts* [2 Ch 13:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:22), [35:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:27); especially in phrases, עַל־סֵפֶר דִּבְרֵי…וְיֶתֶר דִּבְרֵי הַיָּמִים *and the rest of the acts of…in the book of the chronicles* (*daily record of events*) *of* [1 K 14:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:29) \+ 32 t. K.; וְיֶתֶר דִּבְרֵי [2 Ch 13:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:22) \+ 8 t. Ch, וְיֶתֶר דְּבָרָיו [2 Ch 28:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:26), שְׁאָר דִּבְרֵי [2 Ch 9:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:29) are referred to various sources; cf. also במספר דברי הימים [1 Ch 27:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:24), בדברי דויד [1 Ch 23:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:27), על־דברי מלכי ישראל [2 Ch 33:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:18): דברים טובים *good deeds* [2 Ch 12:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:12),19:13; דִּבְרֵי עֲוֺנֹת *acts of iniquity* [Psalm 65:4](https://ref.ly/Ps65:4) (De, *Fälle von Missethaten*); דִּבְרֵי־רָע *evil deeds* [Je 5:28](https://ref.ly/Jer5:28). It is used of God, only in כל דבריו לא יענה [Jb 33:13](https://ref.ly/Job33:13), אֹתוֺתָיו ´ד [Psalm 105:27](https://ref.ly/Ps105:27), נִפְלְאֹתֶיךָ ´ד [Psalm 145:5](https://ref.ly/Ps145:5). **3\.** *matter, affair:* דבר אוריה *affair of Uriah* [1 K 15:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:5); שׁרשׁ דבר *root of the matter* [Jb 19:28](https://ref.ly/Job19:28); מה היה הדבר *how went the matter ?* [1 S 4:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:16), [2 S 1:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:4) cf. [Ru 3:18](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:18); סבב את פני הדבר *change the face of the matter* [2 S 14:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:20); דִּבְרֵי הָאֲתֹנוֺת *the matter of the asses* [1 S 10:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:2); זה דבר הרצח *this is the case of the slayer* [Dt 19:4](https://ref.ly/Deut19:4); לְכָל־דְּבָרָיו [1 K 6:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:38) *as to all its particulars.* **4\.** *events, things:* in the phrase, † אחר הדברים האלה *after these things* (events in time) [Gn 15:1](https://ref.ly/Gen15:1), [22:1](https://ref.ly/Gen22:1), [40:1](https://ref.ly/Gen40:1) (E) [39:7](https://ref.ly/Gen39:7) (J) [1 K 17:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:17), [21:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:1), [Ezr 7:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:1), [Est 2:1](https://ref.ly/Esth2:1), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Esth3:1); † האלה אחרי הדברים [Gn 48:1](https://ref.ly/Gen48:1), [Jos 24:29](https://ref.ly/Josh24:29) (E) [Gn 22:20](https://ref.ly/Gen22:20) (J) [2 Ch 32:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:1). **5\.** *cause, case* for judicial investigation: כִּי־יִהְיֶה לָהֶם דָּבָר *if they have a case* [Ex 18:16](https://ref.ly/Exod18:16) cf. [18:22](https://ref.ly/Exod18:22), [22:8](https://ref.ly/Exod22:8); בעל דברים *one having a case* [Ex 24:14](https://ref.ly/Exod24:14), cf. בדבר משׁפט [2 Ch 19:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:6); דְּבָרֶיךָ טוֺבִים וּנְכֹחִים *pleas, good and right* [2 S 15:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:3) cf. [Jos 20:4](https://ref.ly/Josh20:4). **6\.** *something, anything* (indefinite): עשה דבר *do a thing* [Gn 22:16](https://ref.ly/Gen22:16) \+ (frequent); עשה דברים [Is 42:16](https://ref.ly/Isa42:16) \+ (less frequent); דבר ´היפלא מי [Gn 18:14](https://ref.ly/Gen18:14) cf. [Je 32:17](https://ref.ly/Jer32:17), [32:27](https://ref.ly/Jer32:27); דבר…לא *nothing* [1 K 5:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:7), [10:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:3), [Is 39:2](https://ref.ly/Isa39:2), [Je 42:4](https://ref.ly/Jer42:4) \+ (frequent), אין דבר [Ex 5:11](https://ref.ly/Exod5:11) (E) [Nu 20:19](https://ref.ly/Num20:19), [1 S 20:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:21); ´לא דבר מ *nothing of* [Ex 9:4](https://ref.ly/Exod9:4) (J) [Jos 8:35](https://ref.ly/Josh8:35), [11:15](https://ref.ly/Josh11:15), [21:45](https://ref.ly/Josh21:45), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Josh23:14) (D); כל דבר *anything* [Nu 31:23](https://ref.ly/Num31:23), [Dt 23:20](https://ref.ly/Deut23:20) \+; דבר רע [Psalm 141:4](https://ref.ly/Ps141:4) \+; טוב ´ד [1 K 14:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:13) \+ ; דְּבַר־מָה *whatsoever* [Nu 23:3](https://ref.ly/Num23:3) (E); עֶרְוַת דבר [Dt 23:15](https://ref.ly/Deut23:15) *naked\-ness of a thing*, i.e. unclean or unseemly thing (indecency in camp); so of a wife [24:1](https://ref.ly/Deut24:1). **7\.** *way, manner:* זֶה דְּבַר הַשִּׁמִטָּה *this is the manner of the release* [Dt 15:2](https://ref.ly/Deut15:2) (cf. SI1); כֵּן דְּבַר הַמֶּלֶח *so was the way of the king* [Est 1:13](https://ref.ly/Esth1:13); הזה כדבר *after this manner, thus* † [Gn 18:25](https://ref.ly/Gen18:25), [32:20](https://ref.ly/Gen32:20), [44:7](https://ref.ly/Gen44:7) (JE) [2 S 15:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:6), [Ezr 10:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:5), [Ne 5:12](https://ref.ly/Neh5:12), [5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13) with (עשה), [1 S 9:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:21), [2 S 14:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:3), [17:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:6) (דבר), [1 S 17:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:27); [17:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:30) [Is 8:20](https://ref.ly/Isa8:20) (אמר), [Ne 6:4](https://ref.ly/Neh6:4), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Neh6:5) (שׁלח), [Ne 6:4](https://ref.ly/Neh6:4) (השׁיב) cf. [1 S 17:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:30), [2 K 7:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:19) (היה) (peculiar to D, כדבר הרע הזה *such a wicked deed as this* [Dt 13:12](https://ref.ly/Deut13:12), [19:20](https://ref.ly/Deut19:20), כדבר הגדול הזה [Dt 4:32](https://ref.ly/Deut4:32)); כדברים האלה עשה *thus and thus did he* [Gn 39:19](https://ref.ly/Gen39:19) (J) [1 S 2:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:23) (but usually the pl. \= *these words*). **8\.** *reason, cause:* זֶּה דְבַר־הַמַּס [1 K 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:15); זה הדבר אשׁר *this is the reason why* [Jos 5:4](https://ref.ly/Josh5:4) (D) [1 K 11:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:27); so [1 S 17:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:29) AV RV, *was it not* but *a word ?* Th Ke RVm VB Dr; …עַל דְּבַר *because of, for the sake of* [Gn 20:11](https://ref.ly/Gen20:11), [20:18](https://ref.ly/Gen20:18) (E) [12:17](https://ref.ly/Gen12:17), [43:18](https://ref.ly/Gen43:18), [Ex 8:8](https://ref.ly/Exod8:8) (J) [Nu 17:14](https://ref.ly/Num17:14) (P) [Psalm 45:5](https://ref.ly/Ps45:5), [79:9](https://ref.ly/Ps79:9), עַל דִּבְרֵי [Je 14:1](https://ref.ly/Jer14:1); עַל־דִּבְרֵיכֶם *for your sakes* [Dt 4:21](https://ref.ly/Deut4:21), עַל דְּבַר אשׁר [Dt 22:24](https://ref.ly/Deut22:24), [22:24](https://ref.ly/Deut22:24), [23:5](https://ref.ly/Deut23:5), [2 S 13:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:22).

> \[Str1698] דֶּ֫בֶר:49 **n.m. pestilence** (cf. Ar. دَبْرٌ, إِدْبَارٌ, *departure, death*)—[Ex 9:3](https://ref.ly/Exod9:3) \+ ; דָּבֶר Hb [3:5](https://ref.ly/Hab3:5); sf. דְּבָרֶיךָ [Ho 13:14](https://ref.ly/Hos13:14); —**1\.** *plague, pestilence*, in general [Ex 5:3](https://ref.ly/Exod5:3), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Exod9:15), [Nu 14:12](https://ref.ly/Num14:12) (J) [Lv 26:25](https://ref.ly/Lev26:25) (H) [Dt 28:21](https://ref.ly/Deut28:21), [2 S 24:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:13), [24:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:15) (\+ v:[15](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:15) a LXX We Dr) \= [1 Ch 21:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:12), [21:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:14), [1 K 8:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:37) \= [2 Ch 6:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:28), [2 Ch 7:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:13), [20:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:9), [Psalm 91:3](https://ref.ly/Ps91:3), [91:6](https://ref.ly/Ps91:6), [Je 14:12](https://ref.ly/Jer14:12) \+ 16 t. Je, Ez [5:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:12) \+ 11 t. Ez, [Ho 13:14](https://ref.ly/Hos13:14), [Am 4:10](https://ref.ly/Amos4:10), Hb [3:5](https://ref.ly/Hab3:5). Rd. וּבַדָּבֶר, or the like, [1 S 4:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:8), for MT בַּמִּדְבָּר, We Klo Dr. † **2\.** *cattle\-plague, murrain* [Ex 9:3](https://ref.ly/Exod9:3) (J) \= [Psalm 78:50](https://ref.ly/Ps78:50).

> \[Str1699] † \[דֹּ֫בֶר] **n.\[m.]** pasture (cf. Aram. דַּבְרָא, Syr. ܕܰܒܪܳܐ)—sf. כְּעֵדֶר בְּתוֺח הַדָּֽבְרוֺ *as a flock in the midst of its pasture* [Mi 2:12](https://ref.ly/Mic2:12) (art. with sf., cf. Ges§ 127, 4 b, who attaches ו as conj. to foll. word); וְרָעזּ כְבָשִים כְּדֳבְרָם *and lambs shall feed as (in) their pasture* [Is 5:17](https://ref.ly/Isa5:17).

> \[Str1702] † דֹּבְרוֺת **n.f.pl.** floats, rafts [1 K 5:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:23).

> \[Str1700] † \[דִּבְרָה] **n.f.** cause, reason, manner—cstr. עַל דִּבִרַת *because of, for the sake of* [Ec 3:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:18), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:14), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:2); עַל־דִּבְרָתִי מַלְכִּי־צֶדֶק *after the order, or manner, of Melchizedek* [Psalm 110:4](https://ref.ly/Ps110:4) (י ancient genit. ending, to soften transition in poetry, Ges§ 90 (3\) a); sf. דִּבְרָתִי *my cause, suit* [Jb 5:8](https://ref.ly/Job5:8).

> \[Str1682] † I. דְּבוֺרָה **n.f.** bee (cf. Ar. دِبْرٌ, *swarm of bees*, Aram. דַּבַּרְתָּא, Syr. ܕܶܒܘܽܪܝܺܬܳܐ) [Is 7:18](https://ref.ly/Isa7:18); pl. דְּבֹרִים [Dt 1:44](https://ref.ly/Deut1:44), Ju [14:8](https://ref.ly/Judg14:8), [Psalm 118:12](https://ref.ly/Ps118:12). Rd. also דְּבֹרוֺ *its bees* [1 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26) for MT דְּבַשׁ cf. LXX We Dr.

> \[Str1683] † II. דְּבוֺרָה **n.pr.f.** (*bee*)—**1\.** the nurse of Rebekah [Gn 35:8](https://ref.ly/Gen35:8). **2\.** the prophetess Ju [4:4](https://ref.ly/Judg4:4), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Judg4:5), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Judg4:9), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Judg4:10), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Judg4:14), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Judg5:1), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Judg5:7), [5:12](https://ref.ly/Judg5:12), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Judg5:15).

> \[Str1705] † דָּֽבְרַת **n.pr.loc.** Levitical city in Issachar, the present *Debûrije* at the foot of Mt. Tabor (RobBR iii. 210 BDPal 249\) [Jos 19:12](https://ref.ly/Josh19:12) (הַדָּֽבְרַת) [21:28](https://ref.ly/Josh21:28), [1 Ch 6:57](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:57).

> \[Str1687] † I. דְּבִיר **n.m.** (cf. Ar. دُبُرٌ *back*, دَبْرٌ *part behind*) *hindmost chamber, innermost room of the temple of Solomon* \= קדשׁ הקדשׁים *holy of holies, most holy place*, the place of the ark and the cherubic images, the throne\-room of Yahweh [1 K 6:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:5), [6:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:16), [6:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:19), [6:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:20), [6:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:21), [6:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:22), [6:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:23), [6:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:31), [7:49](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:49) ( \= [2 Ch 4:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:20)) [8:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:6), [8:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:8) ( \= [2 Ch 5:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:7), [5:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:9)) [2 Ch 3:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:16), [Psalm 28:2](https://ref.ly/Ps28:2). Prob. rd. דְּבִיר [2 K 10:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:25) also, for MT עִיר; so Klo after LXXL. (It is translated *oracle* in AV RV after Aq Sym χρηματιστήριον, Vulgate *oraculum*, on the incorrect theory that it was derived from דִּבֶּר *speak*.)

> \[Str1688] † II. דְּבִיר **1\. n.pr.m.** king of Eglon [Jos 10:3](https://ref.ly/Josh10:3). **2\. n.pr.loc. a.** דְּבִ֫רָה c. ה—ָ loc., N. border Judah [Jos 15:7](https://ref.ly/Josh15:7) (*westward* Hup [Psalm 28:2](https://ref.ly/Ps28:2)). **b.** לִדְבִר town of the Gadites [Jos 13:26](https://ref.ly/Josh13:26), perh. *Lôdebâr* [2 S 9:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:4) vid. לאֹ דְבָר Di MV. **c.** דְּבִיר Ju [1:11](https://ref.ly/Judg1:11), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Judg1:11), [1 Ch 6:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:43), דְּבִרָה [Jos 10:38](https://ref.ly/Josh10:38), [10:39](https://ref.ly/Josh10:39), elsewhere דְּבִר [Jos 15:15](https://ref.ly/Josh15:15) \+, a royal city of Canaanites anciently called קִרְיַת סֵפֶר [Jos 15:15](https://ref.ly/Josh15:15), Ju [1:11](https://ref.ly/Judg1:11), קִרְיַת סַנָּה [Jos 15:49](https://ref.ly/Josh15:49); on the mountains of Judah, in region of Hebron [Jos 11:21](https://ref.ly/Josh11:21), [15:49](https://ref.ly/Josh15:49), assigned to the Aaronite priests [Jos 21:15](https://ref.ly/Josh21:15) as a city of refuge [1 Ch 6:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:43); mod. *Dhoherîye*, 5 hours SW. fr. Hebron, acc. to Kn, so Surveyiii. 402; other conject. in Di [Jos 10:39](https://ref.ly/Josh10:39) RiHWB 265\.

> \[Str1699] † דִּבֵּר **n.\[m.]** speaking (so Ew§ 156\) [Je 5:13](https://ref.ly/Jer5:13) וְהַדִּבֵּר אֵין בָּהֶם; acc. to Hi Gf Ki (less prob.) vb. with art. for relat. (vid. \[דָּבַר] **Pi.**): but rd. rather הַדָּבָר, *and the word is not in them*.

> \[Str1703] † \[דַּבֶּ֫רֶת] **n.f.** word—יִשָּא מִדַּבְּרֹתֶיךָ *he receiveth of thy words* [Dt 33:3](https://ref.ly/Deut33:3) (poem).

> \[Str1704] † דִּבְרִי **n.pr.m.** a Danite [Lv 24:11](https://ref.ly/Lev24:11).

> \[Str4057] † I. \[מִדְבָּר] **n.m.** mouth, as organ of speech,—מִדְבָּרֵךְ נָאוֶה *thy mouth is lovely* Ct [4:3](https://ref.ly/Song4:3) ‖שִׁפְתוֺתַיִךְ, LXX λαλιά, Jer *eloquium*.

> \[Str4057] II. מִדְבָּר:270 **n.m.** wilderness—[Dt 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10) \+; c. ה loc. מִדְבָּ֫רָה [Jos 18:12](https://ref.ly/Josh18:12) \+ 15 t.; cstr. [Ex 15:22](https://ref.ly/Exod15:22) \+; מִדְבַּ֫רָה [1 K 19:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:15); sf. מִדְבָּרָהּ [Is 51:3](https://ref.ly/Isa51:3); —**1\.** tracts of land, used for the pasturage of flocks and herds, דָּֽשְׁאוּ נְאוֺת מִדְבָּר *the pastures of the wilderness put forth green grass* [Jo 2:22](https://ref.ly/Joel2:22); ´מ ´יִרְעֲפוּ נ *the pastures of the wilderness drop* (fertility) [Psalm 65:13](https://ref.ly/Ps65:13); ´מ ´יָֽבְשׁוּ נ *are dried up* [Je 23:10](https://ref.ly/Jer23:10), cf. [Je 9:9](https://ref.ly/Jer9:9), [Jo 1:19](https://ref.ly/Joel1:19), [1:20](https://ref.ly/Joel1:20). **2\.** *uninhabited land*, מִדְבָּר לאֹ־אָדָם בּוֺ *wilderness in which is no man* [Jb 38:26](https://ref.ly/Job38:26); the abode of pelicans [Psalm 102:7](https://ref.ly/Ps102:7); wild asses [Jb 24:5](https://ref.ly/Job24:5), [Je 2:24](https://ref.ly/Jer2:24); jackals [Mal 1:3](https://ref.ly/Mal1:3); ostriches [Lam 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3); מִי־יִנֵנִי בַמִּדְבָּר מְלוֺן אֹרְחִים וְאֶֽעֶזְבָה אֶת־עַמִּי *O that I had in the wilderness a lodging place of wayfarers, that I might leave my people* [Je 9:1](https://ref.ly/Jer9:1); טוֺב שֶׁבֶת בְּאֶרֶץ מִדְבָּר *better to dwell in a desert land*, than with a contentious woman [Pr 21:19](https://ref.ly/Prov21:19); בְּאֶרֶץ מִדְבָּר וּבְתֹהוּ יְלֵל יְשִׁמֹן *in a desert land, and in a waste howling wilderness* [Dt 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10). **3\.** *large tracts* of such land bearing various names, in certain districts of which there might be towns and cities: יִשְאוּ מִדְבָּר וְעָרָיו חֲצֵרִים תֵּשֵׁב קֵדָר *let the wilderness and its cities lift up* (their voice), *the villages that Kedar doth inhabit* [Is 42:11](https://ref.ly/Isa42:11). There were six cities in the wilderness of Judah [Jos 15:61](https://ref.ly/Josh15:61), [15:62](https://ref.ly/Josh15:62); הַמִּדְבָּר usually \= wilderness of the wanderings [Gn 14:6](https://ref.ly/Gen14:6), [Nu 14:16](https://ref.ly/Num14:16), [14:29](https://ref.ly/Num14:29), [14:32](https://ref.ly/Num14:32), [14:33](https://ref.ly/Num14:33), [14:33](https://ref.ly/Num14:33) \+, or the great Arabian desert Ju [11:22](https://ref.ly/Judg11:22) \+; but may also refer to any other Ct [3:6](https://ref.ly/Song3:6), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Song8:5). Special tracts—(a) of the wilderness of the wandering were שׁוּר ´מ [Ex 15:22](https://ref.ly/Exod15:22), סין[Ex 16:1](https://ref.ly/Exod16:1) \+, סיני [Ex 19:1](https://ref.ly/Exod19:1) \+, פארן [Nu 13:26](https://ref.ly/Num13:26) \+, צן [Nu 20:1](https://ref.ly/Num20:1) \+, קדשׁ [Psalm 29:8](https://ref.ly/Ps29:8), אתם [Nu 33:8](https://ref.ly/Num33:8); (b) in W. Pal. יהודה ´מ Ju [1:16](https://ref.ly/Judg1:16), [Psalm 63:1](https://ref.ly/Ps63:1) cf. [Jos 15:61](https://ref.ly/Josh15:61), מעוֺן [1 S 23:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:24), [23:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:25), זיף [1 S 23:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:15), [26:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:2), באר שׁבע [Gn 21:14](https://ref.ly/Gen21:14), עין גדי [1 S 24:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:2), תקוע [2 Ch 20:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:20), ירואל [2 Ch 20:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:16), גבעון [2 S 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:24); (c) in East. Palestine מואב [Dt 2:8](https://ref.ly/Deut2:8), אדום [2 K 3:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:8), קדמות [Dt 2:26](https://ref.ly/Deut2:26). **4\.** fig. וְשַמְתִּיהָ כַמִּדְבָּר וְשַׁתִּהָ כְּאֶרֶץ צִיָּה *and (lest I) make her as a wilderness and set her like a dry land* [Ho 2:5](https://ref.ly/Hos2:5); הֲמִדְבָּר הָיִיתִי לְיִשְרָאֵל *have I been a wilderness to Israel ?* [Je 2:31](https://ref.ly/Jer2:31).

## דבשׁ (id: BDB02018)

דבשׁ (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. ادبسّ IX. *become black* or *brown*, of a colour between black and red; vid. deriv.; so Fl v. infr.; yet vb. in this mng. perh. denom. fr. دِبْسُ i.e. having the colour of *dibs*).

> \[Str1706] † דְּבַשׁ:54 **n.m.**:[1 S 14:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:29) honey (named from colour acc. to Fl NHWBi. 430; Ar. دِبْسٌ (دُبْسٌ, دِبِسٌ) *date\-honey;* NH דְּבַשׁ, Aram. דּובשא, ܕܒܰܫ, ܕܶܒܫܳܐ, *honey* both of fruits and of bees; cf. also As. *disûpu* COTGloss ZimBP 84 DlPr 70\)—abs. ´ד [Gn 43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11) \+ 33 t.; דְּבָשׁ [Ex 3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8) \+ 18 t.; sf. דִּבְשִׁי Ct [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1); —*honey*, product of bees, used as food Ju [14:8](https://ref.ly/Judg14:8), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Judg14:9), [14:18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18) (in lion's carcass); found in (clefts of) rock [Dt 32:13](https://ref.ly/Deut32:13) (מִסֶּלַע ´ד, in song, JE), [Psalm 81:17](https://ref.ly/Ps81:17) (´מִצּוּר ד); in forest, on ground [1 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:25) (על־פני השדה) v:[26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26) (´הֵלֶח ד; but read rather הָלַח דְּבֹרוֺ *its bees had departed*, We Dr after LXX) v:[29](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:29), v:[43](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:43); transported in jar or bottle ´בַּקְבֻּק ד [1 K 14:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:3); contrib. to priests and Levites [2 Ch 31:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:5); ‖חֶמְאָה [2 S 17:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:29), [Is 7:15](https://ref.ly/Isa7:15), [7:22](https://ref.ly/Isa7:22) (in last two the being limited to such food is apparently token of destitution); kept in store [Je 41:8](https://ref.ly/Jer41:8); a choice gift [Gn 43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11) (J); article of trade Ez [27:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:17) (in these two perh. \= *grape syrup*, mod. *dibs*, cf. Di [Gn 43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11) v. RobBR ii. 81\); forbidden as burnt offering [Lv 2:11](https://ref.ly/Lev2:11) (‖שְאֹר); *honey comb* \= ´יַעְרַת הַדּ [1 S 14:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:27) (cf. יַעְרִי Ct [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1)); also ´צוּף ד[Pr 16:24](https://ref.ly/Prov16:24) (cf. נֹפֶת צופים [Psalm 19:11](https://ref.ly/Ps19:11)); most often in phrase describing abundance in land of Canaan ´אֶרֶץ זָבַת חָלָב וּד [Ex 3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Exod3:17), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Exod13:5), [33:3](https://ref.ly/Exod33:3) (all JE) [Lv 20:24](https://ref.ly/Lev20:24) (H) [Nu 13:27](https://ref.ly/Num13:27), [14:8](https://ref.ly/Num14:8), [16:18](https://ref.ly/Num16:18), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Num16:14) (all JE) [Dt 6:3](https://ref.ly/Deut6:3), [11:9](https://ref.ly/Deut11:9), [26:9](https://ref.ly/Deut26:9), [26:15](https://ref.ly/Deut26:15), [27:3](https://ref.ly/Deut27:3), [31:20](https://ref.ly/Deut31:20) (all D) [Jos 5:6](https://ref.ly/Josh5:6) (D) [Je 11:5](https://ref.ly/Jer11:5), [32:22](https://ref.ly/Jer32:22), Ez [20:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:6), [20:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:15); cf. [Dt 8:8](https://ref.ly/Deut8:8), [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32) (of land of captivity, words of the Rabshak); v. also Ez [16:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:13), [16:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:19), [Jb 20:17](https://ref.ly/Job20:17) וְחֶמְאָה ´נַחֲלֵי ד; *honey*, used to illustrate moral teachings [Pr 24:13](https://ref.ly/Prov24:13) (‖נֹפֶת), [25:16](https://ref.ly/Prov25:16), [25:27](https://ref.ly/Prov25:27) (danger of surfeit); sim. of sweetness of taste Ez [3:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:3) (the roll), cf. כְּחַפִּיחִת ´בִּד [Ex 16:31](https://ref.ly/Exod16:31) (P; description of manna); of sweetness of the law [Psalm 19:11](https://ref.ly/Ps19:11) (‖נֹפֶת צוּפִים) cf. [119:103](https://ref.ly/Ps119:103); (of pleasant words [Pr 16:24](https://ref.ly/Prov16:24) cf. supr.); sweetness of lips וְחָלָב ´ד Ct [4:11](https://ref.ly/Song4:11); fig. of love דִּבְשִׁי Ct [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1) (‖יַעְרִי, יֵינִי, חֲלָבִי).

> \[Str1707,1708] † \[דַּבֶּ֫שֶׁת] **1\. n.f.** hump (of camel) (etymol.?) cstr. [Is 30:6](https://ref.ly/Isa30:6) על־דַּבֶּשֶׁת גְּמַלִּים. **2\. n.pr.loc.** on border of Zebulum [Jos 19:11](https://ref.ly/Josh19:11) דַּבָּשֶׁת.

> \[Str3031] † יִדְבָּשׁ **n.pr.m.** a son of Etam ? [1 Ch 4:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:3); MT has וְיִדְבָּשׁ…וְאֵלָּה אֲבִי עֵיטָם יִזְרְעֶאל LXX οὗτοι υἱοἰ Αἰτάν, cf. Vulgate; \= ´וְאֵלָּה בְנֵי ע cf. Be Öt ( \< ´אלה בני אבי ע, as some MSS.) On real significance of names cf. עֵיטָם.

## דָּגָה (id: BDB02022)

\[Str1711] † \[דָּגָה] **vb. multiply, increase** (intr.);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. וְיִדְגּוּ לָרֹב בקרב הארץ [Gn 48:16](https://ref.ly/Gen48:16) (E).

> \[Str1709] † דָּג **n.m.**:[Jon 2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:1) **fish** (NH *id.*)—דָּג[Jon 2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:17), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:17), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:10) \+ [Ne 13:16](https://ref.ly/Neh13:16) Qr (Kt דָּאג); דָּגִים [1 K 5:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:13) \+ 6 t.; דְּגֵי [Gn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2) \+ 7 t.;—*fish* of sea [Jon 2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:1), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:17), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:10) (in v:[1](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:17) גָּדוֺל ´ד); [Ne 13:16](https://ref.ly/Neh13:16) (דָּאג Kt דָּג article of Tyrian trade; only here coll., rd. perh. דָּגָה; or regard as late usage, cf. converse דָּגָה of individual [Jon 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:2));—elsewhere always pl.: as subj. of Solomon's utterance [1 K 5:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:13); fish\-spear צִלְצַל דגים [Jb 40:31](https://ref.ly/Job40:31); most often דְּגֵי הַיָּם[Gn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2) (P), [Psalm 8:9](https://ref.ly/Ps8:9), [Ho 4:3](https://ref.ly/Hos4:3), [Zp 1:3](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:3), Ez [38:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:20), [Jb 12:8](https://ref.ly/Job12:8) (in all opp. to beasts \& birds, \& appar. used with them, \+ רֶמֶש Ez. cf. vb. רמש Gn, for animal creation in general; also Hb [1:14](https://ref.ly/Hab1:14) cf. infr.); [Nu 11:22](https://ref.ly/Num11:22) (JE) of fish (with beasts) as food (cf. [Gn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2) f.) in simile of men ensnared, like fish taken in net (מְצוֺדָה) [Ec 9:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:12) cf. Hb [1:14](https://ref.ly/Hab1:14) (דְּגֵי הַיָּם); 4 t. שַׁעַר הַדָּגִים *fish\-gate* [2 Ch 33:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:14), [Zp 1:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:10), [Ne 3:3](https://ref.ly/Neh3:3), [12:39](https://ref.ly/Neh12:39) (on situation cf. שַׁעַר).

B Co); sf. דְּגָתוֺ Ez [47:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:10) LXX Vulgate Co; (MT דגתם), דְּגָתָם [Is 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2), [Psalm 105:29](https://ref.ly/Ps105:29) \+ Ez [47:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:10) MT cf. supr.—*fish*, almost always coll.; fish of sea דְּגַת הַיָּם [Gn 1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26), [1:28](https://ref.ly/Gen1:28) (P) Ez [47:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:10) (in sim.); in Nile (יְאֹר) [Ex 7:18](https://ref.ly/Exod7:18), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Exod7:21) (E) cf. [Psalm 105:29](https://ref.ly/Ps105:29), דְּגַת יְאֹרֶיךָ Ez [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4), [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4) (cf. supr.) v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:5); in sea and rivers [Is 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2), Ez [47:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:9), [47:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:10); food in Egypt [Nu 11:5](https://ref.ly/Num11:5) (JE); image of fish forbidden in worship, as of beasts and birds [Dt 4:18](https://ref.ly/Deut4:18) (on sacredness of fish, and use in sacrifice cf. > \[Str1728,1771] † \[דַּיָּג] **n.m.**:Ex 47:10 fisher, fisherman, only pl. דַּיָּגִים [Is 19:8](https://ref.ly/Isa19:8), דַּיָּגִים Qr [Je 16:16](https://ref.ly/Jer16:16) (Kt דוגים), דַּוָּגִיםEz [47:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:10) \& Kt [Je 16:16](https://ref.ly/Jer16:16).

> \[Str1729] † דּוּגָה **n.f.** fishing, fishery, only in וְנִשָּא בְּסִירוֺת דּוּגָה [Am 4:2](https://ref.ly/Amos4:2) (‖בְּצִנּוֺת) of ´י, metaph. for dragging Isr. captive (cf. for practice referred to, in case of fish, Ez [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4) \& [Jb 40:26](https://ref.ly/Job40:26) scornful summons to do it with crocodile, if possible ! v. Herodii. 70 on mode of capturing crocodiles).

> \[Str1712] † דָּגוֺן **n.pr.m.**: [1 S 5:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:4) god \& idol of Philistines (cf. As. *Dagan, Dakan(nu)*, name perh. non\-Shemitic COT Ju [16:23](https://ref.ly/Judg16:23) DlPa 139 SayceRel. Bab. 188 f. but v. Jen infr.)—god of Ashdod, exc. Ju [16:23](https://ref.ly/Judg16:23) (Gaza) \& [1 Ch 10:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:10) (but cf. infr.);—דגון אֱלֽהֵיהֶם Ju [16:23](https://ref.ly/Judg16:23) cf. דגון אֱלֽהֵינוּ [1 S 5:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:7), ´בֵּית ד[1 S 5:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:2), [5:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:5), [1 Ch 10:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:10) (here hardly orig. cf. ‖ [1 S 31:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:10) \& We Dr); ´כֹּהֲנֵי ד [1 S 5:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:5), ´מִפְתַּן ד v:[5](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:5); as name of image ´ד (אֵצֶל) v:[2](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:2), v:[3](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:3), v:[4](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:4), דגון נֹפֵל v:[3](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:3), v:[4](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:4), נִשְׁאַר עָלָיו ´רַק ד v:[4](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:4) (but sense difficult; We prop. דָּגוֺ *only his fish*, i.e. fishy part, *was left upon him*, v. also Dr;—but was Dagon a fish\-god ? Cf. works cited above \& ScholzGötzendienst 238 ff., Baud in PRE2 iii. 460 ff. esp. JenKosmol. 449 ff.) Vid. בֵּית דָּגוֺן p. III.
> 
> 186:a. דָּגוֺן: oldest tradition (Jerome) seems to waver between דָּג *fish* and דָּגָן *grain,* as explan. of דָּגוֺן, v. בית.

## דָּגַל (id: BDB02029)

\[Str1713] † \[דָּגַל] **vb.** look, bahold (As. *dagâlu*, LotzTP 131; Thes RobGes MV SS De make דָּגוּל a denom. fr. דֶּגֶל \= *lifted up like a banner*, or *furnished with a banner*, i.e. *exalted, distinguished*, AV RV *chiefest*);—*Pt. pass.* דָּגוּל מֵרְבָבָה Ct [5:10](https://ref.ly/Song5:10) *looked at, conspicuous*, acc. to DlHA 40\.

> \[Str1714] † \[דֶּ֫גֶל] **n.m.**:[Nu 10:14](https://ref.ly/Num10:14) **standard, banner** (As. *diglu* DlHA 40 Pr 58; cf. also Di [Nu 2:2](https://ref.ly/Num2:2)); cstr. דֶּגֶל [Nu 2:3](https://ref.ly/Num2:3) \+ 7 t.; sf. דִּגְלוֺ [Nu 1:52](https://ref.ly/Num1:52) \+ 2 t.; pl. sf. דִּגְלֵיהֶם [Nu 2:17](https://ref.ly/Num2:17), [2:31](https://ref.ly/Num2:31), [2:34](https://ref.ly/Num2:34); —*standard*, partic. of separate tribes of Isr. מחנה יהודה ´ד etc. [Nu 2:3](https://ref.ly/Num2:3), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Num2:10), [2:18](https://ref.ly/Num2:18), [2:25](https://ref.ly/Num2:25), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Num10:14), [10:18](https://ref.ly/Num10:18), [10:22](https://ref.ly/Num10:22), [10:25](https://ref.ly/Num10:25); cf. also [1:52](https://ref.ly/Num1:52), [2:2](https://ref.ly/Num2:2) (‖אֹתֹת) v:[17](https://ref.ly/Num2:17), v:[31](https://ref.ly/Num2:31), v:[34](https://ref.ly/Num2:34) (all P.); fig. Ct [2:4](https://ref.ly/Song2:4) ודגלו עלי אהבה.

> \[Str1713] † \[דָּגַל] **vb. denom. carry**, or set up standard, banner—**Qal** *Impf.* 1 pl. נִדְנֹּל [Psalm 20:6](https://ref.ly/Ps20:6) (Gr Che נָגִיל; BI נְגַדֵּל, after LXX SyrVer; Now נִגְדַּל; poss. נַגְדִּל ?) *set up standard* in battle, cf. DlPr 61\. **Niph.** *Pt.* fpl. נִדְגָּלוֺת Ct [6:4](https://ref.ly/Song6:4), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Song6:10) *bannered, supplied with standards*, pt. as subst., of *bannered hosts*, cf. De.

## דגן (id: BDB02032)

דגן (√ of foll. mng. dub.; NH דַּגֵּן Pa. *heap up* etc. seems to be denom.; Ol:§ 215 b. 4 der. דָּגָן fr. דָּגָה, cf. MV; WeSkizzen iii. 170 inclines to regard דָּגָן as der. fr. n.pr. divin. דגן (cf. דָּגוֺן), as *cereal* fr. *Ceres*).

Ph. ארצת דגן)—דָּגָן [Gn 27:28](https://ref.ly/Gen27:28) \+ 29 t.; cstr. דְּגַן [Psalm 78:24](https://ref.ly/Ps78:24); sf. דְּגָנִי [Ho 2:11](https://ref.ly/Hos2:11), דְּגָֽנְךָ [Dt 7:13](https://ref.ly/Deut7:13), [12:17](https://ref.ly/Deut12:17), [14:23](https://ref.ly/Deut14:23), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Deut18:4), דְּגֶָ\+נךָ [Dt 11:14](https://ref.ly/Deut11:14), דְּגָנֵךְ [Is 62:8](https://ref.ly/Isa62:8), דְּגָנָם [Psalm 4:8](https://ref.ly/Ps4:8), [65:10](https://ref.ly/Ps65:10)—*corn*, c. תִּירוֺשׁ *must*, q.v., [Gn 27:28](https://ref.ly/Gen27:28), [27:37](https://ref.ly/Gen27:37) (both J) [Psalm 4:8](https://ref.ly/Ps4:8), [Is 62:8](https://ref.ly/Isa62:8), [Ho 2:11](https://ref.ly/Hos2:11), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Hos9:1), [Zc 9:17](https://ref.ly/Zech9:17); cf. also [Nu 18:27](https://ref.ly/Num18:27) (P; here, for תירושׁ, מְלֵאָה מִן־הַָ\+יּקֶב), [La 2:12](https://ref.ly/Lam2:12) (where יַיִן), [Ho 14:8](https://ref.ly/Hos14:8) (where גֶּפֶן \& לְבָנוֺן יֵין); ארץ דגן ותירושׁ of land of Canaan [Dt 33:28](https://ref.ly/Deut33:28); of land of captiv. (words of the Rabshak) [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32) \= [Is 36:17](https://ref.ly/Isa36:17) (other products, also, named in both); usually c. תירושׁ \& יצהר [Nu 18:12](https://ref.ly/Num18:12), [Dt 7:13](https://ref.ly/Deut7:13), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Deut11:14), [12:17](https://ref.ly/Deut12:17), [14:23](https://ref.ly/Deut14:23), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Deut18:4), [28:51](https://ref.ly/Deut28:51), [2 Ch 31:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:5) (\+ דבשׁ) [32:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:28), [Ne 5:11](https://ref.ly/Neh5:11), [10:40](https://ref.ly/Neh10:40), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Neh13:5), [13:12](https://ref.ly/Neh13:12), [Je 31:12](https://ref.ly/Jer31:12), [Ho 2:10](https://ref.ly/Hos2:10), [2:24](https://ref.ly/Hos2:24), [Jo 1:10](https://ref.ly/Joel1:10), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Joel2:19), [Hg 1:11](https://ref.ly/Hag1:11); also [Jo 1:17](https://ref.ly/Joel1:17) Me; (cf. [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32), [Is 36:17](https://ref.ly/Isa36:17) supr.); alone, rare and late [Ne 5:2](https://ref.ly/Neh5:2), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Neh5:3), [5:10](https://ref.ly/Neh5:10), [Psalm 65:10](https://ref.ly/Ps65:10), Ez [36:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:29); דְּגַן־שָׁמַיִם [Psalm 78:24](https://ref.ly/Ps78:24); —of the above the following refer to firstfruits offered to the priests [Nu 18:12](https://ref.ly/Num18:12) (cf. v:[27](https://ref.ly/Num18:27)) [Dt 18:4](https://ref.ly/Deut18:4), [2 Ch 31:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:5); to tithe [Dt 12:17](https://ref.ly/Deut12:17), [14:23](https://ref.ly/Deut14:23), [Ne 13:5](https://ref.ly/Neh13:5), [13:12](https://ref.ly/Neh13:12); to both generally [Ne 10:40](https://ref.ly/Neh10:40); to loaning on usury [Ne 5:10](https://ref.ly/Neh5:10), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Neh5:11).

Aram. דְּגַר *heap together;* so ܕܓܰܪ Nasar.)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. דָּגָר [Je 17:11](https://ref.ly/Jer17:11); 3 fs. דָּֽגְרָה [Is 34:15](https://ref.ly/Isa34:15)—*gather together* into its shadow [Is 34:15](https://ref.ly/Isa34:15) (of קִפֹּז, q.v.), דָּגָר וְלאֹ יָלָדָ [Je 17:11](https://ref.ly/Jer17:11) (of partridge; sim. of one getting riches unjustly).

[7:18](https://ref.ly/Prov7:18)); of Samaria \& Jerusalem under fig. of young women Ez [23:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:3) (דַּדֵּי בְּתוּלֵיהֶן) cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:8); דַּדַּיִח v:[21](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:21); but prob. del. ´ד in Ez cf. supr. \& GeiUrachrift 397\.—Vid. also שַׁד sub שׁדה.

## דָּדָה (id: BDB02037)

\[Str1718] † \[דָּדָה] **vb.** \[move slowly] (NH Pi. דִּדָּה *lead slowly*, Hithp. הִדַּדָּה; Aram. דַּדִּי, אִידַּדִּי; Ar. دأدأ is *run quickly* (of a camel): on form, cf. Sta§ 112 a, 150 a. 129 b, 581 d)—**Pi.** *Impf.* 1 s. sf. אֲדַדֵּם (so read for MT אֶדַּדֵּם Dy BI \& Che, cf. his crit. note) *lead slowly* (in procession) [Psalm 42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5). **Hithp.** *Impf.* 1 s. אֶדַּדֶּה [Is 38:15](https://ref.ly/Isa38:15); also sf. אֶדַּדֵּם [Psalm 42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5) MT but cf. supr.;—*walk deliberately*, at ease (De Che Di), or as in procession (Ew), [Is 38:15](https://ref.ly/Isa38:15) (cf. Che Di VB).

> \[Str1734] דדי Kt v. דּוֺדוֺ sub דוד

> \[Str1719] † דְּדָן **n.pr.loc.** \& **gent.**;—´ד [Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) \+ 8 t. \+ Ez [27:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:20) (but v. infr.) c. ה loc. דְּדָ֫נֶה Ez [25:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:13) cf. Ges§ 90, 2 ad fin.;—**1\.** under fig. of son of רַעֲמָה and grandson of כּוּשׁ (v. these arts.) [Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) (brother of שְׁבָא) \= [1 Ch 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:9), Ez [27:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:20); cf. ´בְּנֵי ד v:[15](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:15) (but rd. here perh. רדן LXX υἱοὶ Ῥοδίων, cf. StaDe Pop. Javan. 11, also SerVer ܕܪܢ Co; v. sub דֹּדָנִים infr.) cf. also Ez [38:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:13), ´שְׁבָא וּד; also [Je 25:23](https://ref.ly/Jer25:23); south\-Arabian tribe on Persian Gulf, \= Sab. בנו דדן acc. to DHMZMG 1876, 122; Sab. Denkm. 28 (dubit. Mordt); but in NW. of Arabian peninsula, GlaserSkizze ii. 391 ff.; perhaps orig. further south cf. Di [Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7). **2\.** as son of יָקְשָׁן father of אַשּׁוּרִם etc., [Gn 25:3](https://ref.ly/Gen25:3), [25:3](https://ref.ly/Gen25:3) (here also brother of שְׁבָא) \= [1 Ch 1:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:32); [Je 49:8](https://ref.ly/Jer49:8) ´ישְׁבֵי ד. This is appar. a northern branch of **1**, cf. Di [Gn 25:3](https://ref.ly/Gen25:3).—On דדן in MI31, MI31 cf. Sm \& SoMI p. 29 NöLCB Jan. 8, 1887 Cl GannJAs. Jan. 1887\. 107\.

> \[Str1721] † דֹּדָנִים **n.pr.gent.pl.** [Gn 10:4](https://ref.ly/Gen10:4); but \= רוֺדָנִים [1 Ch 1:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:7); rd. here רדנים, so LXX Sam Di StaDe Pop. Javan. 11; cf. Ez [27:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:15) supr. sub דְּדָן.

## דָּהַר (id: BDB02044)

\[Str1725] † \[דָּהַר] **vb.** rush, dash, of horse (onomat. ? perh. cf. Ar. دَهَرَ *befall, fall upon, overcome, conquer*)—**Qal** *Pt.* סוּס דֹּהֵר [Na 3:2](https://ref.ly/Nah3:2).

> \[Str1726] † \[דַּהֲרָה] **n.f.** rushing, dashing, of riders—*Pl.* cstr. דַּהֲרוֺת דַּהֲרוֺת אַבִּירָיו Ju [5:22](https://ref.ly/Judg5:22); repetition for intensity, *furious dashing*.

> \[Str8410] † תִּדְהָר **n.\[m.]** name of a tree, prob. **elm** (Syr. ܕܰܕܳܪ (or ܕܶܕܳܪ PS) LagBN 130 Löw71; Ar. دَرْدَارٌ Lane864 Dozyi. 432 (also *ash*, v. Dozyib.); so Symm \& Vulgate [Is 41:19](https://ref.ly/Isa41:19); cf. Di, where also other views, e.g. *plane\-tree* (Rabb. Che), or *pine;*—√dub.; Ges [Is 41:19](https://ref.ly/Isa41:19) Thes der. from דהר *endure* inferred from Ar. دَهْرٌ *time*, but this questionable; perh. a loan\-word), בְּרוֺשׁ תִּדְהָר וּתְאַשּׁוּר יַחְדָּו [Is 41:19](https://ref.ly/Isa41:19) \= [60:13](https://ref.ly/Isa60:13).

* **Associated Passages:** Nahum 3:2; Judges 5:22; Isaiah 41:19; Isaiah 60:13


## דּוּב (id: BDB02047)

Di; in [1 S 2:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:33) read prob. *Inf.* לְהָדִיב את נפשׁך (for MT לַאֲדִיב) so Dr; trans. to *cause thy* (al. his) *soul to pine away* (cf. VB).

MI12, cf. דָּוִד infra)—**1\.** man of tribe of Issachar Ju [10:1](https://ref.ly/Judg10:1). **2\.** דדי Kt [2 S 23:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:9) ( \= דּוֺדַי [1 Ch 27:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:4)), דּוֺדוֺ Qr \= דּוֺדוֺ [1 Ch 11:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:12). **3\.** father of אֶלְחָנָן [2 S 23:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:24) דּוֺדוֺ \= [1 Ch 11:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:26).

> \[Str1737] † דּוֺדַי **n.pr.m.** \= דּוֺדוֺ (q.v.) [1 Ch 27:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:4) (cf. דדי [2 S 23:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:9) Kt).

> \[Str1735] † דּוֺדָוָהוּ **n.pr.m.** (\< LXX τοῦ Ὠδεια LXXL Δουδιου, i.e. דּוֺדִיָּהוּ, *beloved of* ´י, cf. NesEg 70\) father of Eliezer [2 Ch 20:37](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:37).

[2 S 7:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:11)–[15](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:15) etc.) (*beloved one ?* cf. BaNB 189; acc. to SayceMod. Rev. 1884, 158 ff. Rel. > \[Str1736] † \[דּוּדַי] **n.m.**:[Gn 30:14](https://ref.ly/Gen30:14) mandrake (as love\-producing, cf. Di [Gn 30:14](https://ref.ly/Gen30:14))—pl. דּוּדָאִים (cf. Ew189 g Sta§§ 301\. 122\) [Gn 30:14](https://ref.ly/Gen30:14), Ct [7:14](https://ref.ly/Song7:13); cstr. דּוּדָאֵי [Gn 30:14](https://ref.ly/Gen30:14) \+ 3 t.;—*mandrakes,* as exciting sexual desire, and favouring procreation [Gn 30:14](https://ref.ly/Gen30:14), [30:14](https://ref.ly/Gen30:14), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Gen30:15), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Gen30:15), [30:16](https://ref.ly/Gen30:16) (J); also Ct [7:14](https://ref.ly/Song7:13) where odour referred to. On דּוּדָאֵי [Je 24:1](https://ref.ly/Jer24:1) *vessels, baskets,* v. דּוּד.

> \[Str1731] † דּוּד **n.m.**:[Je 24:2](https://ref.ly/Jer24:2) pot, jar (Aram. דּוּדָא ܕܽܘܕܳܐ perh. cf. Syr. ܕܰܘܶܕ *disturb,* from the idea of boiling)—abs. דּוּד [1 S 2:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:14) \+ 4 t.; pl. דּוּדִים [2 K 10:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:7), דְּוַדִים [2 Ch 35:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:13); pl. cstr. דּוּדָאֵי [Je 24:1](https://ref.ly/Jer24:1)—**a.** *pot, kettle* or cooking (בִּשֵּׁל) [1 S 2:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:14) (‖פָּרוּר, קַלַּחַת, כִּיּוֺר), [2 Ch 35:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:13) (vb. בִּשֵּׁל, ‖צַלָּחוֺת, סִירוֺת) cf. [Jb 41:12](https://ref.ly/Job41:12); **b.** receptacle for carrying, all Vrss *basket,* [2 K 10:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:7) (heads of king's sons), [Je 24:2](https://ref.ly/Jer24:2), [24:2](https://ref.ly/Jer24:2) (figs) cf. דּוּדָאֵי [Je 24:1](https://ref.ly/Jer24:1), as if fr. a II. \[דּוּדַי]; [Psalm 81:7](https://ref.ly/Ps81:7) (clay or bricks, ‖סֵבֶל).

## דָּוָה (id: BDB02059)

\[Str1738] † \[דָּוָה] **vb.** be ill, unwell (NH ´ד *sorrowful, miserable,* not in physical sense; cf. Ar. \[ܕܐ] دَوِىَ *be ill;* Eth. ደወየ፡ As. perh. deriv. *di’û,* *illness* ZimBP 96, 97; Aram. דְּוִי, דְּוָא ܕܘܳܐ, ܕܘܺܝ, *be sad*)—**Qal** *Inf. cstr.* כִּימֵי נִדַּת דְּוֺתָהּ…אִשָּׁה [Lv 12:2](https://ref.ly/Lev12:2).

> \[Str1741] † דְּוַי **n.\[m.]** illness, עֶרֶש דְּוָ\+י [Psalm 41:4](https://ref.ly/Ps41:4) *bed of languishing* (Che); sg. cstr. (Ew De Di) הֵמָּה כִּדְוֵי לַחְמִי [Jb 6:7](https://ref.ly/Job6:7) *they* (i.e. my sufferings) *are like disease* (VB *loathsomeness*) *in my meat,* cf. Di; but txt. dub.

> \[Str1739] † דָּוֶה **adj.** faint, unwell —דָּוֶה [La 5:17](https://ref.ly/Lam5:17), f. דָּוָה [Lv 15:33](https://ref.ly/Lev15:33) \+ 3 t.; **1\.** *faint,* [La 1:13](https://ref.ly/Lam1:13) (‖שֹׁמֵמָה) [5:17](https://ref.ly/Lam5:17). **2\.** *unwell, menstruous* [Lv 15:33](https://ref.ly/Lev15:33), [20:18](https://ref.ly/Lev20:18); [Is 30:22](https://ref.ly/Isa30:22) דָּוָה perh. \= כְּלִי דָוָה De.

> \[Str4064] † \[מַדְוֶה] **n.m.**:[Dt 7:15](https://ref.ly/Deut7:15) sickness—cstr. sg. מַדְיֵה מִצְרַיִם [Dt 28:60](https://ref.ly/Deut28:60) (rd. מַדְוֵר pl., as [7:15](https://ref.ly/Deut7:15) ?); cstr. pl. מַדְוֵי מִצְרַיִם [Dt 7:15](https://ref.ly/Deut7:15) (both ‖חֳלִי).

> \[Str1742] † \[דַּוַּי] **adj.** faint (on form cf. BaNB 487\)—דַּוָּ\+י [Is 1:5](https://ref.ly/Isa1:5) \+ 2 t.—*faint,* always of heart [Is 1:5](https://ref.ly/Isa1:5) (‖לָחֳלִי fig. of condition of people); [Je 8:18](https://ref.ly/Jer8:18), [La 1:22](https://ref.ly/Lam1:22) of sorrow and distress.

> \[Str1773] † דְּיוֺ **n.m.** ink (NH *id.,* Aram. דְּיוּתָא ܕܝܽܘܬܳܐ; Ar. دَوَاةٌ *inkbottle, inkhorn;* Ges\-Dietr Fl NHWBi. 41 der. fr. √ דוה in assumed sense of *slowly flowing;* Fl comp. Ar. دِواية دُواية *thin skin on surface of milk,* cf. also O1:§ 178 g), וַאֲנִי כֹּתֵב עַל־הַסֵּפֶר בַּדְּ\+יוֺ [Je 36:18](https://ref.ly/Jer36:18), cf. LagGes. Abh. 216\.—On erasable quality of Hebrew ink cf. RSOTJC, 400 f. ed. 2, 71; v. further L. LöwGraphische Requisiten etc. bel den Juden, 1870, i. 145 ff.

[50:17](https://ref.ly/Jer50:17)); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָדִיחַ [Is 4:4](https://ref.ly/Isa4:4); 3 mpl. יָדִיחוּ [2 Ch 4:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:6) \+ Ez [40:38](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:38) (Co conj. הַקָּדִים);—**1\.** *rinse,* victims to be offered in sacrifice Ez [40:38](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:38), so [2 Ch 4:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:6) (cf. supr.) **2\.** fig. *cleanse* by washing [Is 4:4](https://ref.ly/Isa4:4), of removing guilt.

Δοῦμα in the Daroma, 17 miles fr. Eleutheropolis LagOnom. 250\. 2nd ed. 258, \= mod. *Daume,* SW. fr. Hebron van de VeldeMemoir 308 GuêrinJudêe iii. 359 ff. cf. (JDMich Ges Hi think **3 \= 1;** LXX has Ἰδουμαια; Jerinterpr. as *part* of Idumea; still another view sub I. דּוּמָה).

I. דָּמָה, LXX πρέπει, v. Che); [22:3](https://ref.ly/Ps22:3) *repose* (fr. grief and tears: [Je 14:17](https://ref.ly/Jer14:17)).
