## דּוּר (id: BDB02081)

\[Str1752] דּוּר **vb. 1\.** heap up, pile. **2\.** dwell (orig. *move in a circle, go about, surround,* cf. Ar. دَارَ; دَارٌ *house,* esp. as group of buildings about a court; As. *dûru, wall, fortress,* COTGloss; thence (denom. Fl NH WBi. 440\) *dwell* Aram. דּוּר, ܕܳܪ (pt. \& Pa), hence in Heb. cf. GiesebrZAW i. 304 Fll.c. CheOP 479; Eth. ድኣር፡ *generation* (\= דּוֺר: prob. loan\-word); acc. to Hpt KAT2 Gloss. i. As. \[*daru§, endure*], *dârû*, *everlasting* (cf. BezOrient. Diplom.) \= Ar. دَهْرٌ *long time, age*)—**Qal** in *Inf.* מִדּוּר באהלי־רשׁע [Psalm 84:11](https://ref.ly/Ps84:11) *than to dwell in tents of wickedness. Imv.* (or *Inf. abs.* irreg. for דּוֺר, cf. ‖לָקוֺחַ) דּוּר העצמים Ez [24:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:5) *heap up.*—In [Gn 6:3](https://ref.ly/Gen6:3) some rd. *Impf.* 3 ms. יָדוּר *shall dwell,* for ידון; v. דין.

189:b. דּוּר *circle, ball*—**1\.** *circle:* Du:[Is 29:3](https://ref.ly/Isa29:3) thinks noun here also is כַּדּוּר (or כִּדּוֺר) \= כִּידוֺר *onset of combatants;* Marti כְּדָוִד aa LXX Lo Brd al.

> \[Str1754] † דּוּר **n.\[m.]** circle, ball—**1\.** *circle* וְחָנִיתִי כַּדּוּר עליך [Is 29:3](https://ref.ly/Isa29:3) ( \> LXX Brd כְּדָוִד). **2\.** *ball* צָנוֺף יִצְנָפְךָ צְנֵפָה כַּדּוּר [Is 22:18](https://ref.ly/Isa22:18) (so most; but BöNÄ ii. 134 De Or JastrPAOS 1888, xcv. ff. regard noun as כַּדּוּר, *ball,* as Talm., cf. also MV SS s.v.; vid. כדר). \[**3\.** *heap, pile* Ez [24:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:5) Thes Ke MV cf. מְדוּרָה v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:9); but v. supr. sub דּוּר **vb.**]

> \[Str1755] † I. דּוֺר, דֹּר **n.m.**:[Dt 32:5](https://ref.ly/Deut32:5) period, generation, dwelling (cf. sub **vb.** supr.; also NH דּוֺר, Aram. (incl. B Aram.) דָּר, ܕܳܪ, *age, generation;* Ar. دَوْرٌ *gyrus, orbis, periodus;* Sab. דר, *one time, once,* דרם דרם *each once* in a year DHM in MV)—abs. דּוֺר [Gn 7:1](https://ref.ly/Gen7:1) \+ 68 t.; דֹּר [Ex 3:15](https://ref.ly/Exod3:15); 36 t.; cstr. דּוֺר [Dt 32:20](https://ref.ly/Deut32:20) \+ 7 t., דֹּר [Ex 3:15](https://ref.ly/Exod3:15); sf. דּוֺרִי [Is 38:12](https://ref.ly/Isa38:12), דּוֺרוֺ [Is 53:8](https://ref.ly/Isa53:8); pl. דּוֺרִים [Psalm 72:5](https://ref.ly/Ps72:5) \+ 2 t., דֹּרוֺת abs. [Jb 42:16](https://ref.ly/Job42:16), [Is 41:4](https://ref.ly/Isa41:4); cstr. Ju [3:2](https://ref.ly/Judg3:2); דּוֺרוֺת cstr. [Is 51:9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9), דֹּרֹת [Gn 9:12](https://ref.ly/Gen9:12); sf. דֹּרֹתָיו [Gn 6:9](https://ref.ly/Gen6:9), [Lv 25:30](https://ref.ly/Lev25:30), דּוֺרוֺתֵינוּ [Jos 22:27](https://ref.ly/Josh22:27), דֹּרֹתֵינוּ v:[28](https://ref.ly/Josh22:28), דּרֹתֵיכֶם [Gn 17:12](https://ref.ly/Gen17:12) \+ 27 t., דֹּרֹתָם [Gn 17:7](https://ref.ly/Gen17:7) \+ 10 t.;—**1\.** *period, age, generation,* mostly poet.: **a.** of duration in the past, *former age(s)* דּוֺרוֺת עוֺלָמִים [Is 51:9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9) (‖קדם ימי); שְׁנוֺת דֹּר וָדֹר [Dt 32:7](https://ref.ly/Deut32:7) (song; ‖ימות עולם); בְּדֹר וָדֹר [Psalm 90:1](https://ref.ly/Ps90:1) (cf. v:[2](https://ref.ly/Ps90:2)); מוֺסְדֵי דּוֺר־וָדוֺר [Is 58:12](https://ref.ly/Isa58:12) (‖חרבות עולם); שֹׁמֲמוֺת דּוֺר וָדוֺר [61:4](https://ref.ly/Isa61:4) (‖ *id.*) **b.** usually of duration to come, *future age(s),* לְדֹר דֹּר [Ex 3:15](https://ref.ly/Exod3:15) (E; ‖לעולם) cf. [Pr 27:24](https://ref.ly/Prov27:24) Kt (Qr לְדוֺר וָדוֺר; ‖ *id.*); מִדֹּר דֹּר [Ex 17:16](https://ref.ly/Exod17:16) (E), \= מִדּוֺר לָדוֺר [Is 34:10](https://ref.ly/Isa34:10); לְדֹר וָדֹר [Psalm 10:6](https://ref.ly/Ps10:6), [33:11](https://ref.ly/Ps33:11) (‖לעולם) [49:12](https://ref.ly/Ps49:12), [79:13](https://ref.ly/Ps79:13), [85:6](https://ref.ly/Ps85:6), [102:13](https://ref.ly/Ps102:13), [119:90](https://ref.ly/Ps119:90), [135:13](https://ref.ly/Ps135:13), [146:10](https://ref.ly/Ps146:10), [Jo 4:20](https://ref.ly/Joel4:20) (all ‖ *id.*); [Psalm 89:2](https://ref.ly/Ps89:2) (‖עולם) [89:5](https://ref.ly/Ps89:5) (‖עד־עולם) [106:31](https://ref.ly/Ps106:31) (‖ *id.*) [Is 34:17](https://ref.ly/Isa34:17) (‖ *id.*); [Psalm 77:9](https://ref.ly/Ps77:9) (‖לנצח); עַד־דֹּר וָדֹר [Psalm 100:5](https://ref.ly/Ps100:5) (‖לעולם) [Is 13:20](https://ref.ly/Isa13:20) (‖לנצח) [Je 50:39](https://ref.ly/Jer50:39) (‖ *id.*); בְּכָל־דֹּר וָדֹר Psalm [45:18](https://ref.ly/Ps45:17) (‖לעולם ועד) cf. [Est 9:28](https://ref.ly/Esth9:28); שְׁנוֺתָיו כְּמוֺ דֹר וָדֹר [Psalm 61:7](https://ref.ly/Ps61:7) cf. שְׁנֵי דּוֺר וָדוֺר [Jo 2:2](https://ref.ly/Joel2:2) (‖העולם); as modifying phrase מְשוֺש דּוֺר וָדוֺר [Is 60:15](https://ref.ly/Isa60:15) (‖נאון עולם); further דּוֺר דּוֺרִים [Psalm 72:5](https://ref.ly/Ps72:5); יְשׁוּעָתִי לְדוֺר דּוֺרִים [Is 51:8](https://ref.ly/Isa51:8) (‖לעולם). **c.** apparently including both past and future בְּדוֺר דּוֺרִים שְׁנוֺתֶיךָ [Psalm 102:25](https://ref.ly/Ps102:25); בְּכָל דּוֺר וָדֹר [Psalm 145:13](https://ref.ly/Ps145:13); לְדוֺר וָדוֺר [La 5:19](https://ref.ly/Lam5:19). **2\.** of men living at a particular time (period, age), *generation,* as transitory דּוֺר הֹלֵח וְדוֺר בָּא [Ec 1:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:4): specific. **a.** in the present, and (or) the past [Gn 7:1](https://ref.ly/Gen7:1) (J) [Ex 1:6](https://ref.ly/Exod1:6) (P) [Nu 32:13](https://ref.ly/Num32:13) (JE) [Dt 1:35](https://ref.ly/Deut1:35), [2:14](https://ref.ly/Deut2:14), Ju [2:10](https://ref.ly/Judg2:10), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Judg2:10), [Is 53:8](https://ref.ly/Isa53:8) (cf. Che crit. n.) [Je 2:31](https://ref.ly/Jer2:31); also [Psalm 95:10](https://ref.ly/Ps95:10), [Jb 8:8](https://ref.ly/Job8:8), [Is 41:4](https://ref.ly/Isa41:4). **b.** Ju [3:2](https://ref.ly/Judg3:2) (present \& future); cf. pl. דֹּרֹתָיו [Gn 6:9](https://ref.ly/Gen6:9) (i.e. his own gen. and those immediately contiguous, before and after). **c.** esp. of a future generation [Gn 15:16](https://ref.ly/Gen15:16) (JE), with numeral, cf. [Dt 23:3](https://ref.ly/Deut23:3), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Deut23:4), [23:9](https://ref.ly/Deut23:9); also [Dt 29:21](https://ref.ly/Deut29:21), [Psalm 48:14](https://ref.ly/Ps48:14), [71:18](https://ref.ly/Ps71:18), [78:4](https://ref.ly/Ps78:4), [78:6](https://ref.ly/Ps78:6), [102:19](https://ref.ly/Ps102:19), [109:13](https://ref.ly/Ps109:13), [Jo 1:3](https://ref.ly/Joel1:3) cf. [Psalm 22:31](https://ref.ly/Ps22:30), [22:32](https://ref.ly/Ps22:31) (Che crit. n.); of a succession of generations דּוֺר לְדוֺר יְשַבַּח מַעֲשֶיךָ [Psalm 145:4](https://ref.ly/Ps145:4); בְּכָל־דּוֺר וָדוֺר [Est 9:28](https://ref.ly/Esth9:28); usually pl. לְדֹרֹת עוֺלָם [Gn 9:12](https://ref.ly/Gen9:12) (P); with num. לְאֶלֶף דּוֺר [Psalm 105:8](https://ref.ly/Ps105:8) (‖עולם) \= [1 Ch 16:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:15) (‖לעולם); [Dt 7:9](https://ref.ly/Deut7:9); v. further, of posterity, אַרְבָּעָה דֹּרוֺת [Jb 42:16](https://ref.ly/Job42:16); usually pl. c. sf. [Gn 17:7](https://ref.ly/Gen17:7), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Gen17:9), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Gen17:12), [Ex 12:14](https://ref.ly/Exod12:14), [12:17](https://ref.ly/Exod12:17), [12:42](https://ref.ly/Exod12:42), [16:32](https://ref.ly/Exod16:32), [16:33](https://ref.ly/Exod16:33), [27:21](https://ref.ly/Exod27:21), [29:42](https://ref.ly/Exod29:42), [30:8](https://ref.ly/Exod30:8), [30:10](https://ref.ly/Exod30:10), [30:21](https://ref.ly/Exod30:21), [30:31](https://ref.ly/Exod30:31), [31:13](https://ref.ly/Exod31:13), [31:16](https://ref.ly/Exod31:16), [40:15](https://ref.ly/Exod40:15), [Lv 3:17](https://ref.ly/Lev3:17), [6:11](https://ref.ly/Lev6:11), [7:36](https://ref.ly/Lev7:36), [10:9](https://ref.ly/Lev10:9) (all P); [17:7](https://ref.ly/Lev17:7), [21:17](https://ref.ly/Lev21:17), [22:3](https://ref.ly/Lev22:3), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Lev23:14), [23:21](https://ref.ly/Lev23:21), [23:31](https://ref.ly/Lev23:31), [23:41](https://ref.ly/Lev23:41), [23:43](https://ref.ly/Lev23:43), [24:3](https://ref.ly/Lev24:3), [25:30](https://ref.ly/Lev25:30), (all H); [Nu 9:10](https://ref.ly/Num9:10), [10:8](https://ref.ly/Num10:8), [15:14](https://ref.ly/Num15:14), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Num15:15), [15:21](https://ref.ly/Num15:21), [15:23](https://ref.ly/Num15:23), [15:38](https://ref.ly/Num15:38), [18:23](https://ref.ly/Num18:23), [35:29](https://ref.ly/Num35:29), [Jos 22:27](https://ref.ly/Josh22:27), [22:28](https://ref.ly/Josh22:28) (all P). **3\.** generation characterized by quality or condition, *class* of men: דּוֺר עִקֵּשׁ *crooked generation* [Dt 32:5](https://ref.ly/Deut32:5) (song) cf. v:[20](https://ref.ly/Deut32:20), [Psalm 78:8](https://ref.ly/Ps78:8), [78:8](https://ref.ly/Ps78:8), [Je 7:29](https://ref.ly/Jer7:29); of diff. classes of wicked, [Pr 30:11](https://ref.ly/Prov30:11), [30:12](https://ref.ly/Prov30:12), [30:13](https://ref.ly/Prov30:13), [30:14](https://ref.ly/Prov30:14) cf. [Psalm 12:8](https://ref.ly/Ps12:8); of the righteous, as a class [Psalm 14:5](https://ref.ly/Ps14:5), [24:6](https://ref.ly/Ps24:6), [73:15](https://ref.ly/Ps73:15) (בָּנֶיךָ דּוֺר) [112:2](https://ref.ly/Ps112:2) (cf. [22:31](https://ref.ly/Ps22:30) MT, but \< Che joins לדור to v:[32](https://ref.ly/Ps22:31) cf. supr. sub **2 c**); so also דּוֺר אֲבוֺתָיו i.e. the dead [Psalm 49:20](https://ref.ly/Ps49:20) (so most; yet v. infr. sub **4**). **4\.** *dwelling\-place, habitation* [Is 38:12](https://ref.ly/Isa38:12) cf. דּוּר vb., so Saad Ki Ges De Che RVm (Ew *life* ‖חַיַּי); so also [Psalm 49:20](https://ref.ly/Ps49:20) De Witt, cf. CheOP 479\.

## דּוּשׁ (id: BDB02086)

\[Str1758,156] † דּוּשׁ, דּישׁ **vb.** tread, thresh (Ar. دَاسَ, As. *dâšu*, Impf. 1 S. *adîš* DlPr 191 COTGloss HomNS 391; cf. NH דּוּשׁ, Aram. *id.,* ܕܳܫ; v. further NöZMG 1883, 538\)—**Qal** *Pf.* דָּשׁ [1 Ch 21:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:20), וְדַשְׁתִּ֫י Ju [8:7](https://ref.ly/Judg8:7); *Impf.* וַיָּדָשׁ so rd. for וַיֹּדַע Ju [8:16](https://ref.ly/Judg8:16) cf. Be Door Bu Vrss; 2 ms. תָּדוּשׁ [Is 41:15](https://ref.ly/Isa41:15), Hb [3:12](https://ref.ly/Hab3:12); 3 ms. sf. יְדוּשֶָׁנּוּ [Is 28:28](https://ref.ly/Isa28:28); 3 fs. sf. תְּדוּשֶָׁהָ [Jb 39:15](https://ref.ly/Job39:15); *Imv.* fs. דּוֺשִׁי [Mi 4:13](https://ref.ly/Mic4:13); *Inf. cstr.* לָדֻשׁ [2 K 13:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:7), לָדוּשׁ [Ho 10:11](https://ref.ly/Hos10:11), sf. דּוּשָׁם [Am 1:13](https://ref.ly/Amos1:13); דִּישׁוֺ [Dt 25:4](https://ref.ly/Deut25:4); *abs.* אָדוֺשׁ [Is 28:28](https://ref.ly/Isa28:28) as if fr. אדשׁ cf. Köi. 444; yet ארשׁ nowhere else \& form very possibly textual error, cf. Ol:§ 245 k; *Pt.* f. דָּשָׁא [Je 50:11](https://ref.ly/Jer50:11); —*tread on, trample on,* c. acc. [Jb 39:15](https://ref.ly/Job39:15) (beast on eggs, ‖זוּר); *thresh,* lit. c. acc. חִטִּים [1 Ch 21:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:20), [Je 50:11](https://ref.ly/Jer50:11), לֶחֶם (*i.e.* its material) [Is 28:28](https://ref.ly/Isa28:28) (in sim.); abs. [2 K 13:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:7) (in sim.); [Ho 10:11](https://ref.ly/Hos10:11) (metaph. of Ephraim as heifer); *tread* or *thresh* Ju [8:7](https://ref.ly/Judg8:7) *with* (אֶת \= *together with*, cf. Stu; Ew§ 234 e takes as acc., \& vb. as abbrev Hiph.) *thorns,* קוֺצִים and בַּרְקָנִים q.v.; and also in Ju [8:16](https://ref.ly/Judg8:16) (rd. וידשׁ cf. supr.) sq. בָּהֶם, *with them,* ref. to קוצים and ברקנים; fig. devastation of land [Am 1:3](https://ref.ly/Amos1:3) (Gilead), destruction of peoples Hb [3:12](https://ref.ly/Hab3:12) (גוים); espec. [Mi 4:13](https://ref.ly/Mic4:13) (abs.), where full metaph.; hyperbol. of Israel [Is 41:15](https://ref.ly/Isa41:15) c. acc. הרים.—**Niph.** *be trampled down,* Pf. וְנָדוֺשׁ consec. [Is. 25:10](https://ref.ly/Isa25:10) fig., subj. Moab; *Inf.* הִדּוּשׁ *ib.* (simile, subj. מתבן *straw\-heap;* on form cf. Ol:§ 193 a BaNB 156\)—**Hoph.** *be threshed, Impf.* יוּדַשׁ [Is 28:27](https://ref.ly/Isa28:27) (subj. קֶצַח).

> \[Str1786] † דַּ֫יִשׁ **n.m.** threshing, i.e. the process of threshing, [Lv 26:5](https://ref.ly/Lev26:5) (H) אֶת־בָּצִיר ´וְהִשִּיג לָכֶם ד.

> \[Str4098] † \[מְדֻשַָׁה] **n.f.** that which is threshed; sf. מְדֻשַָׁתִ [Is 21:10](https://ref.ly/Isa21:10) (‖בֶּן־גָּרְנִי) fig. in address to Israel by prophet.

> \[Str1788] †I. דִּישֹׁן **n.\[m.]** a clean animal, LXX πύγαργος cf. Vulgate; hence AV RV \& most **pygarg**, a kind of antelope or gazelle, cf. Di [Lv 11:2f](https://ref.ly/Lev11:2).; perh. rather mountain\-goat, HomNS 391 cf. Eth.Vrs.; only [Dt 14:5](https://ref.ly/Deut14:5)—(Homl. c. der. fr. √ דושׁ with kindred meaning of *spring, leap* \& comp. As. *daššu*; so already DlS i. 54\).

> \[Str1787] †II. דִּישֹׁן **n.pr.m. 1\.** a son of Seir דִּשׁוֺן [Gn 36:21](https://ref.ly/Gen36:21) \= דִּישֹׁן [1 Ch 1:38](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:38); **2\.** a son of Anah \& grandson of Seir דִּשָֹׁן [Gn 36:25](https://ref.ly/Gen36:25) cf. v:[30](https://ref.ly/Gen36:30) so also v:[26](https://ref.ly/Gen36:26) (for דִּישָׁן q.v.) \= דִּישׁוֺן [1 Ch 1:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:41), [1:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:41), [1:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:42), cf. also foll.

> \[Str1789] † דִּישָׁן **n.pr.m.** a son of Seir דִּישָׁן [Gn 36:21](https://ref.ly/Gen36:21) (LXX Ῥισών; so v:[28](https://ref.ly/Gen36:28), v:[30](https://ref.ly/Gen36:30) cf. Di) [1 Ch 1:38](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:38); (v:[42](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:42) rd. דִּישׁוֺן q.v.) [Gn 36:26](https://ref.ly/Gen36:26) (rd. דִּישׁוֺן LXX SyrVer Vulgate Ol Di) v:[30](https://ref.ly/Gen36:30) דִּישָׁ֭ן [Gn 36:28](https://ref.ly/Gen36:28).

## דָּחָה (id: BDB02092)

\[Str1760] דָּחָה **vb.** push, thrust (NH *id.,* Aram. דְּחִי, דְּחָא, ܕܚܳܐ; cf also Ar. دَحَا, *spread, extend,* also *throw, propel*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. sf. דְּחִיתַנִי [Psalm 118:13](https://ref.ly/Ps118:13); *Inf. cstr.* לִרְחוֺת [Psalm 140:5](https://ref.ly/Ps140:5); *abs.* דָּחֹה [Psalm 118:13](https://ref.ly/Ps118:13); *Pt. act.* דֹּחֶה [Psalm 35:5](https://ref.ly/Ps35:5) (rd. דֹּחָם LXX De Che); *pass.* f. הַדְּחוּיָה [Psalm 62:4](https://ref.ly/Ps62:4); —*push,* דָּחֹה רָחָה *push* *violently* fig. c. acc. [Psalm 118:13](https://ref.ly/Ps118:13) sq. cl. of purpose לִנְפֹּל, cf. לִדְחוֺת פעמי [Psalm 140:5](https://ref.ly/Ps140:5) (Che *trip up my feet*), \& [Psalm 35:5](https://ref.ly/Ps35:5) (transp. דחה with רדפם v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ps35:6) and rd. דֹּחָם LXX; cf. יְהִי־דרכם חשׁך וַחֲלַקְלַקֹּת va, and [Je 23:12](https://ref.ly/Jer23:12)); *pushed in* (Che), in sim., pt. pass., said of גָּדֵר *wall* [Psalm 62:4](https://ref.ly/Ps62:4) (‖קיר נטוי). **Niph.** *Impf.* יִדָּחֶה [Pr 14:32](https://ref.ly/Prov14:32); (3 mpl. יִדַּ֫חוּ [Je 23:12](https://ref.ly/Jer23:12) is fr. דחח, if rightly pointed; נִדְחֵי [Is 11:12](https://ref.ly/Isa11:12), [56:8](https://ref.ly/Isa56:8), [Psalm 147:2](https://ref.ly/Ps147:2), v. sub נדח);—*be thrust* or *cast down*, fig. of wicked [Pr 14:32](https://ref.ly/Prov14:32), cf. [Psalm 35:6](https://ref.ly/Ps35:6) supr. **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 *pl.* דֹּחוּ [Psalm 36:13](https://ref.ly/Ps36:13) *they are thrust down* (‖נפלו \& לא יכלו קום).

> \[Str1762] † \[דְּחִי] **n. \[m.]** stumbling, רַגְלַי…הִצַּלְתָּ מִדֶּחִָי [Psalm 56:14](https://ref.ly/Ps56:14) \= [116:8](https://ref.ly/Ps116:8).

> \[Str4072] † מִדְחֶה **n.m.** means or occasion of stumbling, [Pr 26:28](https://ref.ly/Prov26:28) ´פֶּה־חָלָק יַעֲשֶה מ (‖לְשׁוֺן־שֶׁקֶר יִשְנָא דַכָּו).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:4; Isaiah 56:8; Psalms 36:13; Psalms 116:8; Psalms 140:5; Isaiah 11:12; Psalms 35:5; Psalms 56:14; Proverbs 26:28; Proverbs 14:32; Psalms 147:2; Jeremiah 23:12; Psalms 118:13; Psalms 35:6


## דָּחַח (id: BDB02095)

\[Str1760] \[דָּחַח] **vb.** only **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִדַּ֫חוּ (Köi. 377\) [Je 23:12](https://ref.ly/Jer23:12) *they shall be thrust down*, fig., of wicked; rd. perh. יִתדָּח֫וּ, fr. דחה q.v.

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 23:12


## דחן (id: BDB02096)

דחן (√ of following; perh. cf. Ar. دَخَنَ *smoke arose*, hence *become dusky, dingy, inclining to black*).

> \[Str1764] † דֹּ֫חַן **n.m.** millet (NH דּוֺחַן, Aram. דּוֺחִינָא ´ד), Ez [4:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:9) in the series חִטִּין וּשְעֹרִים וּפוֺל וַעֲדָשִׁים וְכֻסְּמִים ´וְד v. Löw72.

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 4:9


## דָּחַף (id: BDB02098)

\[Str1765] † \[דָּחַף] **vb.** drive, hasten (late) (NH *id.*, Aram. דְּחַף)—**Qal** *Pt. pass.* pl. דְּחוּפִים of runners [Est 3:15](https://ref.ly/Esth3:15), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Esth8:14) (‖מְבֹהָלִים). **Niph.** *Pf. hasten one's self, hurry* נִדְחַף אֶ־ל [Est 6:12](https://ref.ly/Esth6:12); נִדְחַף לָצֵאת [2 Ch 26:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:20) (‖בהל).

> \[Str4073] † \[מַדְחֵפָה] **n.f.** thrust, [Psalm 140:12](https://ref.ly/Ps140:12) יְצוּדֶנּוּ לְמַדְחֵפֹת (*evil*) *shall hunt him with thrust upon thrust* (Che, cf. De).

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 26:20; Psalms 140:12; Esther 8:14; Esther 3:15; Esther 6:12


## דָּחַק (id: BDB02100)

\[Str1766] † \[דָּחַק] **vb.** thrust, crowd, oppress (NH *id.*, Ar. دَحَقَ *drive away, remove*, Aram. דְּחַק, ܕܚܰܩ)—**Qal** *Impf.* יִדְחָק֔וּן *thrust, crowd*, of locusts in swarm [Jo 2:8](https://ref.ly/Joel2:8); *Pt. act.* pl. sf. לֽחֲצֵיהֶם וְדֹחֲקֵיהֶם Ju [2:18](https://ref.ly/Judg2:18) (because of) *them that maltreated and oppressed them.*

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 2:18; Joel 2:8


## דַּי (id: BDB02101)

\[Str1767] † דַּי **subst.** sufficiency, enough (NH, but not known in other cogn. languages)—cstr. דֵּי, with sf. (v. infr.) דֶַּ\+יּךָּ, דַּיָּם:—**1\.** absol. thrice only [Mal 3:10](https://ref.ly/Mal3:10) I will pour you out a blessing עַד־בְּלִי־דָֽי until there is not *sufficiency*, i.e. until my abundance can be exhausted, or, as this can never be, for ever (cf. [Psalm 72:7](https://ref.ly/Ps72:7)), [Est 1:18](https://ref.ly/Esth1:18) וּכְדַי בִּזָּיוֺן וָקָצֶף and (there will be) *as enough* (i.e. in plenty) contempt and wrath, [2 Ch 30:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:3) לְמַדַּי \= לְמַה־דַּי for what was *sufficient.* With a gen. of the person or thing *for* which anything suffices: [Ex 36:5](https://ref.ly/Exod36:5) מִדֵּי הָעֲבֹדָה (more) than *enough for* the work, [Lv 5:7](https://ref.ly/Lev5:7) and if his hand do not reach (if he do not command) דֵּי שֶה *enough for* (i.e. to buy) a lamb, [12:8](https://ref.ly/Lev12:8), [25:28](https://ref.ly/Lev25:28) דֵּי הָשִׁיב לוֺ *enough for* recovering it, [Dt 15:8](https://ref.ly/Deut15:8) דֵּי מַחְסֹרוֺ *enough for* his need, [Is 40:16](https://ref.ly/Isa40:16), [40:16](https://ref.ly/Isa40:16). With suff. [Pr 25:16](https://ref.ly/Prov25:16) אֱכֹל דֶַּ\+יּךָּ eat *that which is sufficient for* thee; דַּיָּם [Ex 36:7](https://ref.ly/Exod36:7), [Je 49:9](https://ref.ly/Jer49:9), Ob v:[5](https://ref.ly/Obad1:5). Once with gen. of the thing which is sufficient, [Pr 27:27](https://ref.ly/Prov27:27) דֵּי חֲלֵב עִזִּים *enough of* goats' milk.

 **2\.** Combined with בְּ, כְּ, and esp. מִן, דַּי (דֵּי) has a tendency to form compound prepositions, used idiomatically in certain applications:—**a.** בְּדֵי (α) *for* (the בְּ *pretii*) *what suffices for:* [Na 2:13](https://ref.ly/Nah2:13) the lion tare in pieces בְּדֵי גֹרוֺתָיו *for the need of* his whelps (‖לְלִבְאֹתָיו; but ? כְּדֵי, v. **b**); iron. Hb [2:13](https://ref.ly/Hab2:13), [Je 51:58](https://ref.ly/Jer51:58) the peoples labour בְּדֵי־אֵשׁ *for* fire (only to satisfy the fire), and the nations weary themselves בְּדֵי־רִיק *for* what is empty. (β) *in the abundance of*, i.e. *as often* as [Job 39:25](https://ref.ly/Job39:25) בְּדֵי שׁוֺפָר *in the abundance of* the trumpet, i.e. as often as the trumpet sounds (מִדֵּי elsewhere in this sense). **b.** כְּדֵי *according to the sufficiency*, or *abundance, of* [Lv 25:26](https://ref.ly/Lev25:26) and find כְּדֵי גְאֻלָּתוֺ *acc. to the sufficiency of* his redemption, i.e. *as much as* it demands, [Dt 25:2](https://ref.ly/Deut25:2), [Ne 5:8](https://ref.ly/Neh5:8) כְּדֵי בָנוּ (*st. c.* before בְּ: Ges§ 130, 1\) ‘quantum in nobis erat,’ after our ability, Ju [6:5](https://ref.ly/Judg6:5) they came כְּדֵי אַרְבֶּה לָרֹב *acc. to the abundance of* the locust in multitude (for which כָּאַרְבֶּה לָרֹב would ordinarily be said: cf. [7:12](https://ref.ly/Judg7:12)). **c.** מִדֵּי *out of the abundance of*, hence *as often as;*—(α) sq. inf. [1 S 1:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:7) מִדֵּי עֲלֽתָהּ \= *as often as* she went up, [18:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:30), [1 K 14:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:28) (\= [2 Ch 12:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:11)) [2 K 4:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:8), [Is 28:19](https://ref.ly/Isa28:19) מִדֵּי עָבְרוֺ *as often as* it passeth over, [Je 31:20](https://ref.ly/Jer31:20); (β) sq. subst., [Je 48:27](https://ref.ly/Jer48:27) מִדֵּי דְבָרֶיךָ בּוֺ *as often as* thy words (are) of him; and in the idiom. phrases מִדֵּי שָׁנָה בְּשָׁנָה \= *yearly* (a combination of מִדֵּי שָׁנָה and שָׁנָה בְּשָׁנָה: v. sub שָׁנָה) [1 S 7:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:16) (v. Dr) [Zc 14:16](https://ref.ly/Zech14:16), [2 Ch 24:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:5); and מִדֵּי חֹדֶשׁ בְּחָדְשׁוֺ [Is 66:23](https://ref.ly/Isa66:23) *as often as* month (comes) in its month (i.e. in its own time: חֹדֶשׁ made more precise by the add. of בְּחָדְשׁוֺ; cf. the phrase דְּבַר יוֺם בְּיוֺמוֺ): so מִדֵּי שַׁבָּת בְּשַׁבַּתּוֺ *ib.;* (γ) as conj., with the finite verb (אֲשֶׁר being understood: cf. בַּעֲבוּר etc.), [Je 20:8](https://ref.ly/Jer20:8) מִדֵּי אֲדַבֵּר *as often as* I speak. 

> \[Str1774] † דִּי זָהָב **n.pr.loc.** appar. on border of Moab [Dt 1:1](https://ref.ly/Deut1:1) (LXX Καταχρύσεα).

> \[Str1769,1775,1775,1776] † דִּיבֹן, דִּיבוֺן **n.pr.loc.**—**1\.** city in Moab (MI21, MI28 דיבן, cf. ib.:1 adj. gent. הדיבני)—דִּיבֹן [Nu 21:30](https://ref.ly/Num21:30), [32:3](https://ref.ly/Num32:3), [32:34](https://ref.ly/Num32:34) (built up or at by Gad) [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2); in territory of Gad, hence דִּיבֹן גָּדָ [Nu 33:45](https://ref.ly/Num33:45), [33:46](https://ref.ly/Num33:46) (cf. on these vv. \& [32:34](https://ref.ly/Num32:34) MI10 f.); also דִּיבוֺן [Jos 13:9](https://ref.ly/Josh13:9), [13:17](https://ref.ly/Josh13:17), [Je 48:22](https://ref.ly/Jer48:22); ישֶׁבֶת בַּת דִּיבוֺן [Je 48:18](https://ref.ly/Jer48:18); \= דִּימוֺן [Is 15:9](https://ref.ly/Isa15:9), vid. also מֵי דִימוֺן v:[9](https://ref.ly/Isa15:9) \= Arnon, cf. Che \& HptZA 1887, 268;—mod. *Dîbân*, north of Arnon, cf. SeetzenReisen i. 409 TristrLand of Moab 132 ff. BDPal 193\.
> 
> **2\.** דִּיבֹן [Ne 11:25](https://ref.ly/Neh11:25) place in Judah, toward south \= דִּימוֺנָה [Jos 15:22](https://ref.ly/Josh15:22), cf. HptL.c.; conject. by Kn Ke (cf. Di) to be *Tell ed Dheib* (van de VeldeMem. 252\) called also Ehdeib (RobBR ii. 102\).

> \[Str1770–71] דִּיג, דַּיָּג v. sub דגה. p. 185f

> \[Str1772] דַּיָּה v. sub דאה. p. 178

> \[Str1773] דְּיוֺ v. sub דוה. p. 188

> \[Str1775] † דִּימוֺן **n.pr.loc.** \= דִּיבוֺן **1,** q.v. above

> \[Str1776] † דִּימוֺנָה **n.pr.loc.** \= דִּיבוֺן **2,** q.v. above

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 20:8; Psalms 72:7; Nehemiah 11:25; Joshua 13:17; Isaiah 15:2; Jeremiah 48:18; 2 Kings 4:8; Numbers 32:3; 1 Samuel 7:16; Numbers 33:46; 2 Chronicles 12:11; Leviticus 25:28; Habakkuk 2:13; Proverbs 25:16; Isaiah 15:9; Job 39:25; Jeremiah 31:20; Deuteronomy 1:1; Numbers 21:30; Zechariah 14:16; Numbers 33:45; Jeremiah 48:22; Malachi 3:10; 2 Chronicles 24:5; Isaiah 28:19; Judges 6:5; Nahum 2:13; Joshua 15:22; Jeremiah 48:27; Nehemiah 5:8; Isaiah 40:16; Judges 7:12; 2 Chronicles 30:3; Deuteronomy 25:2; Proverbs 27:27; Leviticus 5:7; 1 Samuel 18:30; Jeremiah 49:9; Deuteronomy 15:8; Jeremiah 51:58; Leviticus 12:8; Isaiah 66:23; Leviticus 25:26; Joshua 13:9; Numbers 32:34; 1 Samuel 1:7; 1 Kings 14:28; Exodus 36:5; Exodus 36:7; Esther 1:18


## דִּין (id: BDB02109)

\[Str1777] † דִּין **vb.** judge (cf. Ar. دَانَ intrans. *to be obedient, submissive*, trans. *requite, compensate, rule, govern*, دِينٌ *obedience, abasement, recompense*, دَيَّانٌ *requiter, governor*, مَدِينَةٌ *city;* As. *dânu*, Impf. *idin, judge* AsrbAnn. x. 120 DlHA 49; Aram. דִּין, ܢܕܽܘܢ *judge, exercise judgment, punish*, דַּיָּינָא, ܕܰܝܳܢܳܐ, *judge*, דִּינָא, ܕܝܻܢ *judgment*, ܡܕܝܺܢܻܬܳܐ *city*;—on √ י“ע v. NöZMG 1883, 533\), syn. שָׁפַט—**Qal** *Pf.* דָּן [Je 22:16](https://ref.ly/Jer22:16) etc.; *Impf.* יָדִין [Gn 49:16](https://ref.ly/Gen49:16) \+ 9 t., יָדוֺן [Gn 6:3](https://ref.ly/Gen6:3) cf. infr., etc.; *Imv.* דִּין [Pr 31:9](https://ref.ly/Prov31:9), [Jb 35:14](https://ref.ly/Job35:14); דִּינוּ [Je 21:12](https://ref.ly/Jer21:12); *Inf.* לָדִין [Psalm 50:4](https://ref.ly/Ps50:4) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* דָּן [Gn 15:14](https://ref.ly/Gen15:14), [Je 30:13](https://ref.ly/Jer30:13); —**1\.** *act as judge, minister judgment*, of God ידין עמוֺ [Dt 32:36](https://ref.ly/Deut32:36) (poet. \= [Psalm 135:14](https://ref.ly/Ps135:14)) [Psalm 50:4](https://ref.ly/Ps50:4); דין עמים [Psalm 7:9](https://ref.ly/Ps7:9), [96:10](https://ref.ly/Ps96:10), [Is 3:13](https://ref.ly/Isa3:13); יָדִין לְאֻמִּים בְּמֵישָׁרִים [Psalm 9:9](https://ref.ly/Ps9:9); Messianic king [Psalm 72:2](https://ref.ly/Ps72:2). **2\.** *plead the cause*, **a.** of men, usu. c. acc. cogn., אֵין־דָּן דִּינֵךְ

 *there is none to plead thy cause* [Je 30:13](https://ref.ly/Jer30:13); דִּין יָתוֺם ´ד *plead cause of orphan* [Je 5:28](https://ref.ly/Jer5:28); דין עני ואביון ´ד [Je 22:16](https://ref.ly/Jer22:16); also, acc. cogn. om., [Pr 31:9](https://ref.ly/Prov31:9). **b.** of God, דָּנַנִּי אֱלֽהִים *God has pled my cause* [Gn 30:6](https://ref.ly/Gen30:6) (E). **3\.** *execute judgment, vindicate*, in battle against enemies, דָּן יָדִין עַמּוֺ *Dan will judge* (vindicate as a warrior) *his people* (v. Di) [Gn 49:16](https://ref.ly/Gen49:16) (poet.); the Messianic king בגוים [Psalm 110:6](https://ref.ly/Ps110:6); God, בִּגְבוּרָֽתְךָ תְדִינֵנִי *in thy might judge me* (vindicate me) [Psalm 54:3](https://ref.ly/Ps54:3). **4\.** *execute judgment, requite*, of man, דִּינוּ לַבֹּקֶר מִשְׁפָּט *execute judgment every morning* [Je 21:12](https://ref.ly/Jer21:12); of God, upon Egypt [Gn 15:14](https://ref.ly/Gen15:14) (E); עמים by means of powers of nature [Jb 36:31](https://ref.ly/Job36:31); אפסי ארץ [1 S 2:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:10) (poet.) **5\.** *govern*, אַתָּה תָּדִין אֶת־בֵּיתִי *thou shalt govern my house* [Zc 3:7](https://ref.ly/Zech3:7). **6\.** לאֹ־יוּכַל לָדִין עִם *he cannot contend with* one mightier than he [Ec 6:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:10). **Niph.** *Pt.* וַיְהִי כָל־הָעָם נָדוֺן בְּכָל־שִׁבְטֵי יִשְרָאֵל *and all the people were at strife throughout all the tribes of Israel* [2 S 19:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:10) (reciprocal, cf. Dr.) *Note.*—לאֹ־יָדוֺן רוּחִי בָֽאָרָם לְעֹלָם [Gn 6:3](https://ref.ly/Gen6:3) is difficult. (1\) LXX Vulgate SyrVer Onk rd. ידיר or (Kue) ילון *abide in, dwell*,—*My spirit will not abide in man for ever;* this best suits the context, but ידור, as Aramaism, is dub. (2\) Kn De Schr RVm render *rule in*, supported by [Zc 3:7](https://ref.ly/Zech3:7) only. (3\) Thes Ew Di render *be humbled in*, sustained by Ar. usage, but not by Heb. (4\) *strive with* of AV RV (cf. **6** supr.) is hardly justified. 

> \[Str1779] † דִּין **n.\[m.]** judgment—[Dt 17:8](https://ref.ly/Deut17:8) \+ 16 t.; sf. דִּינִי [Psalm 9:5](https://ref.ly/Ps9:5), דִּינֵךְ [Je 30:13](https://ref.ly/Jer30:13); —**1\.** כסא דין *throne of judgment* [Pr 20:8](https://ref.ly/Prov20:8); משׁמים השׁמעת דין *from heaven thou didst cause judgment to be heard* [Psalm 76:9](https://ref.ly/Ps76:9). **2\.** *cause, plea*, בין דין לדין *between plea and plea* [Dt 17:8](https://ref.ly/Deut17:8); דין דין *plead a cause* [Je 5:28](https://ref.ly/Jer5:28), [22:16](https://ref.ly/Jer22:16), [30:13](https://ref.ly/Jer30:13); עשה דין *maintain, the cause* [Psalm 9:5](https://ref.ly/Ps9:5), [140:13](https://ref.ly/Ps140:13); ידע דין *consider the cause* [Pr 29:7](https://ref.ly/Prov29:7); דין לפניו *the cause is before him* [Jb 35:14](https://ref.ly/Job35:14); שׁנה דין *change the cause* [Pr 31:5](https://ref.ly/Prov31:5); לְהַטּוֺת מִדִּין דַּלִּים [Is 10:2](https://ref.ly/Isa10:2); דין יתום [Je 5:28](https://ref.ly/Jer5:28); דין כל בני חֲלוֺף [Pr 31:8](https://ref.ly/Prov31:8). **3\.** *judgment, condemnation*, דין רשׁע *judgment of the wicked* [Jb 36:17](https://ref.ly/Job36:17); דין ומשׁפט *judgment and justice* v:[17](https://ref.ly/Job36:17). **4\.** *strife*, דין רקלון [Pr 22:10](https://ref.ly/Prov22:10) (*legal strife, law\-suit* Str). **5\.** *government*, דת ודין *law and government* [Est 1:13](https://ref.ly/Esth1:13).
> 
>  *Note.*—לְמַעַן תֵּדְעוּן שַׁדּיּ֯ן [Jb 19:29](https://ref.ly/Job19:29) is variously explained. (1\) AV RV *that ye may know there is a judgment* follow Aq Symm Theod, so De Da, in interpreting שׁ relative \+ דִּין *judgment*, but שׁ is unknown to the dialect of Job and דִּין is used only in Elihu section. (2\) LXX rds. ὕλη, or ἴσχυς (i.e. שַׁדַּי (cf. [29:5](https://ref.ly/Job29:5) LXX) ? or שֹׁד?), \& Qr שַׁדּוּן, indicating ancient uncertainty and a corrupt text. (3\) Ew Di rd. שַׁדַּי *the Almighty*, which accords with usage of Job. (4\) Siegf כִּי שֻׁדָּדוּ (doubtfully).

> \[Str1783] † דִּינָה **n.pr.f.** daughter of Jacob [Gn 30:21](https://ref.ly/Gen30:21), [34:1](https://ref.ly/Gen34:1), [34:3](https://ref.ly/Gen34:3), [34:5](https://ref.ly/Gen34:5), [34:13](https://ref.ly/Gen34:13), [34:25](https://ref.ly/Gen34:25), [34:26](https://ref.ly/Gen34:26), [46:15](https://ref.ly/Gen46:15).

> \[Str1835,1842] † דָּן **n.pr.m.** (*judge*)—**1\.** son of Jacob and Bilhah [Gn 30:6](https://ref.ly/Gen30:6), [35:25](https://ref.ly/Gen35:25), [Ex 1:4](https://ref.ly/Exod1:4), [Jos 19:47](https://ref.ly/Josh19:47), Ju [18:29](https://ref.ly/Judg18:29), [1 Ch 2:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:2). **2\.** the tribe of Dan, מַטֵּה דָן [Ex 31:6](https://ref.ly/Exod31:6), [35:34](https://ref.ly/Exod35:34), [38:23](https://ref.ly/Exod38:23), [Lv 24:11](https://ref.ly/Lev24:11), [Nu 1:39](https://ref.ly/Num1:39), [13:12](https://ref.ly/Num13:12), [Jos 21:5](https://ref.ly/Josh21:5), [21:23](https://ref.ly/Josh21:23) (all P); בְּנֵי דָן [Gn 46:23](https://ref.ly/Gen46:23), [Nu 1:38](https://ref.ly/Num1:38), [2:25](https://ref.ly/Num2:25), [7:66](https://ref.ly/Num7:66), [10:25](https://ref.ly/Num10:25), [26:42](https://ref.ly/Num26:42), [34:22](https://ref.ly/Num34:22), [Jos 19:40](https://ref.ly/Josh19:40), [19:47](https://ref.ly/Josh19:47), [19:47](https://ref.ly/Josh19:47), [19:48](https://ref.ly/Josh19:48) (all P) Ju [1:34](https://ref.ly/Judg1:34), [18:2](https://ref.ly/Judg18:2), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Judg18:16), [18:22](https://ref.ly/Judg18:22), [18:23](https://ref.ly/Judg18:23), [18:25](https://ref.ly/Judg18:25), [18:26](https://ref.ly/Judg18:26), [18:30](https://ref.ly/Judg18:30); מַחֲנֵה דָן [Nu 2:25](https://ref.ly/Num2:25), [2:31](https://ref.ly/Num2:31) (P)—on Ju [13:25](https://ref.ly/Judg13:25), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Judg18:12) v. מַחֲנֶה; מִשְׁפְּחֹת דָּן [Nu 26:42](https://ref.ly/Num26:42) (P); דָּן alone in poetry [Gn 49:16](https://ref.ly/Gen49:16), [49:17](https://ref.ly/Gen49:17), [Dt 33:22](https://ref.ly/Deut33:22), [33:22](https://ref.ly/Deut33:22), Ju [5:17](https://ref.ly/Judg5:17), elsewhere [Nu 1:12](https://ref.ly/Num1:12) (P) [Dt 27:13](https://ref.ly/Deut27:13), [1 Ch 27:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:22), [2 Ch 2:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:13), Ez [48:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:1), [48:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:2), [48:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:32). **3\.** a city on northern frontier of Israel at one of the sources of the Jordan, originally לַיִשׁ Ju [18:29](https://ref.ly/Judg18:29) (cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Judg18:7)); \= לֶשֶׁם [Jos 19:47](https://ref.ly/Josh19:47); captured by a colony of Danites; named Dan, and made a sacred place with rites of worship that lasted until the exile (Ju [18:30](https://ref.ly/Judg18:30)); \= *Tell el Kâdî*, RobBR iii. 351, 358 BDPa1 265\. This place is frequently referred to: [Gn 14:14](https://ref.ly/Gen14:14), [Dt 34:1](https://ref.ly/Deut34:1), [1 K 12:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:29), [12:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:30), [15:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:20), [2 K 10:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:29), [2 Ch 16:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:4), [Je 4:15](https://ref.ly/Jer4:15), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Jer8:16), [Am 8:14](https://ref.ly/Amos8:14); rd. also וּבְדָן for וְכֵן [2 S 20:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:18) LXX Ew We cf. Dr; and v. the phrase מִדָּן וְעַד־בְּאֵר שֶׁבַע Ju [20:1](https://ref.ly/Judg20:1), [1 S 3:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:20), [2 S 3:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:10), [17:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:11), [24:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:2), [24:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:15), [1 K 5:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:5); given by Chr מִבְּאֵר שֶׁבַע וְעַד־דָּן [1 Ch 21:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:2), [2 Ch 30:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:5). (Ez [27:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:19) v. ודן.)

> \[Str1839] † דָּנִי **adj. gent.** alw. c. art. as n.pr. coll. הַדָּנִי [1 Ch 12:35](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:35); שֵׁבֶט הַדָּנִי Ju [18:1](https://ref.ly/Judg18:1), [18:30](https://ref.ly/Judg18:30); מִשְׁפַּחַת הַדָּנִי Ju [13:2](https://ref.ly/Judg13:2), [18:11](https://ref.ly/Judg18:11).

> \[Str1781] † דַּיָּן **n.m.** judge [1 S 24:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:16); cstr. דַּיַּן [Psalm 68:6](https://ref.ly/Ps68:6).

> \[Str1840] דָּנִאֵל, later דָּנִיֵּאל **n.pr.m.** (cf. Ew§ 45 d; *Ēl is my judge;* on a possible connexion with Zend *dânu, wise*, or *wisdom* cf. CheOP 107; v. Palm. דנאל Vog93)—**1\.** son of David [1 Ch 3:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:1), דָּנִיֵּאל, but dub, cf. CheOP 106; LXX Δαμνιηλ, LXXL \& Codd. Δαλουια; ‖ [2 S 3:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:3) כִּלְאָב, LXX Δαλουια; KloSm prop. דֹּדִיָּה, \& in Ch דֹּדִיאֵל, but grounds precarious. **2\.** דָּנִיֵּאל, priest of the line of Ithamar [Ezr 8:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:2), [Ne 10:7](https://ref.ly/Neh10:7). **3\.** דָּנִאֵל a great sage Ez [28:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:3); classed with Noah and Job as models of righteousness Ez [14:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:14), [14:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:20); perh. the same as **4\.** דָּנִיֵּאל, one of the noble young men taken into captivity by Nebuchadrezzar acc. to [Dn 1:1](https://ref.ly/Dan1:1)–[6](https://ref.ly/Dan1:6), the hero of the book of Daniel, [Dn 1:6](https://ref.ly/Dan1:6) \+ 22 t. (also frequently in Aramaic section).

> \[Str4066,4079,4090,4067,4060] † I. מָדוֺן **n.m.**:[Pr 22:10](https://ref.ly/Prov22:10) strife, contention—[Psalm 80:7](https://ref.ly/Ps80:7) \+ 9 t.; pl. מִדְיָנִים [Pr 18:18](https://ref.ly/Prov18:18), מִדְ֯וָנִים [Pr 18:19](https://ref.ly/Prov18:19) \+ 7 t.; contracted into מְדָנִים [Pr 6:19](https://ref.ly/Prov6:19), [10:12](https://ref.ly/Prov10:12), מִדְ֯נִים [Pr 6:14](https://ref.ly/Prov6:14); cstr. מִדְיְנֵי [Pr 19:13](https://ref.ly/Prov19:13). **1\.** *strife, contention, alm. wholly* Pr:—[Pr 18:19](https://ref.ly/Prov18:19), [23:29](https://ref.ly/Prov23:29), [26:20](https://ref.ly/Prov26:20); also [17:14](https://ref.ly/Prov17:14) (‖רִיב), [22:10](https://ref.ly/Prov22:10) (‖דִּין וְקָלוֺן); יִשָּא ´מ Hb [1:3](https://ref.ly/Hab1:3) *contention ariseth* (‖ריב); ´שׁלּח מ *let loose strife* [Pr 6:14](https://ref.ly/Prov6:14), [16:28](https://ref.ly/Prov16:28); ´יְגָרֶה מ *he stirreth up strife* [15:18](https://ref.ly/Prov15:18), [28:25](https://ref.ly/Prov28:25), [29:22](https://ref.ly/Prov29:22); note esp. אִישׁ מִדְוָ֯נִים [26:21](https://ref.ly/Prov26:21) \= *a contentious, quarrelsome man;* oftener ´אֵשֶׁת מ \= *a contentious woman* [21:9](https://ref.ly/Prov21:9), [25:24](https://ref.ly/Prov25:24), [27:15](https://ref.ly/Prov27:15), cf. וָכָעַם ´אשׁת מ [21:19](https://ref.ly/Prov21:19). **2\.** *object of contention* [Psalm 80:7](https://ref.ly/Ps80:7), cf. אִישׁ מָדוֺן [Je 15:10](https://ref.ly/Jer15:10) (‖אִישׁ רִיב).—On מָדוֺן [2 S 21:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:20) Qr, v. I. מִדָּה sub מדד. p. 551

> \[Str4068] † II. מָדוֺן **n.pr.loc.** a royal city of the Canaanites [Jos 11:1](https://ref.ly/Josh11:1), [12:19](https://ref.ly/Josh12:19); LXX Μαρρων, Μαδων, Λαμορων; \= *Madîn* close to *Hattîn*, Surveyi. 365, dub.; a village *Maron* lies 2 hours WSW. fr. Kedesh, van de VeldeMem. 146, cf. Di.

> \[Str4091] † מְדָן **n.pr.m.** son of Abraham and Keturah [Gn 25:2](https://ref.ly/Gen25:2), [1 Ch 1:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:32).

> \[Str4080] מִדְיָן **n.pr.m. 1\.** son of Abraham and Keturah [Gn 25:2](https://ref.ly/Gen25:2), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Gen25:4) (J) [1 Ch 1:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:32), [1:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:33). **2\.** an Arabian tribe [Gn 36:35](https://ref.ly/Gen36:35) (\= [1 Ch 1:46](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:46)) [Nu 31:3](https://ref.ly/Num31:3), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Num31:3), [31:7](https://ref.ly/Num31:7), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Num31:8), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Num31:8), [31:9](https://ref.ly/Num31:9) (P) Ju 6–9 (31 t.); having a כֹּהֵן (as chieftain?) [Ex 2:16](https://ref.ly/Exod2:16), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Exod3:1), [18:1](https://ref.ly/Exod18:1) (E); זקנים [Nu 22:4](https://ref.ly/Num22:4); [22:7](https://ref.ly/Num22:7) (JE), (ים)נשיא [Nu 25:18](https://ref.ly/Num25:18) (P) [Jos 13:21](https://ref.ly/Josh13:21) (P), שרים Ju [7:25](https://ref.ly/Judg7:25), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Judg8:3), מלכים [Nu 31:8](https://ref.ly/Num31:8), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Num31:8), Ju [8:5](https://ref.ly/Judg8:5), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Judg8:12), [8:26](https://ref.ly/Judg8:26); בִּכְרֵי מִדְיָן *dromedaries of Midian* [Is 60:6](https://ref.ly/Isa60:6); יוֺם מִדְיָן *day of Midian* [Is 9:3](https://ref.ly/Isa9:3) (the victory over Midian Ju 7–8) cf. [10:26](https://ref.ly/Isa10:26), [Psalm 83:10](https://ref.ly/Ps83:10), **3\.** אֶרֶץ מִרְיָן *the land of Midian* [Ex 2:15](https://ref.ly/Exod2:15) (E) Hb [3:7](https://ref.ly/Hab3:7); מִדְיָן [Ex 4:19](https://ref.ly/Exod4:19) (J) [Nu 25:15](https://ref.ly/Num25:15) (P) [1 K 11:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:18); land on Ælanitic gulf (where Arab. geographers still place town مدين). Cf. on Midian, GlaserSkizze ii. 447 ff.; HomAufsätze i. (l892\), 4f.

> \[Str4084,4092] † מִדְיָנִי **adj. gent.** Midianite—[Nu 10:29](https://ref.ly/Num10:29) (J); f. מִדְיָנִית [Nu 25:6](https://ref.ly/Num25:6), [25:14](https://ref.ly/Num25:14), [25:15](https://ref.ly/Num25:15) (P); pl. מִדְיָנִים [Gn 37:28](https://ref.ly/Gen37:28) (JE) [Nu 25:17](https://ref.ly/Num25:17), [31:2](https://ref.ly/Num31:2) (P), מְדָנִים [Gn 37:36](https://ref.ly/Gen37:36) (E).

> \[Str4082] מְדִינָה **n.f.** province (an Aramaic word, cf. Syr. ܡܕܝܺܢܻܬܳܐ, Ar. مَدِينَةٌ *city*)—[Est 1:1](https://ref.ly/Esth1:1) \+ 28 t.; pl. מְדִינוֺת [1 K 20:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:14) \+ 23 t.;—a district of an empire, **1\.** districts of realm of Ahab with שרים over them [1 K 20:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:14), [20:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:15), [20:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:17), [20:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:19). **2\.** of the Babylonian empire: Ez [19:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:8); [Dn 8:2](https://ref.ly/Dan8:2) (of Elam); שָרָתִי בַּמְּדִינוֺת *princess among the provinces* [La 1:1](https://ref.ly/Lam1:1) (of Judea). **3\.** of the Persian empire [Est 1:1](https://ref.ly/Esth1:1) \+ oft.; one of which was Palestine, [Ne 1:3](https://ref.ly/Neh1:3); the returning exiles, בְּנֵי הַמְּדִינָה *children of the province* [Ezr 2:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:1), [Ne 7:6](https://ref.ly/Neh7:6) having רָאשֵׁי הַמְּדִינָה [Ne 11:3](https://ref.ly/Neh11:3). **4\.** provinces in general [Ec 2:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:8), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:7); בְּמִשְׁמַנֵּי מְדִינָה *in the fat places of the province* [Dn 11:24](https://ref.ly/Dan11:24) (fertile regions, prob. Egypt; other trans. vid. in Bev.)
> 
> 193:b. מְדִינָה: cf. also Oaram. מדינתא *city,* Palm. מדיתא *city,* sy. מדיתהון, pl. מדינתא SAC70 Lzb306 Cooke266.

> \[Str3036] † יָדוֺן **n.pr.m.** a Meronothite, one of the builders of the walls of Jerusalem [Ne 3:7](https://ref.ly/Neh3:7).

> \[Str7384,7384] † דִּיפַת **n.pr.m.** a son of Gomer son of Japhet [1 Ch 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:6); but rd. prob. רִיפַת (q.v.); LXX Ἐρειφαθ, Ῥιφαε; LXXL Ῥιφαθ; ‖ [Gn 10:3](https://ref.ly/Gen10:3) רִיפַת LXX Ῥιφαθ, Ἐριφαθ.

## דָּכָא (id: BDB02126)

\[Str1792] † \[דָּכָא] **vb.** crush, poet. (As. *dakû, crush*, Muss\-ArnoltHbr. Oct. 1890, 66\) not in **Qal**;—**Niph.** *Pt.* נִדְכָּאִים [Is 57:15](https://ref.ly/Isa57:15); —*crushed*, fig. \= *contrite ones.* **Pi.** *Pf.* דִּכָּא [Psalm 143:3](https://ref.ly/Ps143:3); 2 ms. דִּכִּאתָ [Psalm 89:11](https://ref.ly/Ps89:11); *Impf.* וִידַכֵּא [Psalm 72:4](https://ref.ly/Ps72:4); 2 ms. תְּדַכֵּא [Pr 22:22](https://ref.ly/Prov22:22) (juss); יְדַכְּאוּ [Psalm 94:5](https://ref.ly/Ps94:5), תְּדַכְּאוּ [Is 3:15](https://ref.ly/Isa3:15); sf. וִידַכְּאֵנִי [Jb 6:9](https://ref.ly/Job6:9), יְדַכְּאוּם [Jb 4:19](https://ref.ly/Job4:19); 2 mpl. וּתְדַכּאוּנַּנִי [Jb 19:2](https://ref.ly/Job19:2) (so Baer, v. his ed. p. 44, cf. Norzi); *Inf.* לִדַכֵּא [La 3:34](https://ref.ly/Lam3:34); *sf.* דַּכְּאוֺ [Is 53:10](https://ref.ly/Isa53:10); —*crush*, (fig.) c. acc. one's life to the earth [Psalm 143:3](https://ref.ly/Ps143:3), Egypt [Psalm 89:11](https://ref.ly/Ps89:11) (cf. De Che; ´י subj.), servant of Yahweh [Is 53:10](https://ref.ly/Isa53:10) (´י subj.), [Jb 6:9](https://ref.ly/Job6:9) ´וְיֹאֵל אֱלוֺהַּ וִיד; oppressor [Psalm 72:4](https://ref.ly/Ps72:4), God's people [Psalm 94:5](https://ref.ly/Ps94:5) (‖יְּעַנּוּ), [Is 3:15](https://ref.ly/Isa3:15), cf. [Pr 22:22](https://ref.ly/Prov22:22) illegally in tribunal, [La 3:34](https://ref.ly/Lam3:34) תחת רגליו ´ד (in all human oppressor subj.); *crush me* בְּמִלִּים [Jb 19:2](https://ref.ly/Job19:2) (Job's friends, subj.; ‖תּוֺגְיוּן נַפְשִׁי); never lit., not even [Jb 4:19](https://ref.ly/Job4:19) (יְדַכְּאוּם, with indef. subj.)., for suff. ref. not to בָּֽתֵּי־חֹמֶר, but rather to שֹׁכְנֵי, i.e. men inhabiting the clay houses, bodies, cf. Di. **Pu.** *Pf.* דֻּכְּאוּ [Je 44:10](https://ref.ly/Jer44:10); *Impf.* יְדֻכָּא [Jb 22:9](https://ref.ly/Job22:9); *Pt.* מְדֻכָּא [Is 53:5](https://ref.ly/Isa53:5), מְדֻכָּאִים [Is 19:10](https://ref.ly/Isa19:10); —*crushed*, broken in pieces, shattered [Jb 22:9](https://ref.ly/Job22:9) c. obj. fem. זרועות; [Is 19:10](https://ref.ly/Isa19:10) subj. שָׁתוֺת (here metaphor. for nobles); fig. of servant of ´י [Is 53:5](https://ref.ly/Isa53:5); made humble, contrite [Je 44:10](https://ref.ly/Jer44:10). **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִדַּכְּאוּ [Jb 5:4](https://ref.ly/Job5:4), יִדַּכָּאוּ [Jb 34:25](https://ref.ly/Job34:25); —*must let themselves be crushed*, i.e. maltreated [Jb 5:4](https://ref.ly/Job5:4) (in court בשׁער, cf. **Pi.** [Pr 22:22](https://ref.ly/Prov22:22)); *are crushed* [Jb 34:25](https://ref.ly/Job34:25) (the mighty, by God).

> \[Str1793] † I. דַּכָּא **adj.** contrite (crushed)—דַּכָּא [Is 57:15](https://ref.ly/Isa57:15); דַּכְּאֵי [Psalm 34:19](https://ref.ly/Ps34:19)—*contrite* [Is 57:15](https://ref.ly/Isa57:15) (‖שְׁפַל־רוּחַ); רוּחַ ´ד [Psalm 34:19](https://ref.ly/Ps34:19) (‖נִשְׁבְּרֵי־לֵב).

> \[Str1793] † II. דַּכָּא **n.\[m.]** dust (as pulverized), תָּשֵׁב אֱנוֺשׁ עַד־דַּכָּא [Psalm 90:3](https://ref.ly/Ps90:3) (on form cf. BaNB 143\).

## דָּכָה (id: BDB02129)

\[Str1794] † \[דָּכָה] **vb.** id. (only Psalm);—**Qal** dub.; ודכה (יָשֹׁחַ) [Psalm 10:10](https://ref.ly/Ps10:10) Kt is rd. as **Qal** *Pf.* וְדָבָה consec. by RobGes Ol De al., i.e. *and he is crushed*, or *and he croucheth* (Qr יִדְכֶּה *Impf.* so AV RV *he croucheth*); others rd. וְדָכֶה יָשֹׁחַ (adj. intrans. or passive) *and crushed he sinketh down*, cf. VB Che; possible wd. be יִדָּכֶה Niph. (‖יָשֹׁחַ and ונפל). **Niph.** *Pf.* וְנִדְכֵּ֫יתִי [Psalm 38:9](https://ref.ly/Ps38:9); *Pt.* נִדְכֶּה [Psalm 51:19](https://ref.ly/Ps51:19); —*be crushed, broken*, of physical distress [Psalm 38:9](https://ref.ly/Ps38:9) (‖נְפוּגֹתִי); *be contrite* (לֵב נִדְכֶּה) [Psalm 51:19](https://ref.ly/Ps51:19) (‖נִשְׁבָּר). **Pi.** *Pf.* דִּכִּיתָ [Psalm 51:10](https://ref.ly/Ps51:10); sf. דִּכִּיתָנוּ [Psalm 44:20](https://ref.ly/Ps44:20); *crush down*, of divine wrath; c. acc. [Psalm 44:20](https://ref.ly/Ps44:20), and *crush to pieces*, obj. ref. to עצמות, in metaph. [Psalm 51:10](https://ref.ly/Ps51:10).—דָּכוּ֙ [Nu 11:8](https://ref.ly/Num11:8) v. דוך.

> \[Str1796] † \[דֳּכִי] **n.\[m.]** (crushing), crashing, dashing, sf. ישאִו נהרות דָּכְיָם [Psalm 93:3](https://ref.ly/Ps93:3) of ocean (waves).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 44:20; Psalms 51:19; Psalms 51:10; Psalms 10:10; Psalms 38:9; Psalms 93:3; Numbers 11:8
* **Associated Articles:** דּוּךְ (ID: `BDB02067@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## דכך (id: BDB02131)

דכך (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. دَكَّ, Aram. דְּכַח NH in deriv.; v. also דכא, דכה).

> \[Str1790] † דַּךְ **adj.** crushed, oppressed—דַּךְ [Psalm 74:21](https://ref.ly/Ps74:21); דָּךְ [Psalm 9:10](https://ref.ly/Ps9:10), [10:18](https://ref.ly/Ps10:18); pl. sf. דַּכָּו [Pr 26:28](https://ref.ly/Prov26:28) (Baer; edd. al. דַּכָּיו)—poet.; always fig., *oppressed, distressed* [Psalm 9:10](https://ref.ly/Ps9:10), [10:18](https://ref.ly/Ps10:18) (‖יתום) [74:21](https://ref.ly/Ps74:21)(‖עָנִי and אביון); [Pr 26:28](https://ref.ly/Prov26:28) דַּכָּו \= *those crushed by it* (a lying tongue).

> \[Str1795] † דַּכָּה **n.f.** crushing, פְּחוּעַ־דַּכָּה [Dt 23:2](https://ref.ly/Deut23:2) *one wounded by crushing* (viz. of testicles).

> \[Str1817] דָּל, דַּל **n.** *door*, v. sub דלה. below

> \[Str1800] דַּל **adj.** v. sub דלל. p. 195

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 26:28; Psalms 9:10; Psalms 74:21; Psalms 10:18; Deuteronomy 23:2


## דָּלַג (id: BDB02136)

\[Str1801] † \[דָּלַג] **vb.** leap ( NH *spring over, skip* (a verse), omit)—**Qal** *Pt.* דּוֺלֵג [Zp 1:9](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:9) *leap*, c. art. *one leaping* על־הַמִּפְתָּן; LXX [1 S 5:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:5) ὑπερβαίνοντες ὑπερβαίνουσί \= דָּלֽג יִדְלָֽגוּ cf. Dr. **Pi.** *Impf.* יְדַלֵּג [Is 35:6](https://ref.ly/Isa35:6); אֲדַלֶּ־ג [Psalm 18:30](https://ref.ly/Ps18:30) \= [2 S 22:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:30); *Pt.* מְדַלֵּג Ct [2:8](https://ref.ly/Song2:8); —*leap, leap over* c. acc. שׁוּר [Psalm 18:30](https://ref.ly/Ps18:30) \= [2 S 22:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:30); abs. *leap as the stag*, כָּאַיָּל פִּסֵּחַ ´יְד [Is 35:6](https://ref.ly/Isa35:6); sq. על of locality Ct [2:8](https://ref.ly/Song2:8) (‖מְקַפֵּץ).

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Samuel 22:30; Zephaniah 1:9; 1 Samuel 5:5; Psalms 18:30; Isaiah 35:6; Song of Songs 2:8


## דָּלָה (id: BDB02137)

\[Str1802] † דָּלָה **vb.** draw (water) (NH *id.*, Aram. דְּלָא, ܕܠܴܐ; Ar. دَلَا *pull up* bucket; II, IV *let down;* v *hang down*; cf. دَلْوٌ *bucket;* Eth. ደለወ፡: *weigh;* As. *dalû, draw water*, cf. Dl § 108 C AdlerPAOS Oct. 1888, xcix.; also *dilûtu, bucket*, Idib; *dalâni* COTGloss)—**Qal** *Pf.* דָּלָה [Ex 2:19](https://ref.ly/Exod2:19); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יִדְלֶָנָּה [Pr 20:5](https://ref.ly/Prov20:5); דַּלְיוּ [Pr 26:7](https://ref.ly/Prov26:7) v.infr.; 3f.p1\. וַתִּדְלֶנָה [Ex 2:16](https://ref.ly/Exod2:16); *Inf. abs* דָּלֽה [Ex 2:19](https://ref.ly/Exod2:19); —*draw* (water, but no obj. expr.) [Ex 2:6](https://ref.ly/Exod2:6), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Exod2:19), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Exod2:19), fig. of drawing counsel (עֵצָזה) out of heart, c. acc. (sf.) [Pr 20:5](https://ref.ly/Prov20:5).—דַּלְיוּ [Pr 26:7](https://ref.ly/Prov26:7) is difficult; rd. perh. 3 m. pl. דָּלָיוּ intrans., a lame man's legs *hang down* (helpless) so Ew Sta§ 413 a; other views in De Now. **Pi.** *Pf.* 2 ms. sf. דִּלִּיתָנִי [Psalm 30:2](https://ref.ly/Ps30:2) fig. (cf. Pa. in Syr.) subj. ´י *thou hast drawn me up* (out of Shₑ’ôl, cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ps30:4)).

> \[Str1817] † \[דָּל] **n.\[m.]** door (Ph. דל) only fig.: cstr. דַּל שָפָתָי [Psalm 141:3](https://ref.ly/Ps141:3).

> \[Str1817] † \[דָּלָה] **n.f.** door, only Qr דְּלָתְֽךָ (Kt דלתיך) [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20) fig., door of chamber in which people (personified) hides (cf. also דֶּלֶת du.) p. 195

Löw: 65\).

## דָּלַח (id: BDB02144)

\[Str1804] † \[דָּלַח] **vb.** make turbid (As. *dalâḫu, disturb*, ZimBP 81 also in deriv.; Pal. Targum דְּלַח fig. *be anxious, fear*, ܕܠܰܚ *turbavit, conturbavit*)—**Qal** *Impf.* 2 ms., וַתִּדְלַח Ez [32:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:2); 3 fs. sf. תִּדְלָחֵם Ez [32:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:13) \+ v:[13](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:13) (Co תרפשם);—*stir up, trouble, make turbid* (always with *feet*) sq. מים Ez [32:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:2) (‖תִּרְפֹּס,—ס Baer, and not ש); so [32:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:13) a, b (but v:[13](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:13) b. Co rds. תרפש (cf. v:[2](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:2)) for תדלחם).

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 32:13; Ezekiel 32:2


## דָּלַל (id: BDB02145)

\[Str1809] † דָּלַל **vb.** hang, be low, languish (NH Hiph. *thin, thin out* vines, etc.; As. *dalâlu, be weak, humble* ZimBP 74\. 97; Ar. دَلَّ *direct, guide*, دُلَّ, *be directed, guided*; دَلْدَلَ *put in motion, commotion;* تَدَلْدَلَ *be in motion, hang, dangle;* also دَلٌّ *amorous, coquettish, gesture* or *behaviour* of women)—**Qal** *Pf.* דֵּלּֽתִי [Psalm 116:6](https://ref.ly/Ps116:6), דַּלּוֺתִי [142:7](https://ref.ly/Ps142:7); 1 pl. דַּלּוֺנוּ [Psalm 79:8](https://ref.ly/Ps79:8), דָּֽלֲלוּ [Is 19:6](https://ref.ly/Isa19:6), דַּלּוּ [Is 38:14](https://ref.ly/Isa38:14), [Jb 28:4](https://ref.ly/Job28:4) (on דַּלְיוּ [Pr 26:7](https://ref.ly/Prov26:7) cf. דָּלָה);—*hang, ‘depend’*, [Jb 28:4](https://ref.ly/Job28:4) (of one descending a miner's shaft ‖נָעוּ); *be low*, of streams [Is 19:6](https://ref.ly/Isa19:6) (‖חָֽרְבוּ); *be low, brought low*, metaph. of distress [Psalm 79:8](https://ref.ly/Ps79:8), [116:6](https://ref.ly/Ps116:6), [142:7](https://ref.ly/Ps142:7); *languish*, of eyes, *look languishingly* (Che) [Is 38:14](https://ref.ly/Isa38:14).—**Niph.** *Impf.* יִדַּל [Is 17:4](https://ref.ly/Isa17:4), וַיִּדַּל Ju [6:6](https://ref.ly/Judg6:6), —*be brought low, laid low*, Ju [6:6](https://ref.ly/Judg6:6) fig. of Israel; [Is 17:4](https://ref.ly/Isa17:4) of glory of Jacob.

> \[Str1800] † דַּל **adj.** low, weak, poor, thin (especially common in Wisd. lit. and poet.)—דַּל [Lv 14:21](https://ref.ly/Lev14:21) \+ 8 t., דָּל [Ex 23:3](https://ref.ly/Exod23:3) \+ 17 t. \+ שָדּל [Psalm 82:3](https://ref.ly/Ps82:3); דַּלִּים [Am 2:7](https://ref.ly/Amos2:7) \+ 17 t.; דַּלּוֺת [Gn 41:19](https://ref.ly/Gen41:19); —*weak, thin*, of kine [Gn 41:19](https://ref.ly/Gen41:19) (E); of Amnon [2 S 13:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:4); *weak*, of family of Saul 2 S 3:l (opp. חָזֵק) cf. Ju [6:15](https://ref.ly/Judg6:15); *reduced, poor* (opp. rich) [Lv 14:21](https://ref.ly/Lev14:21)(P) [Ru 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:10) (opp. עָשִׁיר) [Je 5:4](https://ref.ly/Jer5:4) (opp. to הגדולים, v:[5](https://ref.ly/Jer5:5)) [Pr 28:15](https://ref.ly/Prov28:15), [Zp 3:12](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:12) (עַם עָנִי וָדָל); mostly subst., *a poor* (man), *the poor* [Ex 23:3](https://ref.ly/Exod23:3) (JE); opp. to עָשִׁיר [Ex 30:15](https://ref.ly/Exod30:15) (P) [Pr 10:15](https://ref.ly/Prov10:15), [22:16](https://ref.ly/Prov22:16), [28:11](https://ref.ly/Prov28:11); opp. הוֺן [Pr 19:4](https://ref.ly/Prov19:4); ‖אביון [1 S 2:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:8), [Jb 5:16](https://ref.ly/Job5:16), [Psalm 72:13](https://ref.ly/Ps72:13), [82:4](https://ref.ly/Ps82:4), [113:7](https://ref.ly/Ps113:7), [Pr 14:31](https://ref.ly/Prov14:31), [Is 14:30](https://ref.ly/Isa14:30), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Isa25:4), [Am 4:1](https://ref.ly/Amos4:1), [8:6](https://ref.ly/Amos8:6); cf. also [Jb 20:10](https://ref.ly/Job20:10), [20:19](https://ref.ly/Job20:19), [Pr 22:9](https://ref.ly/Prov22:9), [Je 39:10](https://ref.ly/Jer39:10); *reduced, weak, helpless* (‖עני) [Jb 34:28](https://ref.ly/Job34:28), [Psalm 82:3](https://ref.ly/Ps82:3)(‖ also יתום), [Pr 22:22](https://ref.ly/Prov22:22), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Prov10:2), [11:4](https://ref.ly/Prov11:4), [26:6](https://ref.ly/Prov26:6), [Am 2:7](https://ref.ly/Amos2:7); ‖אלמנה [Jb 31:16](https://ref.ly/Job31:16); opp. שׁוֺעַ [Jb 34:19](https://ref.ly/Job34:19); —cf. also [Psalm 41:2](https://ref.ly/Ps41:2), [Pr 19:17](https://ref.ly/Prov19:17), [21:13](https://ref.ly/Prov21:13), [28:3](https://ref.ly/Prov28:3), [28:8](https://ref.ly/Prov28:8), [29:7](https://ref.ly/Prov29:7), [29:14](https://ref.ly/Prov29:14).

> \[Str1803] † I. דַּלָּה **n.f.** coll., hair, thrum (both front hanging down)—דַּלָּה [Is 38:12](https://ref.ly/Isa38:12); cstr. דַּלַּת Ct [7:6](https://ref.ly/Song7:6); —*hair*, ראשׁך ´ד Ct [7:6](https://ref.ly/Song7:6); *thrum* (threads of warp hanging in loom) in sim. of premature death מִדַּלָּה יְבַצְּעֵנִי [Is 38:12](https://ref.ly/Isa38:12).

> \[Str1803] † II. \[דַּלָּה] **n.f.** the poor—cstr. דַּלַּת [Je 40:7](https://ref.ly/Jer40:7) \+ 2 t.; pl. דַּלּוֺת [Je 52:15](https://ref.ly/Jer52:15), [52:16](https://ref.ly/Jer52:16); —*the poor* (coll., weak, helpless ones) [Je 40:7](https://ref.ly/Jer40:7), [2 K 24:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:14), [25:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:12) (in ‖ [Je 52:16](https://ref.ly/Jer52:16), as also v:[15](https://ref.ly/Jer52:15), MT has strangely the pl.)

> \[Str1807] † דְּלִילָה **n.pr.f.** Delila, Philistine woman, mistress of Samson Ju [16:4](https://ref.ly/Judg16:4), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Judg16:6), [16:10](https://ref.ly/Judg16:10), [16:12](https://ref.ly/Judg16:12), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Judg16:13), [16:18](https://ref.ly/Judg16:18).

> \[Str1810] † דִּלְעָן **n.pr.loc.** a city of Judah, [Jos 15:38](https://ref.ly/Josh15:38).

## דָּלַף (id: BDB02151)

\[Str1811] † דָּלַף **vb.** drop, drip (WisdLt.) (NH *id.* (זלף more common), דֶּלֶף *through*, etc., Aram. דְּלַף, ܕܠܰܦ, *drip*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. אֶל־אֱלוֺהַּ דָּֽלְפָה עֵינִי [Jb 16:20](https://ref.ly/Job16:20) i.e. weeps (*drops* in tears); דָּֽלְפָה נַפְשִׁי מִתּוּגָה [Psalm 119:28](https://ref.ly/Ps119:28) i.e. weeps (itself away Che cf. De); *Impf.* :בְּשִׁפְלוּת יָדַיִם יִדְלֽף הַבָּֽיִת [Ec 10:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:18) *the house drips*, i.e. leaks, because cracks are not mended.

> \[Str1812] † דֶּ֫לֶף **n.m.** a dropping (of rain, cf. WetzstZPV xiv.1891, 5\), fig. דֶּלֶף טֹרֵד מִדְיְנֵי אִשָּׁה [Pr 19:13](https://ref.ly/Prov19:13) etc.; :טוֺרֵד בְּיוֺם סֵגְרִָיר וְאֵשֶׁת מִדְוָ֯נִים נִשְׁתָּֽוָהּ ´ד [Pr 27:15](https://ref.ly/Prov27:15).

> \[Str1813] † דַּלְפוֺן **n.pr.m.** a son of Haman, [Est 9:7](https://ref.ly/Esth9:7).

* **Associated Passages:** Esther 9:7; Ecclesiastes 10:18; Psalms 119:28; Job 16:20; Proverbs 19:13; Proverbs 27:15


## דָּלַק (id: BDB02154)

\[Str1814] † דָּלַק **vb.** burn (intrans.), hotly pursue (NH *id.*, Aram. דְּלַק, ܕܠܰܩ all intrans. *burn, kindle;* Hiph., Aph. transitive)—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. דָּלַקְתָּ [Gn 31:36](https://ref.ly/Gen31:36); 3 mpl. וְדָֽלְקוּ consec. Ob:[18](https://ref.ly/Obad1:18); sf. דְּלָקֻנִי [La 4:19](https://ref.ly/Lam4:19); *Impf.* יִדְלַק [Psalm 10:2](https://ref.ly/Ps10:2); *Inf. cstr.* דְּלֽק [1 S 17:53](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:53); *Pt. act.* pl. דֹּלְקִים [Psalm 7:14](https://ref.ly/Ps7:14), [Pr 26:23](https://ref.ly/Prov26:23); —**1\.** *burn*, וְדָֽלְקוּ בָהֶם Ob:[18](https://ref.ly/Obad1:18) (fig. of Isr. ravaging among Edomites, ‖וַאֲכָלוּם; on construction c. בְּ cf. [2 K 17:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:25)); also in חִצָּיו לְדֹלְקִים יִפְעָל [Psalm 7:14](https://ref.ly/Ps7:14) *his arrows he maketh burning ones* (subj. God) RV De Che al.; \& שְפָתַיִם דֹּלְקִים [Pr 26:23](https://ref.ly/Prov26:23) *burning (fervent) lips* (opp. לֵב רָע). **2\.** *hotly pursue* [Gn 31:36](https://ref.ly/Gen31:36) (E; sq. אַחֲרֵי) [1 S 17:53](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:53) (sq. id.); [Psalm 10:2](https://ref.ly/Ps10:2), [La 4:19](https://ref.ly/Lam4:19) (both sq. acc.) **Hiph.** *Impf.* sf. יַדְלִיקֵם [Is 5:11](https://ref.ly/Isa5:11); *Imv.* הַדְלֵק Ez [24:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:10); —*inflame*, c. acc., subj. wine [Is 5:11](https://ref.ly/Isa5:11); *kindle* obj. הָאֵשׁ Ez [24:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:10).

> \[Str1816] † דַּלֶּ֫קֶת **n.f.** inflammation, [Dt 28:22](https://ref.ly/Deut28:22).

> \[Str1817] דֶּלֶת v. sub דלה. p. 195

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 5:11; Proverbs 26:23; Psalms 10:2; Genesis 31:36; Ezekiel 24:10; Deuteronomy 28:22; Psalms 7:14; 2 Kings 17:25; 1 Samuel 17:53; Lamentations 4:19

H Vrss Co Comm.; אָרַב לְדָם *lie in wait for blood* [Pr 1:11](https://ref.ly/Prov1:11) cf. v:[18](https://ref.ly/Prov1:18), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Prov12:6), [Mi 7:2](https://ref.ly/Mic7:2); דָּמִים of a *plan of murder* [Is 33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15) (obj. of שׁמע); מִשְׁפַּט דָּמִים Ez [7:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:23) *bloody crime;* as symbol of oppression, violence [Mi 3:10](https://ref.ly/Mic3:10) (‖עַוְלָה), Hb [2:12](https://ref.ly/Hab2:12) (‖ id.) **g.** דָּמִים \= *guilt of bloodshed, blood\-guiltiness* [Ex 22:1](https://ref.ly/Exod22:1), then more generally, *mortal sin* (RSOTJC 417, 2nd ed. 441, cf. Ez [18:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:13)) [Psalm 51:16](https://ref.ly/Ps51:16) (cf. [Ho 12:15](https://ref.ly/Hos12:15)); also בּוֺא בְדָמִים [1 S 25:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:26), [25:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:33). **h.** pl. also in pharses: חֲתַן דָּמִים *bloody bridegroom* [Ex 4:25](https://ref.ly/Exod4:25), [4:26](https://ref.ly/Exod4:26); also those denoting character, chiefly poet., אִישׁ דָּמִים *bloody man* (sanguinary) [2 S 16:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:8), [Psalm 5:7](https://ref.ly/Ps5:7) אִישׁ הַדָּמִים [2 S 16:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:7); אַנְשֵׁי דָּמִים, [Psalm 26:9](https://ref.ly/Ps26:9), [55:24](https://ref.ly/Ps55:24), [59:3](https://ref.ly/Ps59:3), [139:19](https://ref.ly/Ps139:19), [Pr 29:10](https://ref.ly/Prov29:10); ואל־בֵּית הַדָּמִים *bloody house* [2 S 21:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:1) but rd. with LXX We Dr ואל בֵּיתֹה דמים, *\& on his house is blood;* עִיר דָּמִים [Na 3:1](https://ref.ly/Nah3:1); עִיר הַדָּמִים Ez [22:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:2), [24:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:6) \+ v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:9) (del. B Co). **i.** דָּמָיו בּוֺ *his blood is in (upon) him*, he is responsible for his own death [Lv 20:9](https://ref.ly/Lev20:9) cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/Lev20:11), v:[12](https://ref.ly/Lev20:12), v:[13](https://ref.ly/Lev20:13), v:[16](https://ref.ly/Lev20:16), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Lev20:27) (all H), Ez [18:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:13) ( \+ יִהְיֶה); דָּמוֺ בּוֺ Ez [33:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:5); דָּמוֺ בְּראֹשׁוֺ [Jos 2:19](https://ref.ly/Josh2:19), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Josh2:19); so with שָׁבוּ [1 K 2:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:33), יִהְיֶה v:[37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:37), Ez [33:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:4); דָּֽמְ֯יךָ עַל ראֹשֶָׁךָ [2 S 1:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:16), and אֶת־דָּמוֺ עַל־ראֹשׁוֺ ´הֵשִׁיב י [1 K 2:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:32); also לאֹ תָשִים דָּמִים בְּבֵיתְךָ [Dt 22:8](https://ref.ly/Deut22:8); and דָּמָם לָשוּם עַל אֲבִימֶלֶח Ju [9:24](https://ref.ly/Judg9:24); (אל)נָתַן דָּם על [Je 26:15](https://ref.ly/Jer26:15), [Jon 1:14](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:14); וְהָיָה עָלָיךָ דָּמִים [Dt 19:10](https://ref.ly/Deut19:10), v. [2 S 21:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:1) supr.; also sg. Ez [22:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:4) בְּדָמֵח אָשַׁמְתְּ, and as subj. of יֵחָשֵׁב לְ *shall be imputed to* [Lv 17:4](https://ref.ly/Lev17:4) (H); אֵין לוֺ דָמִים [Ex 22:1](https://ref.ly/Exod22:1) cf. v:[2](https://ref.ly/Exod22:2) (both JE) \& (sg.) [Nu 35:27](https://ref.ly/Num35:27) (P). **j.** of judicial process in case of bloodshed דָּבָר לַמִּשְׁפָּט בֵּין־דָּם לְדָם [Dt 17:8](https://ref.ly/Deut17:8) cf. [2 Ch 19:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:10); לאֹ תַּעֲמֹד עַל־דַּם רֵעֶָךָ [Lv 19:16](https://ref.ly/Lev19:16) (H). **k.** in phrase *require blood at the hand of*, exact vengeance (for it) from, דָּרַשׁ דָּמוֺ מִיַּד Ez [33:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:6), [Gn 9:5](https://ref.ly/Gen9:5); דָּמוֺ נִדְרָשׁ [Gn 42:22](https://ref.ly/Gen42:22) (E); abs. דֹּרשׁ דמים [Psalm 9:13](https://ref.ly/Ps9:13); אֲבַקֵּשׁ אֶת־דָּמוֺ מִיֶּדְכֶם [2 S 4:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:11) cf. Ez [3:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:18), [3:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:20), [33:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:8); עַ־ל ´פָּקַד ד [Ho 1:4](https://ref.ly/Hos1:4); ´וְנִקֵּיתִי ד [Jo 4:21](https://ref.ly/Joel4:21); Ez [35:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:6), [35:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:6), [35:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:6), [35:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:6); cf. on the other hand ´אַל־יִפֹּל דָּמִי אַרְצָה מִנֶּגֶד פְּנֵי י [1 S 26:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:20) i.e. let it not de unavenged. **1\.** of blood as defiling a land [Nu 35:33](https://ref.ly/Num35:33) (P) cf. [Psalm 106:38](https://ref.ly/Ps106:38) (pl. בדמים); עֲקֻבָּה מִדָּם [Ho 6:8](https://ref.ly/Hos6:8) of a city *foot\-printed* (VB) *with blood;* defiling hands [Is 1:15](https://ref.ly/Isa1:15), [59:3](https://ref.ly/Isa59:3), [La 4:14](https://ref.ly/Lam4:14) cf. Ez [23:37](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:37), [23:45](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:45); further, of blood as oppressing one who has shed it עָשֻׁק בְּדַם נֶפֶשׁ Pr 28:l7\. **m.** atonement for blood\-guilt is expressed by וְנִכַּפֵּר לָהֶם הַדָּם [Dt 21:8](https://ref.ly/Deut21:8) (on form of vb. cf. Ges§ 55, 9 \& Di). **n.** blood (\-shedding) as connected with divine wrath וְשָׁפַכְתִּ֫י חֲמָתִי עָלָיהָ בְּדָם Ez [14:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:19); דַּם חֵמָה וְקִנְאָה Ez [16:38](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:38); v. further אַשְׁכִּיר חִצַּי מִדָּם [Dt 32:42](https://ref.ly/Deut32:42), cf. [Je 46:10](https://ref.ly/Jer46:10); cf. also of human vengeance by divine help יִרְחַץ בְּדַם הָרָשָׁע [Psalm 58:11](https://ref.ly/Ps58:11); תִּמֲחַץ רַגְלְךָ בְּדָם [68:24](https://ref.ly/Ps68:24); note further [Is 49:26](https://ref.ly/Isa49:26), Ez [39:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:19) (in both fig. of being drunk with blood); then of simple human vengeance וְהוֺרַדְתָּ֫ אֶת־שֵיבָתוֺ בְּדָם שְׁאוֺל [1 K 2:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:9). **o.** of water turned into blood (sg.) in Egypt, by divine power [Ex 4:9](https://ref.ly/Exod4:9) (J) [7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Exod7:20) (JE) v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod7:19), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod7:19), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Exod7:21) (P) [Psalm 78:44](https://ref.ly/Ps78:44), [105:29](https://ref.ly/Ps105:29); cf. [Is 15:9](https://ref.ly/Isa15:9) \& Ez [32:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:6); further, of appearance of blood in sky [Jo 3:3](https://ref.ly/Joel3:3), \& of colour of moon v:[4](https://ref.ly/Joel3:4); specifically of red colour of water אֲדֻמִּים כַּדָּם [2 K 3:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:22) cf. v:[23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:23). **3\.** blood used with religious significance, **a.** blood of passover\-lamb [Ex 12:7](https://ref.ly/Exod12:7), [12:13](https://ref.ly/Exod12:13), [12:13](https://ref.ly/Exod12:13) (all P) v:[22](https://ref.ly/Exod12:22), v:[22](https://ref.ly/Exod12:22), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Exod12:23) (all JE); note also דַּם הַבְּרִית, i.e. blood by which covenant was ratified [Ex 24:8](https://ref.ly/Exod24:8) (JE) [Zc 9:11](https://ref.ly/Zech9:11). **b.** blood used in ritual, [Lv 17:11](https://ref.ly/Lev17:11) הַדָּם הוּא בַּנֶּפֶשׁ יְכַפֵּר (H); cf. ## I דָּמָה (id: BDB02158)

\[Str1819] † I. דָּמָה **vb.** be like, resemble (NH *id.,* Aram. דְּמֵי, דְּמָא, ܕܡܳܐ; cf. דמין, ܕܡܰܝܳܐ *price*, Nab. דמי *id.* (Nö in EutNab. Inscr. p. 35\); also ܕܡܽܘܬܳܐ, and ܕܽܘܡܝܳܐ *likeness* (whence Ar. دُمْيَةٌ *image, effigy,* as loan\-wd. Frä272) cf. דְּמוּת)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´ד Ez [31:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:8), [Psalm 144:4](https://ref.ly/Ps144:4); 3 fs. דָּֽמְתָה Ct [7:8](https://ref.ly/Song7:8); 2 ms. דָּמִיתָ Ez [31:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:2), [31:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:18); 1 s. דָּמִיתִי [Psalm 102:7](https://ref.ly/Ps102:7); 3 pl. דָּמוּ Ez [31:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:8); 1 pl. דָּמִינוּ [Is 1:9](https://ref.ly/Isa1:9); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִדְמֶה [Psalm 89:7](https://ref.ly/Ps89:7); 1 pl. וְנִדְמֶה consec. [Is 46:5](https://ref.ly/Isa46:5); *Imv.* ms. דְּמֵה Ct [2:17](https://ref.ly/Song2:17), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Song8:14); *Pt.* דּוֺמֶה Ct [2:9](https://ref.ly/Song2:9); —*be like, resemble,* of external appearance, sq. לְ, Ct [7:8](https://ref.ly/Song7:8), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Song2:9), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Song2:17), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Song8:14); sq. אֶ־ל Ez [31:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:8), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:8), [31:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:18) (all of tree, fig. of Pharaoh); of condition or quality sq. לְ [Is 1:9](https://ref.ly/Isa1:9), [Psalm 102:7](https://ref.ly/Ps102:7), [144:4](https://ref.ly/Ps144:4); so יִדְמֶה ליהוה…מִי [Psalm 89:7](https://ref.ly/Ps89:7); also abs. [Is 46:5](https://ref.ly/Isa46:5); sq. אֶל Ez [31:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:2). **Pi.** *Pf.* דִּמָּה [2 S 21:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:5), דִּמִּיתָ [Psalm 50:21](https://ref.ly/Ps50:21); דִּמִּיתִי [Nu 33:56](https://ref.ly/Num33:56), [Is 14:24](https://ref.ly/Isa14:24); sf. דִּמִּיתִיךָ Ct [1:9](https://ref.ly/Song1:9), דִּמּוּ [2 S 21:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:5); *Impf.* יְדַמֶּה [Is 10:7](https://ref.ly/Isa10:7); 2 fs. תְּדַמִּי [Est 4:13](https://ref.ly/Esth4:13) (juss.); אֲדַמֶּה [La 2:13](https://ref.ly/Lam2:13), [Ho 12:11](https://ref.ly/Hos12:11); 2 mpl. תְּדַמְּיוּן [Is 40:18](https://ref.ly/Isa40:18); sf. תְּדַמְּיוּנִי [Is 40:25](https://ref.ly/Isa40:25), [46:5](https://ref.ly/Isa46:5); —*liken, compare; imagine, think, devise;*—**1\.** *liken,* i.e. *consider to be like, compare,* causat. of **Qal** sq. לְ of external appearance Ct [1:9](https://ref.ly/Song1:9); of quality or condition [Is 46:5](https://ref.ly/Isa46:5) (obj. ´י) [La 2:13](https://ref.ly/Lam2:13); sq. אֶל of external appearance [Is 40:18](https://ref.ly/Isa40:18), [40:25](https://ref.ly/Isa40:25) (both obj. God in ref. to idols); abs. *use comparisons* or *similitudes* (parables, symbols, etc.) [Ho 12:11](https://ref.ly/Hos12:11) (subj. ´י). **2\.** *imagine, form an idea, devise* [2 S 21:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:5) (on context cf. Dr); *think, intend,* sq. Inf. [Nu 33:56](https://ref.ly/Num33:56) (J or H) Ju [20:5](https://ref.ly/Judg20:5), [Est 4:13](https://ref.ly/Esth4:13); abs. [Is 10:7](https://ref.ly/Isa10:7) cf. [14:24](https://ref.ly/Isa14:24); *think that,* sq. obj. cl. (Impf. without conj.) [Psalm 50:21](https://ref.ly/Ps50:21); *think of,* sq. acc. [Psalm 48:10](https://ref.ly/Ps48:10). *Hithp.* *make oneself* or *become like; Impf.* אֶדַּמֶּה לְעֶלְיוֺן [Is 14:14](https://ref.ly/Isa14:14) *I will make myself like the Most High* (on form cf. Ges§ 54\. 2\).

> \[Str1823] † דְּמוּת **n.f.** likeness, similitude (mostly late) (acc. to LagBN 12\. 147 ff. mispunct. for דִּמְוַת fr. דִּמְוָה; acc. to WeProl. 413\. Eng. Tr. 389 an Aram. Loan\-word, but v. Di Gn 5:l, DrJPh xi. 216 CheOP. 474\)—abs. ´ד [Is 40:18](https://ref.ly/Isa40:18) \+ 3 t.; cstr. ´ד [Gn 5:1](https://ref.ly/Gen5:1) \+ 16 t.; sf. דְּמוּתוֺ [Gn 5:3](https://ref.ly/Gen5:3); דְּמוּתֵנוּ [1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26); —**1\.** *likeness, similitude,* of external appearance, chiefly in Ezek.: Ez [1:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:5) (*likeness,* i.e. something that appeared like) so v:[26](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:26), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:2) (אֵשׁ)דְּמוּת כְּמַרְאֵה (cf. Co), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:1) כִּסֵּא ´כְּמַרְאֵה ד; cf. also [Dn 10:16](https://ref.ly/Dan10:16) כִּדְמוּת בְּנֵי אָדָם i.e. *one like the sons of man; similitude, resemblance* Ez [1:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:5), [1:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:10), [1:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:16), [1:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:22), [1:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:26), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:10), [10:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:21), [10:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:22); דְּמוּת כְּמַרְאֵה אָדָם [1:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:26); ´מַרְאֵה ד ´כְּבוֺד י v:[28](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:28); also [2 K 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:10) (pattern of altar), [2 Ch 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:3) (images of oxen); of son in likeness of father [Gn 5:3](https://ref.ly/Gen5:3) (P); so also of man in likeness of God [Gn 1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26) (‖צֶלֶם) [5:1](https://ref.ly/Gen5:1) (both P); cf. [Is 40:18](https://ref.ly/Isa40:18) *what* ´ד *will ye compare to him* (אֵל) ? ‖דִּמָּה q.v. **2\.** adverbially, *in likeness of, like as* [Is 13:4](https://ref.ly/Isa13:4) cf. Ez [23:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:15) \& ´כִּד [Psalm 58:5](https://ref.ly/Ps58:5).—Ez [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:13) rd. וּבֵינוֺת, v. בֵּין and J P PetersJBL 1892 40\. 42\. On דְּמוּת אֶחָד Ez [1:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:16), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:10) (apparently masc.) cf. Thes \& Sm who trans. *the likeness of one had they all four;* Co rds. אחת.

> \[Str1825] † \[דִּמְיֹן] **n.\[m.]** likeness, דִּמְיֹנוֺ כְּאַרְיֵה [Psalm 17:12](https://ref.ly/Ps17:12) *his likeness is as a lion,* i.e. he is like a lion.

## II דָּמָה (id: BDB02161)

\[Str1820] † II. \[דָּמָה] **vb.** cease, cause to cease, cut off, destroy—**Qal** *Perf.* 1 s. דָּמִיתִי [Je 6:2](https://ref.ly/Jer6:2), וְדָמִ֫יתִי consec. [Ho 4:5](https://ref.ly/Hos4:5); *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּדְמֶה [La 3:49](https://ref.ly/Lam3:49); 3 fpl. juss. תִּדְמֶינָה [Je 14:17](https://ref.ly/Jer14:17); —**1\.** intr. *cease* [Je 14:17](https://ref.ly/Jer14:17) (eyes from weeping) [La 3:49](https://ref.ly/Lam3:49) (*id.*) **2\.** *cause to cease, cut off, destroy,* c. acc. Israel (under fig. of בת־ציון) [Je 6:2](https://ref.ly/Jer6:2); (under fig. of אִמֶָּךָ) [Ho 4:5](https://ref.ly/Hos4:5). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִדְמָה [Is 15:1](https://ref.ly/Isa15:1), [15:1](https://ref.ly/Isa15:1) \+; 3 fs. נִדְמְתָה [Je 47:15](https://ref.ly/Jer47:15); 2 ms. נִדְמֵיתָה Ob:[5](https://ref.ly/Obad1:5), נִדְמֵיתָ Ez [32:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:2); 1 s. נִדְמֵיתִי [Is 6:5](https://ref.ly/Isa6:5); 3 pl. נִדְמוּ [Ho 4:6](https://ref.ly/Hos4:6) \+; *Inf. abs.* נִדְמֹה [Ho 10:15](https://ref.ly/Hos10:15); *Pt.* נִדְמֶה [Ho 10:7](https://ref.ly/Hos10:7); *be cut off, destroyed, ruined,* of a city [Is 15:1](https://ref.ly/Isa15:1), [15:1](https://ref.ly/Isa15:1) (‖שֻׁדַּד) [Je 47:5](https://ref.ly/Jer47:5); of people [Ho 4:6](https://ref.ly/Hos4:6), [Zp 1:11](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:11), Ob:[5](https://ref.ly/Obad1:5); of king [Ho 10:7](https://ref.ly/Hos10:7), [10:15](https://ref.ly/Hos10:15), [10:15](https://ref.ly/Hos10:15) cf. Ez [32:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:2); of beasts [Psalm 49:13](https://ref.ly/Ps49:12), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Ps49:20); *be ruined, undone,* prophet at sight of Yahweh [Is 6:5](https://ref.ly/Isa6:5).

> \[Str1824] † דֳּמִי **n.\[m.]** cessation, pause, quiet, rest; ´ד [Is 62:6](https://ref.ly/Isa62:6) \+ 2 t.; cstr. דְּמִי [Is 38:10](https://ref.ly/Isa38:10) (but v. infr.);—**1\.** *quiet,* in phr. לָכֶם ´אַל־ד [Is 62:6](https://ref.ly/Isa62:6) *keep not quiet* (let there be *no quiet to you*); in prayer to God לָח ´אַל־ד [Psalm 83:2](https://ref.ly/Ps83:2) *keep not quiet* (inactive; ‖חרש, שׁקט); cf. לוֺ ´וְאַל־תִּתְּנוּ ד [Is 62:7](https://ref.ly/Isa62:7) *and give no rest to him* (´י). **2\.** in phr. בִּדְמִי יָמַי, appar. *quiet, peacefulness,* even tenour, of my days (so De Or SS); others, as Hi Ew Che Di, *pause, resting\-time,* i.e. *noon\-day* (cf. SyrVer Vulgate, \& *height* LXX), fig. of middle life, but usage dub.; KloSK 1884, 157 *cessation, pause,* of natural end of life, Hezekiah's natural expectation, in, contrast with the speedy death implied in vb; but parallelism of v:[11a](https://ref.ly/Isa62:11) is ag. this; Brd emends בְּרֻם after LXX; Klol. c. suggests בְּתֹם or כְּתֹם as poss., though not necessary;—but view stated first is on the whole best.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 49:12; Hosea 4:5; Isaiah 15:1; Isaiah 62:6; Psalms 49:20; Hosea 10:7; Psalms 83:2; Zephaniah 1:11; Jeremiah 14:17; Isaiah 62:7; Hosea 10:15; Ezekiel 32:2; Lamentations 3:49; Isaiah 6:5; Jeremiah 6:2; Isaiah 62:11; Jeremiah 47:5; Jeremiah 47:15; Isaiah 38:10; Hosea 4:6


## I דָּמַם (id: BDB02163)

\[Str1826] † I. \[דָּמַם] **vb.** be or grow dumb, silent, still (NH *id.;* Eth. in II. አድመመ፡ *stupefy;* III., 1 \& 2 ተደመ, ተደመመ፡ *be astounded, stupefied*)—**Qal** *Pf.* דַּ֫מּוּ [Jb 30:27](https://ref.ly/Job30:27), דָּמּוּ [Psalm 35:15](https://ref.ly/Ps35:15); *Impf.* יִדֹּם [Am 5:13](https://ref.ly/Amos5:13) \+ 2 t., וַיִּדֹּם [Lv 10:3](https://ref.ly/Lev10:3), [Jos 10:13](https://ref.ly/Josh10:13); 3 fs. תִּדֹּם [La 2:18](https://ref.ly/Lam2:18); 2 fs. תִּדֹּמִּי [Je 48:2](https://ref.ly/Jer48:2) (so Ki Ol \& Kö327 q.v.; others Niph.); וָאֶדֹּם [Jb 31:34](https://ref.ly/Job31:34): יִדְּמוּ [Ex 15:16](https://ref.ly/Exod15:16) \+ 3 t.; 1 pl. cohort. נִדְּמָה [Je 8:14](https://ref.ly/Jer8:14) (so Thes Ol Bö Kö327 q. v.; others Niph.);—**1\.** *be silent* [Psalm 4:5](https://ref.ly/Ps4:5), [30:13](https://ref.ly/Ps30:13), [35:15](https://ref.ly/Ps35:15) (prob., cf. De Che), Ez [24:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:17), [Lv 10:3](https://ref.ly/Lev10:3) (P) [Am 5:13](https://ref.ly/Amos5:13); in grief [La 2:10](https://ref.ly/Lam2:10), [3:28](https://ref.ly/Lam3:28). **2\.** *be still* (opp. to both speech and motion) [Jb 31:34](https://ref.ly/Job31:34); \= perish [Je 8:14](https://ref.ly/Jer8:14), [48:2](https://ref.ly/Jer48:2), [Psalm 31:18](https://ref.ly/Ps31:18) (לשׁאול ´ד); c. לְ *be silent to* i.e. be resigned to [Psalm 37:7](https://ref.ly/Ps37:7), [62:6](https://ref.ly/Ps62:6), sq. לְמוֺ עֲצָתִי [Jb 29:21](https://ref.ly/Job29:21) (‖לִי שָֽׁמְעוּ); *be still,* motionless, stand still [Jos 10:12](https://ref.ly/Josh10:12), [10:13](https://ref.ly/Josh10:13) (of sun ‖עמד); [1 S 14:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:9) (men); [Je 47:6](https://ref.ly/Jer47:6) (of sword ‖הֵרָֽגְעִי); so also of bowels, as seat of mental excitement [Jb 30:27](https://ref.ly/Job30:27); [La 2:18](https://ref.ly/Lam2:18) אַל־תִּדֹּם בַּת־עֵינֵח, i.e. cease not to weep (‖הוֺרִידִי כַנַּחַל דִּמְעָה). **3\.** *be struck dumb, astounded,* in amazement and fear [Ex 15:16](https://ref.ly/Exod15:16) (כאבן) [Is 23:2](https://ref.ly/Isa23:2) (but cf. II. דמם). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. וְנָדַמּוּ consec. [Je 25:37](https://ref.ly/Jer25:37); *Impf.* יִדַּמּוּ [Je 49:26](https://ref.ly/Jer49:26), [50:30](https://ref.ly/Jer50:30), יִדָּמּוּ [1 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:9); 2 mpl. תִּדַּמּוּ [Je 51:6](https://ref.ly/Jer51:6)—*be made silent,* i.e. destroyed; the wicked [1 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:9) (בַּחשֶׁח), cf. [Je 51:6](https://ref.ly/Jer51:6); men of war [Je 49:25](https://ref.ly/Jer49:25), [50:30](https://ref.ly/Jer50:30); dwellings [Je 25:37](https://ref.ly/Jer25:37). **Po.** *Pf.* 1 s. שִׁוִּיתִי וְדוֺמַ֫מְתִּי נַפְשִׁי [Psalm 131:2](https://ref.ly/Ps131:2) ‘*composed and quieted my soul*’ Che (as a weaned child). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. הֲדִמָּנוּ *he* (God) *hath silenced us* ( \= caused to perish) [Je 8:14](https://ref.ly/Jer8:14).

> \[Str1827] † דְּמָמָה **n.f.** (silence) whisper (on format. cf. BaNB 87\)—וְאַחַר הָאֵשׁ קוֺל דְּמָמָה דַקָּה [1 K 19:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:12); דְּמָמָה זָקוֺל אֶשְׁמָע [Jb 4:16](https://ref.ly/Job4:16) *a whisper* and a voice, i.e. an articulate whisper (cf. Di); יָקֵם סְעָרָה לִדְמָמָה [Psalm 107:29](https://ref.ly/Ps107:29) *he settleth storm into whisper.*

> \[Str1822] † דֻּמָּה **n.f.** one silenced, brought to silence (?; destroyed?) מִי כְצוֺר כְּדֻמָּה בְּתוֺח הַָ\+יּם Ez [27:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:32) (form pecul. \& sense dub.: Baer כְּדֻמַָה; Co כְּבֻדָּה fr. כָּבֹד adj., cf. [23:41](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:41), [Psalm 45:14](https://ref.ly/Ps45:14)) AV *What city is like Tyrus, like the destroyed* (as though for מְדֻמָּה cf. ההללה [26:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:1); but Pi. Pu. not elsewh.) *in the midst of the sea?* RV *like her that is brought to silence.*—DlBaer Ezech p.xi. \& Pr 64 der., hesitantly, fr. II. דמם.

## II דָּמַם (id: BDB02166)

\[Str1826] † II. \[דָּמַם] **vb.** wait (?; cf. As. *damâmu, groan, wail, lament,* DlBaer's Ezech xi; pr 64\)—only **Qal** *Imv.* דֹּ֫מּוּ ישְׁבֵי אִי [Is 23:2](https://ref.ly/Isa23:2) *wail, ye coast dwellers* (cf. Dll. c.), ‖הֵילִילוּ v:[1](https://ref.ly/Isa23:1), \& הֵילִילוּ ישְׁבֵי אִי v:[6](https://ref.ly/Isa23:6); most, however, assign this to I. דמם q. v. (**3**).

## דמן (id: BDB02167)

דמן (√ of foll., mng. dub.; Ar. دَمَنَ, *prepare, improve, manure land,* appar. denom.)

> \[Str1828] † דֹּ֫מֶן **n.m.** dung (Ar. دِمْنٌ) always in this form and always of corpses, lying on ground as offal [2 K 9:37](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:37), [Je 8:2](https://ref.ly/Jer8:2), [9:21](https://ref.ly/Jer9:21), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Jer16:4), [25:33](https://ref.ly/Jer25:33), [Psalm 83:11](https://ref.ly/Ps83:11).

> \[Str1829] † דִּמְנָה **n.pr.loc.** Levitical city in Zebulun [Jos 21:35](https://ref.ly/Josh21:35) (but rd. perh. רִמֹּנָה \+; cf. [I Ch 6:62](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:62), [Jos 19:13](https://ref.ly/Josh19:13), and v. Di).

> \[Str4086] † מַדְמֵן **n.pr.loc.** in Moab [Je 48:2](https://ref.ly/Jer48:2) (on text, however, cf. Che [Is 25:10](https://ref.ly/Isa25:10)).

> \[Str4087] † I. מַדְמֵנָה **n.f.** dung\-place, dung\-pit, במי ´מ [Is 25:10](https://ref.ly/Isa25:10) (Qr ´בְּמוֺ מ).

> \[Str4088] † II. מַדְמֵנָה **n.pr.loc.** in Benjamin, N. of Jerusalem [Is 10:31](https://ref.ly/Isa10:31).

> \[Str4089] † מַדְמַנָּה **1\. n.pr.loc.** city of southern Judah [Jos 15:31](https://ref.ly/Josh15:31); loc. dub., Onom. Μηδεβηνα \= Μηνοεις near Gaza LagOnom. 279, 2nd ed. 276; cf. *Minyay* S. of Gaza RobBR i. 602; on other proposed identif. cf. Di. **2\. n.pr.m.** descendant of Caleb [1 Ch 2:49](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:49).

[Zc 9:1](https://ref.ly/Zech9:1); once (late) ´מֶלֶח ד [2 Ch 24:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:23); note also ´אֲרַם ד [2 S 8:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:5), [8:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:6) \= [1 Ch 18:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:5), [18:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:6); further ´אֱלֽהֵי ד [2 Ch 28:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:23) (‖אֱלֽהֵי מַלְכֵי אֲרָם).

[Am 3:12](https://ref.ly/Amos3:12) עָרֶש ´וּבִד; punct. \& mng. dub.: all ancient Vrss דַּמֶּשֶק, and so Pusey HoffmZAW iii. 102 (‘ in Damascus on a couch ’) al., yet this hardly suitable in context; Thes Hi Ew Baur Ke Gunning RV al. follow MT, \& render *damask, silk,* etc. (Ar. دِمَقْسٌ, connex. with city דַּמֶּשֶק (Ar. دِمَشْقُ) disputed; acc. to Frä40. 288 Ar. دِمَقْسٌ is by metath. from مِقْدَشٌ, \& this a loan\-word from Syr. ܡܝܛܟܣܐ, \& this from Gk. μέταξα (Old Lat. *metaxa*)).

> \[Str1835] דָּן v. sub דין. p. 192

> \[Str1840] דָּנִאֵל v. sub דין. p. 193

## דנג (id: BDB02181)

דנג (√ of foll., mng. unknown).

> \[Str1838] † דִּנְהָבָה **n.pr.loc.** capital city of king Bela in Edom [Gn 36:32](https://ref.ly/Gen36:32) \= [1 Ch 1:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:43); identif. with *Tennib* (perh. \= *Thenib*, near the edge of the Belka, ENE. from Heshbon, described by TristrMoab 222\) by NeubauerAcad. 1891, 260 cf. Tomkinsib. 284\.

## דָּעַךְ (id: BDB02188)

\[Str1846] † \[דָּעַךְ] **vb.** go out, be extinguished (poet. \& esp. Wisd. lit.) (Aram. דְּעִיח, ܕܥܶܟ)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 mpl. דָּֽעֲכוּ [Is 43:17](https://ref.ly/Isa43:17); *Impf.* יִדְעַח [Pr 20:20](https://ref.ly/Prov20:20), יִדְעָח [Jb 18:5](https://ref.ly/Job18:5) \+ 4 t.;—*go out, be extinguished,* of lamp, always fig., lamp of wicked (i.e. prosperity) אוֺר רְשָׁעִים [Jb 18:5](https://ref.ly/Job18:5), ´נֵר ר [Jb 18:6](https://ref.ly/Job18:6), [21:17](https://ref.ly/Job21:17), [Pr 13:9](https://ref.ly/Prov13:9), [20:20](https://ref.ly/Prov20:20), [24:20](https://ref.ly/Prov24:20); of hostile armies [Is 43:17](https://ref.ly/Isa43:17) (‖כָּב֫וּ). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. נִדְעֲכוּ *be made extinct, dried up,* [Jb 6:17](https://ref.ly/Job6:17) (of brooks). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 pl. דֹּעֲכוּ of assailants, *be extinguished, quenched*, כְּאֵשׁ קוֺצִים [Psalm 118:12](https://ref.ly/Ps118:12); but rd. perh. בערו with LXX Bae \& Che, v. Checrit. n.

> \[Str1847] דַּעַת v. sub ידע. p. 395
