## דָּפַק (id: BDB02192)

\[Str1849] † \[דָּפַק] **vb.** beat, knock (Ar. دَفَقَ *pour out*, also *drive* (beasts))—**Qal** *Perf.* 3 mpl. sf. וּדְפָקוּם consec. [Gn 33:13](https://ref.ly/Gen33:13), but rd. 1 s. sf. וּדְפַקְתִּים LXX SyrVer Sam Di *beat* (in driving, drive severely or cruelly); *Pt.* דּוֺפֵק Ct [5:2](https://ref.ly/Song5:2) abs. *knocking* (at door). **Hithp.** *Pt.* מִתְדַּפְּקִים עַל־הַדָּלֶת Ju [19:22](https://ref.ly/Judg19:22) *beat violently* (beat themselves tired) against the door.

> \[Str1850] † דָּפְקָה **n.pr.loc.** first station of Isr. after מדבר סין [Nu 33:12](https://ref.ly/Num33:12), [33:13](https://ref.ly/Num33:13) LXX Π̔αφακα; situation unknown, cf. views in Di. (On an interpret. of name from a stone or metal *Mafkat, Ta\-Mafkat* \= *Mafkat\-* district, v. EbGS 148 f. 552 ff.)

> \[Str1851–52] דַּק, דֹּק v. sub דקק. p. 201

> \[Str1853] † דִּקְלָה **n.pr.m.(loc.)** a son of Joktan, i.e. an Arabian territory or people [Gn 10:27](https://ref.ly/Gen10:27) \= [1 Ch 1:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:21); unknown, cf. Di [Gn 10:27](https://ref.ly/Gen10:27).

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 1:21; Genesis 10:27


## דָּקַק (id: BDB02197)

\[Str1854] † \[דָּקַק] **vb.** crush, pulverise, thresh; be fine (Ar. دَقَّ *be* or *become thin, minute;* Eth. ደቀቀ፡ As. *daḳâḳu* Pa. *break in pieces* COTGloss; Ph. דק *id.,* Aram. דַּקֵּק Pa. *id.,* ܕܰܩ)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. דַּק [Dt 9:21](https://ref.ly/Deut9:21), דָּק [Ex 32:20](https://ref.ly/Exod32:20); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יְדֻקֶָּנּוּ [Is 28:28](https://ref.ly/Isa28:28); 2 ms. תָּדֹק [Is 41:15](https://ref.ly/Isa41:15); —**1\.** *crush* (trans.) sq. acc. לֶחֶם *bread\-stuff, corn* [Is 28:28](https://ref.ly/Isa28:28); of threshing ‖דּוּשׁ [Is 41:15](https://ref.ly/Isa41:15) (fig. of pulverizing mts.) **2\.** only Pf.; *be fine,* of state to wh. Moses reduced the golden calf by grinding וַיִּטְחַן עַד אֲשֶׁר־דָּקָ [Ex 32:20](https://ref.ly/Exod32:20); וָאֶכֹּת אֹתוֺ טָחוֺן הֵיטֵב עַד אֲשֶׁר־דַּק לְעָפָר [Dt 9:21](https://ref.ly/Deut9:21). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֵדַק [2 K 23:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:15), [2 Ch 34:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:4); 2 fs. וַהֲדִקּוֺתְ consec. [Mi 4:13](https://ref.ly/Mic4:13); *Impf.* וַיָּ֫דֶק [2 K 23:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:6), [2 Ch 15:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:16); 1 s. sf. אֲדִקֵּם [2 S 22:43](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:43) (but אֲרִיקֵם ‖ [Psalm 18:43](https://ref.ly/Ps18:43)); *Inf. abs.* הָדֵק [Ex 30:36](https://ref.ly/Exod30:36); *cstr.* לְהֵדַק [2 Ch 34:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7) (form anomalous cf. Ew§ 238 d O1:§ 258 b Be; rd. perh. לְהָדֵק Ki; or לָדַק \[v. כַּדַּק [Is 40:15](https://ref.ly/Isa40:15)] cf. Öt);—*make dust of, pulverize* וַיָּ֫דֶק לְעָפָר [2 K 23:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:6) (i.e. the Ashera), ‖ [2 Ch 34:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:4), [34:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7) (כִּתַּת לְהֵדַק); so of the *bamah* הֵדַק לְעָפָר [2 K 23:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:15); of the מִפְלֶצֶת [2 Ch 15:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:16); fig. [Mi 4:13](https://ref.ly/Mic4:13) (obj. עַמִּים רַבִּים); [Ex 30:36](https://ref.ly/Exod30:36) (P) has וְשָׁחַקְתָּ֫ מִמֶּנָּה הָדֵק *and thou shalt beat some of it fine* (i.e. the incense); [2 S 22:43](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:43) rd. אֲרִיקֵם (‖ [Psalm 18:43](https://ref.ly/Ps18:43)) for אֲדִקֵּם. **Hoph.** *Impf.* יוּדָק [Is 28:28](https://ref.ly/Isa28:28) *be crushed*, subj. לֶחֶם *bread corn* (cf. supr. **Qal**);—on sense cf. Che Di.

> \[Str1851] † דַּק **adj.** thin, small, fine—abs. m. ´דּ [Ex 16:14](https://ref.ly/Exod16:14) \+ 4 t.; דָּק [Lv 13:30](https://ref.ly/Lev13:30); f. דַּקָּה [Lv 16:12](https://ref.ly/Lev16:12), [1 K 19:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:12); pl. f. abs. דַּקּוֺת [Gn 41:6](https://ref.ly/Gen41:6) \+ 2 t.; דַּקֹּת [41:24](https://ref.ly/Gen41:24); cstr. דַּקּוֺת [41:3](https://ref.ly/Gen41:3), דַּקֹּת v:[4](https://ref.ly/Gen41:4); —**1\.** *thin*, of kine בָּשָר ´דּ [Gn 41:3](https://ref.ly/Gen41:3) cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Gen41:4) (Sam has in both רַקּוֺת cf. MT v:[19](https://ref.ly/Gen41:19), v:[20](https://ref.ly/Gen41:20), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Gen41:27)); of ears of corn v:[6](https://ref.ly/Gen41:6), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Gen41:7), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen41:23), v:[24](https://ref.ly/Gen41:24) (all E); *thin, shrunk, withered*, of man [Lv 21:20](https://ref.ly/Lev21:20) (H). **2\.** *small, fine*, of the manna דַּק מְחֻסְפָּס דַּק כַּכְּפֹר [Ex 16:14](https://ref.ly/Exod16:14); of incense [Lv 16:12](https://ref.ly/Lev16:12), hair [13:30](https://ref.ly/Lev13:30) (all P); of dust אָבָק דַּק [Is 29:5](https://ref.ly/Isa29:5); in sim. of isles in hand of ´י, אִיִּים כַּדַּק יִטּוֺל [Is 40:15](https://ref.ly/Isa40:15) (דַּק \= subst. *fine thing, fine dust*); once of a low whisper קוֺל דְּמָמָה דַקָּה [1 K 19:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:12).

> \[Str1852] † דֹּק **n.\[m.]** veil, curtain (as thin), הַנּוֺטֶה כַּדֹּק שָׁמַיִם [Is 40:22](https://ref.ly/Isa40:22) *he who spreadeth out, like a veil, the heavens*.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 40:15; 2 Kings 23:6; Genesis 41:19; Genesis 41:23; 2 Kings 23:15; Genesis 41:24; 2 Chronicles 15:16; 2 Samuel 22:43; Micah 4:13; Leviticus 16:12; Genesis 41:4; Leviticus 21:20; Isaiah 28:28; Exodus 30:36; Isaiah 41:15; Genesis 41:3; Isaiah 29:5; Psalms 18:43; 2 Chronicles 34:7; Genesis 41:7; Genesis 41:6; 2 Chronicles 34:4; Genesis 41:20; Exodus 16:14; Genesis 41:27; Exodus 32:20; Isaiah 40:22; 1 Kings 19:12; Deuteronomy 9:21; Leviticus 13:30


## דָּקַר (id: BDB02200)

\[Str1856] † \[דָּקַר] **vb.** pierce, pierce through (NH *id.*, Aram. דְּקַר, ܕܩܰܪ)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 *pl.* דָּקָרוּ [Zc 12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10); *sf.* וּדְקָרֻנִי consec. [1 S 31:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:4), וּדְקָרֻהוּ consec. [Zc 13:3](https://ref.ly/Zech13:3); *Impf.* וַיִּדְקֹר [Nu 25:8](https://ref.ly/Num25:8), וַיִּדְקְרֵהוּ Ju [9:54](https://ref.ly/Judg9:54)—*pierce, run through* (always c. acc.): as retributive act, Israelite and Midianit. woman [Nu 25:8](https://ref.ly/Num25:8); false prophet [Zc 13:3](https://ref.ly/Zech13:3); but also as speedy death Ju [9:54](https://ref.ly/Judg9:54) (Abimelech); [1 S 31:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:4) a (Saul) \= [1 Ch 10:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:4), also [1 S 31:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:4) b (but del. Be We after [1 Ch 10:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:4) \& so LXXL [1 S 31:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:4) b), see also [Zc 12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10) (cf. [John 19:37](https://ref.ly/John19:37)). **Niph.** *Impf.* יִדָּקֵר *be pierced through*, slain [Is 13:15](https://ref.ly/Isa13:15) (in conquest of Bab. by Medes). **Pu.** *Pt.* pl. מְדֻקָּרִים *pierced, riddled*, (i.e. desperately wounded) warriors, [Je 37:10](https://ref.ly/Jer37:10), slain [Je 51:4](https://ref.ly/Jer51:4) (‖חללים); by hunger [La 4:9](https://ref.ly/Lam4:9) (‖חַלְלֵי רָעָב).

> \[Str1857] † דֶּ֫קֶר **n.pr.m.** (*piercing*, i.e. *sharp weapon ?* Talm. *pick, mattock*) [1 K 4:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:9) בֶּן־דֶּקֶר cf. sub. בן.

> \[Str4094] † \[מַדְקָרָה] **n.f.** piercing, stab, thrust, only *pl. cstr.* כְּמַדְקְרוֺת חָרֶב [Pr 12:18](https://ref.ly/Prov12:18) *like thrusts of a sword*, sim. of rash speaking.

> \[Str1858] דַּר v. דרר

> \[Str1755] דֹּר v. דור

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Samuel 31:4; Proverbs 12:18; Numbers 25:8; 1 Kings 4:9; Isaiah 13:15; 1 Chronicles 10:4; Zechariah 12:10; Jeremiah 37:10; Lamentations 4:9; Judges 9:54; Zechariah 13:3; Jeremiah 51:4; Jonah 19:37
* **Associated Articles:** דרר (ID: `BDB02227@BDBHebrewLexicon`); דּוּר (ID: `BDB02081@BDBHebrewLexicon`)

## דרג (id: BDB02210)

דרג (√ of foll. cf. Ar. دَرَجَ *go on foot, step by step, walk*, دَرِجَ *rise in grade, rank;* NH דרג Hiph. *raise, make high;* Eth. ድርግ፡ is *be connected;* Aram. ܕܰܪܶܓ (verb denom. acc. to PS); also As. *darâgu, be high, lift*, LyonSargontexte 80, *durgu, daragu, path, way* (of steep mountain paths) COTGloss (cf. also דרך); Ar. دَرَجَةٌ *a step, stair*, Aram. דַּרְגָּא, ܕܪܶܓ, ܕܰܪܓܳܐ, *id.*)

> \[Str1864] דָּרוֺם, דְּרוֺר v. דרר

> \[Str1867] † דָּֽרְיָ֫וֶשׁ **n.pr.m.** Darius (Old Pers. *Dârayaua’ush* cf. SpiegAPK 81\)—**1\.** Darius Hystaspis, 522–485 [Ezr 4:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:5), [Hg 1:1](https://ref.ly/Hag1:1), [1:15](https://ref.ly/Hag1:15), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Hag2:10), [Zc 1:1](https://ref.ly/Zech1:1), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Zech1:7), [7:1](https://ref.ly/Zech7:1). **2\.** Darius Codomannus, 336–332 [Ne 12:22](https://ref.ly/Neh12:22). **3\.** ‘ Darius the Mede, ’ [Dn 9:1](https://ref.ly/Dan9:1), [11:1](https://ref.ly/Dan11:1), cf. BevDan. p. 19\.

> \[Str1875] דַּרְיוֺשׁ [Ezr 10:16](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:16) v. דרשׁ

* **Associated Passages:** Haggai 2:10; Ezekiel 38:20; Song of Songs 2:14; Daniel 9:1; Zechariah 1:7; Zechariah 7:1; Daniel 11:1; 1 Chronicles 2:6; Zechariah 1:1; Haggai 1:15; 1 Kings 5:11; Ezra 10:16; Nehemiah 12:22; Haggai 1:1; Ezra 4:5
* **Associated Articles:** דרר (ID: `BDB02227@BDBHebrewLexicon`); דָּרַשׁ (ID: `BDB02233@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## דָּרַךְ (id: BDB02217)

\[Str1869] † דָּרַךְ **vb.** tread, march (Aram. דְּרַח, ܕܪܶܟ; NH Hiph. *lead, conduct;* Ar. IV. ادرك *reach, overtake;* Eth. ደረከ፡ *be rough, serve* (from *calcavit*, Di); መድረክ፡ *threshold, vestibule*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. דָּרַךְ [Nu 24:17](https://ref.ly/Num24:17) \+ 6 t.; 3 fs. דָּֽרְכָה [Jos 14:9](https://ref.ly/Josh14:9); 2 ms. דָּרַכְתָּ Hb [3:15](https://ref.ly/Hab3:15); 1 S. דָּרַכְתִּי [Is 63:3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3), [Zc 9:13](https://ref.ly/Zech9:13); pl. דָּֽרְכוּ [Jb 22:15](https://ref.ly/Job22:15) \+ 3 t.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִדְרֹח [Mi 5:4](https://ref.ly/Mic5:4) \+ 6 t. \+ ידרך [Je 51:3](https://ref.ly/Jer51:3) (כתיב ולא קוי); 3 fs. תִּדְרֹח [Dt 11:24](https://ref.ly/Deut11:24), [Jos 1:3](https://ref.ly/Josh1:3); 2 ms. תִּדְרֹח [Dt 33:29](https://ref.ly/Deut33:29) \+ 2 t.; 2 fs. תִּדְרְכִי Ju [5:21](https://ref.ly/Judg5:21); 1 S. sf. וְאֶדְרְכֵם [Is 63:3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3); 3 mpl. יִדְרְכוּ [1 S 5:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:5), יִדְרְכוּן [Psalm 11:2](https://ref.ly/Ps11:2), וַיִּדְרְכוּ Ju [9:27](https://ref.ly/Judg9:27); 2 mpl. תִּדְרְכוּ [Dt 11:25](https://ref.ly/Deut11:25); *Pt.* act. דֹּרֵח [Am 4:13](https://ref.ly/Amos4:13) \+ 5 t.; דּוֺרֵח [Jb 9:8](https://ref.ly/Job9:8); דֹּרְכִים [Je 25:30](https://ref.ly/Jer25:30), [Ne 13:15](https://ref.ly/Neh13:15); דֹּרְכֵי [Je 46:9](https://ref.ly/Jer46:9) \+ 5 t.; *pass.* f. דְּרוּכָה [Is 21:15](https://ref.ly/Isa21:15); דְּרֻכוֺת [5:28](https://ref.ly/Isa5:28); —**1\.** *tread, march, march forth*, abs., תִּדְרְכִי נַפְשִׁי עֹז Ju [5:21](https://ref.ly/Judg5:21); cf. דָּרַךְ כּוֺכָב מִיַּעֲקֹב [Nu 24:17](https://ref.ly/Num24:17) (poem) *a star hath marched forth from Jacob*. **2\.** *tread upon* (land), sq. בְּ [Dt 1:36](https://ref.ly/Deut1:36), [11:25](https://ref.ly/Deut11:25) (subj. Pers.), [Jos 14:9](https://ref.ly/Josh14:9) (subj. רֶגֶל) cf. [Dt 11:24](https://ref.ly/Deut11:24) \= [Jos 1:3](https://ref.ly/Josh1:3) (subj. כַּף רַגְלְכֶם); tread in a path, sq. בְּ [Is 59:8](https://ref.ly/Isa59:8) (fig. of mode of life); tread in (on) the sea, sq. בְּ Hb [3:15](https://ref.ly/Hab3:15) (but txt. dub. cf. VB); of an invader יִדְרֹח בְּאַרְמְנוֺתֵינוּ [Mi 5:4](https://ref.ly/Mic5:4) (‖יָבוֺא בְּאַרְצֵנוּ); בִּגְבלֵינוּ ´יִד v:[5](https://ref.ly/Mic5:5) (‖ *id.*); sq. עַל [1 S 5:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:5) (עַל־מִפְתַּן דָּגוֺן); [Psalm 91:3](https://ref.ly/Ps91:3) (עַל־שַׁחַל וָפֶתֶן); esp. (poet.) עַל־בָּמֳו֯תֵי אֶרֶץ [Mi 1:3](https://ref.ly/Mic1:3), of ´י, so [Am 4:13](https://ref.ly/Amos4:13), \& (עַל־בָּמֳתֵי ָ\+ים) [Jb 9:8](https://ref.ly/Job9:8); of Isr. treading on heights of enemies, i.e. subduing them [Dt 33:29](https://ref.ly/Deut33:29). Once sq. acc. (אֲשֶׁר ref. אֹרַח) [Jb 22:15](https://ref.ly/Job22:15). In technical senses **3\.** *tread* wine\- (or oil\-) press, sq. acc. יְקָבִים [Jb 24:11](https://ref.ly/Job24:11) (‖יַצְהִירוּ), גִּתּוֺת [Ne 13:15](https://ref.ly/Neh13:15); sq. acc. of thing pressed עֲנָבִים ´ד [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13); also of product וְתִירוֺשׁ…תִּדְרֹח־זַיִת [Mi 6:15](https://ref.ly/Mic6:15); יַיִן בַּיְקָבִים לאֹ יִדְרֹח הַדֹּרֵח [Is 16:10](https://ref.ly/Isa16:10); sq. בְּגַת [Is 63:2](https://ref.ly/Isa63:2) (in sim.); abs. [Je 25:30](https://ref.ly/Jer25:30) (sim.) [48:33](https://ref.ly/Jer48:33); fig. of judgment [Is 63:3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3) sq. acc. פּוּרָה *winepress*, subj. ´י, cf. v:[3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3) וְאֶדְרְכֵם בְּאַפִּי (‖רמס), i.e. *tread them down;* so גַּת דָּרַךְ אֲדֹנָי לִבְתוּלַת בַּת־יְהוּדָה[La 1:15](https://ref.ly/Lam1:15). **4\.** *tread* (i.e. bend) the bow, sq. acc. קֶשֶׁת [Je 51:8](https://ref.ly/Jer51:8) (אֶל־יִדְרֹח יד֯רך הַדֹּרֵח קַשְׁתּוֺ); fig. of assaults of wicked [Psalm 11:2](https://ref.ly/Ps11:2), [37:14](https://ref.ly/Ps37:14), of judgments of ´י [Psalm 7:18](https://ref.ly/Ps7:18), [La 2:4](https://ref.ly/Lam2:4), [3:12](https://ref.ly/Lam3:12); also fig. of Judah as bow in hand of ´י, כִּי דָרַכְתִּי לִי יְהוּדָה [Zc 9:13](https://ref.ly/Zech9:13) (‖קֶשֶׁת מִלֵּאתִי אֶפְרַיִם); twice in like mng. c. obj. חֵץ *arrow* [Psalm 58:8](https://ref.ly/Ps58:8) (but on txt. cf. Checrit. n.) [64:4](https://ref.ly/Ps64:4); דֹּרְכֵי קֶשֶׁת \= *bow\-benders, archers* [Je 50:14](https://ref.ly/Jer50:14), [50:29](https://ref.ly/Jer50:29), [1 Ch 5:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:18), [8:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:40), [2 Ch 14:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:7); קֶשֶׁת דְּרוּכָה *bent bow* [Is 21:15](https://ref.ly/Isa21:15); קַשְׁתַֹתָיו דְּרֻכוֺת [5:28](https://ref.ly/Isa5:28). **Hiph.** *Pf.* וְהִדְרִיח [Is 11:15](https://ref.ly/Isa11:15); sf. הִדְרִיכָהּ [Je 51:33](https://ref.ly/Jer51:33) (where many Inf. cstr.) cf. RSJ Ph xvi. (1888\) 72; 1 S. sf. הִדְרַכְתִּיךָ [Pr 4:11](https://ref.ly/Prov4:11); 3 pl. sf. הִדְרִיכָֻהוּ Ju [20:43](https://ref.ly/Judg20:43), הִדְרִיכוּהוּ [Jb 28:8](https://ref.ly/Job28:8); *Impf.* יַדְרֵח [Psalm 25:9](https://ref.ly/Ps25:9), sf. יַדְרִכֵנִי Hb [3:19](https://ref.ly/Hab3:19); וַיַּדְרִיכֵם [Psalm 107:7](https://ref.ly/Ps107:7); 1 S. sf. אַדְרִיכֵם [Is 42:16](https://ref.ly/Isa42:16); וַיַּדְרְכוּ [Je 9:2](https://ref.ly/Jer9:2) (cf. Ges§ 53, 3, R 4\); *Imv.* ms. sf. הַדְרִיכֵנִי [Psalm 25:5](https://ref.ly/Ps25:5), [119:35](https://ref.ly/Ps119:35); *Inf. cstr.* v. supr.; *Pt.* sf. מַדְרִיכְךָ [Is 48:17](https://ref.ly/Isa48:17); —**1\.** *tread, tread down*, of treading down enemy in battle Ju [20:43](https://ref.ly/Judg20:43); of treading a path [Jb 28:8](https://ref.ly/Job28:8); levelling threshing\-floor [Je 51:33](https://ref.ly/Jer51:33) (all sq. sf.). **2\.** *tread* (bend) the bow (\= **Qal 4**), only fig. וַיַּדְרְכוּ אֶת־לְשׁוֺנָם קַשְׁתָּם שֶׁקֶר [Je 9:2](https://ref.ly/Jer9:2) *and they have bent their tongue as their bow in falsehood*. **3\.** causat., *cause to tread or march, lead*, returning exiles [Is 11:15](https://ref.ly/Isa11:15) (subj. ´י; no obj. expressed); cf. וַיַּדְרִיכֵם בְּדֶרֶח יְשָׁרָה [Psalm 107:7](https://ref.ly/Ps107:7) *and he led them in a straight way;* v. also [Is 42:16](https://ref.ly/Isa42:16) (‖הוֺלִיח); with a moral application הִדְרַכְתִּיךָ בְּמַעְגְּלֵי־ישֶׁר [Pr 4:11](https://ref.ly/Prov4:11); cf. [Psalm 119:35](https://ref.ly/Ps119:35) הַדְרִיכֵנִי בִּנְתִיב מִצְוֺתֶיךָ *lead me in the path of thy commandments;* [Psalm 25:5](https://ref.ly/Ps25:5) בַּאֲמִתֶָּךָ (‖לַמְּדֵנִי) cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ps25:9) בַּמִּשְׁפָּט (יְלַמֵּד דַּרְכּוֺ, [Is 48:17](https://ref.ly/Isa48:17) בְּדֶרֶח תֵּלֵח (‖לְהוֺעִל מְלַמֶּדְךָ); once of giving security \& triumph עַל־בָּמוֺתַי יַדְרִכֵנִי Hb [3:19](https://ref.ly/Hab3:19) *upon mine heights he maketh me tread* (cf. **Qal 2**).

> \[Str4096] † \[מִדְרָךְ] **n.\[m.]** treading\- or stepping\-place, place for the foot to tread on, only cstr. מִדְרַח כַּף־רָגֶל [Dt 2:5](https://ref.ly/Deut2:5) *a treading\-place for the sole of a foot* (cf. [11:24](https://ref.ly/Deut11:24) \= [Jos 1:3](https://ref.ly/Josh1:3)).

> \[Str150,187l] † \[דַּרְכְּמוֺן, אֲדַרְכּוֺן (א prosthet.)] **n.\[m.]** unit (appar. of weight, certainly) of value, rare \& late, perh. drachma, others daric, v. infr.:—only pl. דַּרְכְּמוֺנִים of gold [Ezr 2:69](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:69) (‖מָנִים of silver) \= [Ne 7:70](https://ref.ly/Neh7:70) (‖ *id.*); so דַּרְכְּמֹנִים [Ne 7:69](https://ref.ly/Neh7:69), [7:71](https://ref.ly/Neh7:71); also אֲדַרְכֹּנִים of gold money [1 Ch 29:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:7) (‖כִּכָּרִים; ´כ also in same v. of silver, brass \& iron); of weight (or worth) of gold utensils [Ezr 8:27](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:27).—Weight of Gk. δραχμή \= 4\.32 grammes (\= 66\.5 Eng. gr.); value of silver dr.\=c. 9 3/4 **d.** Eng.; value of gold dr. (1/2 stater) \= c. 9 *s.* 5 *d.* Eng., cf. HultschGr. u. Röm. Metrol. (1882\) 224, 227, 230–250, \& Tab. xiv, xvi.—(If ´ד \= *drachma*, then perhaps edit. insertion in Ne Ezr (regarded as loan\-word in both Gk. \& Heb. fr. some Asiatic source by EwGGA 1855, 1392 ff.; 1856, 798; Gesch. i. 274, H. i. 189 cf. SmListen 18, N. 24, but on Gk. deriv. cf. Lex. Lidd. \& Sc., also BrandisMünz\-Mass\-u. Gewichtssytem 58 f. Hultsch1. c. 131\); cf. Ph. pl. דרכמנם, דרכנם \= *drachmæ* acc. to ReRa 1888, 7 BergerMêm. Soc. Ling. de Paris, 1889, 385 HoffmAGG xxxvi Mai, 1889, 8\. Acc. to view commonly current hitherto ´ד \= *daric*, Gk. δαρεικός cf. Syr. ܕܪܝܺܟܽܘܢܳܐ, Pers. gold coin \= c. Eng. sovereign (weight \= c. 2 drachm.): Brandisl. c. 62, 244 ff. Hultschl. c. 466 Schr in RiHWB Art. Darike ErmanZPV ii, 75 HoffmZA 1887, 49 ff. (Hoffml. c. abandons), cf. Ryle [Ezr 2:69](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:69).)

## דרע (id: BDB02222)

† דרע (acc. to Thes connected with Aram. דְּרָע, אֶדְרָע *arm*, Heb. זְרוֺעַ, whence foll. in sense *strong*, of fortified city; this, however, is dub.)

> \[Str154] † אֶדְרֶ֫עִי **n.pr.loc.** a chief city of Bashan אֶדְרֶ֭עִי [Jos 19:37](https://ref.ly/Josh19:37); אֶדְרֶָעִי [Nu 21:33](https://ref.ly/Num21:33) \= [Dt 3:1](https://ref.ly/Deut3:1), [Dt 3:10](https://ref.ly/Deut3:10); בְּעַשְׁתָּרֹת בְּאֶדְרֶָעִי [Dt 1:4](https://ref.ly/Deut1:4); בְּעַשְׁתָּרֹת וּבְאֶדְרֶָעִי [Jos 12:4](https://ref.ly/Josh12:4), [13:12](https://ref.ly/Josh13:12) cf. [13:31](https://ref.ly/Josh13:31); —on identity of all these cf. Di [Dt 3:10](https://ref.ly/Deut3:10); modern *Der‘ât*, 7 1/2 hours WNW. of Bosra; Euseb. Ἀδραά (LagOnom. 213, 86 2nd ed. 235\) cf. Di [Nu 21:33](https://ref.ly/Num21:33) WetzstHauran 47, 77, 123 BDPal 201\.

> \[Str1873] דָּ֫רַע [1 Ch 2:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:6) v. דרדע. p. 201

* **Associated Passages:** Joshua 13:31; 1 Chronicles 2:6; Numbers 21:33; Joshua 12:4; Deuteronomy 1:4; Joshua 13:12; Deuteronomy 3:10; Joshua 19:37; Deuteronomy 3:1


## דרק (id: BDB02225)

דרק (√ of foll. mng. dub.; Ar. دَرَقَ \= *walk rapidly, hasten*, دَرَقَةٌ *shield*).

> \[Str1874] † דַּרְקוֺן **n.pr.m.** head of a family of Nethinim, who went up with Zerubbabel, ´בְּנֵי־ד [Ezr 2:56](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:56) \= [Ne 7:58](https://ref.ly/Neh7:58).

* **Associated Passages:** Ezra 2:56; Nehemiah 7:58


## דרר (id: BDB02227)

דרר (√ of foll. words: Ar. دَرَّ;—**1\.** *stream, flow abundantly* (of milk, tears, rain, etc.), cf. I. דְּרוֺר. **2\.** *be abundant, luxuriant* (of her\-bage), cf. دَرْدَارٌ a kind of tree, now *elm* (Lane), \& דַּרְדַּר. **3\.** *run vehemently, easily* (of horse), cf. دَرِيرٌ *swift* horse, or other beast, \& II. דְּרוֺר. **4\.** *give light, shine* (of lamp), cf. كَوْكَبٌ دُرِّىٌّ *shining star*, دُرِّىٌّ *glistening* or *shining* of sword, دُرٌّ *pearls* (coll.) \& דַּר; hence prob. דָּרוֺם).

> \[Str1858] † דַּר **n.\[m.]** pearl ? mother of pearl ? (Ar. دُرٌّ *pearls*, دُرَّةٌ *a pearl*) [Est 1:6](https://ref.ly/Esth1:6).

> \[Str1865] † I. דְּרוֺר **n.\[m.]** a flowing; free run, liberty;—**1\.** מָר־דְּרוֺר [Ex 30:23](https://ref.ly/Exod30:23) (P) i.e. *myrrh of flowing*, fine\-flowing m. cf. מֹר \& Di. **2\.** c. קרא *proclaim liberty*, וּקְרָאתֶם דְּרוֺר בָּאָרֶץ לְכָל־ישְׁבֶָיהָ [Lv 25:10](https://ref.ly/Lev25:10) (H) in Jubilee year, cf. [Is 61:1](https://ref.ly/Isa61:1) (‖פְּקַח־קוֺחַ); [Je 34:8](https://ref.ly/Jer34:8), [34:15](https://ref.ly/Jer34:15), [34:17](https://ref.ly/Jer34:17) all ref. to liberty of Sabbatical year; v:[17](https://ref.ly/Jer34:17) (iron.) *lo ! I proclaim to you liberty unto the sword, unto pestilence \& unto famine*, i.e. liberty to be destroyed by sword, pestilence and famine, (a judgment of Yahweh); ´שְׁנַת־הַדּ Ez [46:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:17) i.e. year of Jubilee (cf [Lv 25:10](https://ref.ly/Lev25:10)).

> \[Str1866] † II. דְּרוֺר **n.f.**:[Psalm 84:4](https://ref.ly/Ps84:4) swallow [Psalm 84:4](https://ref.ly/Ps84:4) (‖צִפּוֺר), Che (q.v.) makes simile of quiet, peace, security, and supplies ellipsis; in simile, [Pr 26:2](https://ref.ly/Prov26:2) כַּצִפּוֺר לָנוּד כַּדְּרוֺר לָעוּף (of groundless curse, which does not alight, i.e. is not fulfilled).

> \[Str1864] † דָּרוֺם **n.m.** south (poet. \& late) (on formation cf. Ol:§ 216 a Sta§ 295\)—דָּרוֺם [Jb 37:17](https://ref.ly/Job37:17) \+ 14 t. \+ Ez [40:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:27) b, [40:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:28) b (del. SyrVer Co in v:[27](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:27) b; AB Vulgate Co in v:[28](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:28) b—*south*, Ez [42:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:18) (over against קָדִים, צָפוֺן, יָם); 12 t. more in Ezek. (of wh. Co del. 2, cf. supr.) [21:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:2) (\= תֵּימָנָה and נֶגֶב); ´דֶּרֶךְ הַדּ *toward south* [40:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:24), [40:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:24), [40:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:27), rd. also ´דֶּרֶךְ הַדּ [42:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:10) for הַקָּדִים ´ד LXX Ke Co; ´שַׁעַר הַדּ *south gate* [40:\[27]](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:27), [40:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:28), [40:\[28]](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:28) cf. Co supr., [40:44](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:44) (opp. צפון; also קדים), v:[45](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:45) (opp. צפון) [42:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:12) \= ´לַדָּ [41:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:11) (opp. צפון), ´לִשְׁכוֺת הד [42:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:13) (opp. צפון). Elsewhere only [Dt 33:23](https://ref.ly/Deut33:23) (‖יַם), [Jb 37:17](https://ref.ly/Job37:17); [Ec 1:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:6), [11:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:3) (both opp. צפון).

> \[Str1863] † דַּרְדַּר **n.\[m.]** thistles (coll.) (Ar. دردار, still current in Pal., Löwp. 100; Eth. ደንደር፡) קוֺץ וְדַרְדַּר [Gn 3:18](https://ref.ly/Gen3:18) (J) [Ho 10:8](https://ref.ly/Hos10:8) symbol of wildness, desert.

Ju [6:29](https://ref.ly/Judg6:29), [Dt 13:15](https://ref.ly/Deut13:15), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Deut17:4), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Deut17:9), [19:18](https://ref.ly/Deut19:18); with acc. כָּל־לְבָבוֺת ´דּוֺרֵשׁ י *all hearts Yahweh searches* [1 Ch 28:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:9); לִדְרשׁ הַמּוֺפֵת *to inquire into the wonder* [2 Ch 32:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:31); with לְ, *to inquire about* [2 S 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:3), [Jb 10:6](https://ref.ly/Job10:6); sq. עַל Pers. \+ inf. [2 Ch 24:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:6) *apply to the Levites to bring*, etc., cf. [31:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:9) (עַל both Pers. and rei). **5\.** *ask for, require, demand*, c. acc. rei Ez [20:40](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:40); \+ מִן Pers. [Dt 23:22](https://ref.ly/Deut23:22), [Mi 6:8](https://ref.ly/Mic6:8); *exact*, oft. with collat. idea of *avenging*, מֵעִם [Dt 18:19](https://ref.ly/Deut18:19), so with מִיַּד Ez [34:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:10), דָּרַשׁ דָּם מִיַּד [Gn 9:5](https://ref.ly/Gen9:5) (P) Ez [33:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:6); acc. only, דֹּרֵשׁ דמים [Psalm 9:13](https://ref.ly/Ps9:13), רִשְׁעוֺ ´ד [10:15](https://ref.ly/Ps10:15); abs. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ps10:4), v:[13](https://ref.ly/Ps10:13), [2 Ch 24:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:22). **6\.** *seek with application, study, follow, practise*, abs. by wisdom [Ec 1:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:13); with acc. מִשְׁפָּט ´ד *study* or *practise justice* [Is 1:17](https://ref.ly/Isa1:17), [16:5](https://ref.ly/Isa16:5); ´מַעֲשֵי י [Psalm 111:2](https://ref.ly/Ps111:2); טוֺב וְאַל־רַע [Am 5:14](https://ref.ly/Amos5:14); commands of God [Psalm 119:45](https://ref.ly/Ps119:45), [119:94](https://ref.ly/Ps119:94), [119:155](https://ref.ly/Ps119:155), [1 Ch 28:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:8); the law Ezr [7:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:10); † שׁלום ´ד *seek* or *study the peace, welfare* of any one [Dt 23:7](https://ref.ly/Deut23:7), [Je 29:7](https://ref.ly/Jer29:7), [Ezr 9:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:12), לשׁלום [Je 38:4](https://ref.ly/Jer38:4), טוב ´ד [Est 10:3](https://ref.ly/Esth10:3), רעה ´ד [Pr 11:27](https://ref.ly/Prov11:27), [Psalm 38:13](https://ref.ly/Ps38:13) (cf. בקשׁ **2,** where this use is more common). **7\.** *seek with care, care for*, ארץ אשׁר יהוה אלהיך דֹּרֵשׁ אתהּ *land which Yahweh thy God careth for* [Dt 11:12](https://ref.ly/Deut11:12); אַל־יִדְרְשֵׁהוּ אֱלוֺהַּ *let not Eloah care for it* [Jb 3:4](https://ref.ly/Job3:4); ´אוֺתָח לאֹ יִד [Je 30:14](https://ref.ly/Jer30:14); דָּֽרְשָׁה צֶמֶר [Pr 31:13](https://ref.ly/Prov31:13); with לְ [Je 30:17](https://ref.ly/Jer30:17); אֵין דּוֺרֵשׁ לְנַפְשִׁי *no one careth for me* [Psalm 142:5](https://ref.ly/Ps142:5); but in both these לְ perh. belongs to אֵין, cf. ‖אֵין לִי מַכִּיר [Psalm 142:5](https://ref.ly/Ps142:5), also [La 4:4](https://ref.ly/Lam4:4) etc.; דְּרוּשָׁה *cared for* (of Jerus.) [Is 62:12](https://ref.ly/Isa62:12). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִדְרָשׁ [Gn 42:22](https://ref.ly/Gen42:22), נִדְרַשְׁתִּי [Is 65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1), נִדְרָשׁוּ [1 Ch 26:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:31); *Impf.* אִדָּרֵשׁ Ez [14:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:3), [20:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:3), [20:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:31), [20:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:31), [36:37](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:37); *Inf.* abs. הַאִדָּרשׁ Ez [14:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:3) (for ההדרשׁ);—**1\.** *let oneself be inquired of, consulted*, only of God Ez [14:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:3), [14:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:3), [20:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:3), [20:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:31), [20:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:31), [36:37](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:37), [Is 65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1). **2\.** *be sought out* [1 Ch 26:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:31). **3\.** *be required*, of blood (cf. **Qal 5**) [Gn 42:22](https://ref.ly/Gen42:22) (E).

 *Note.*—וְדָרְשׁוּ מֵחָרְבוֺתֵיהֶם *and beg out of their desolate places* [Psalm 109:10](https://ref.ly/Ps109:10) (Ki al. *wedŏršu*, vid. Baer); many MSS. rd. דָּֽרְשׁוּ, so most interpr.; LXX Hup BI Che rd. גֹּרְשׁוּ *be driven from*. 

> \[Str4097] † \[מִדְרָשׁ] **n.\[m.]** study, exposition, midrash, only cstr. מִדְרַשׁ (late; common in NH, in sense of *imaginative exposition* or *didactic story*)—מִדְרַשׁ סֵפֶר הַמְּלָכִים *midrash of the book of Kings* [2 Ch 24:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:27); מִדְרַשׁ הַנָּבִיא עִדּוֺ *midrash of the prophet Iddo* [2 Ch 13:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:22). ## דָּשָׁא (id: BDB02235)

\[Str1876] † \[דָּשָׁא] **vb.** sprout, shoot, grow green (As. *dašû* Pi *make abundant* LyonSargontexte 77; cf. also sub דֶּשֶׁא, whence, acc. to others, vb. denom.)—**Qal** *Pf.* דָּֽשְׁאוּ [Jo 2:22](https://ref.ly/Joel2:22) (subj. נְאוֺת מִדְבָּר). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 fs. תַּדְשֵׁא (juss.) [Gn 1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11) *cause to sprout* or *shoot forth* תַּדְשֵׁא הָאָרֶץ דֶּשֶׁא.—דָּשָׁא [Je 50:11](https://ref.ly/Jer50:11) v. sub דּוּשׁ.

> \[Str1877] † דֶּ֫שֶׁא **n.m.**:[Is 15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6) grass (NH *id.*, As. *disûu, herb*, LyonSargontexte 69; Sab. דתַא *fresh shoots* DHMZMG 1875, 597 \= *springtime* CISiv. p. 11; Aram. דִּתְאָה, ܬܰܕܐܴܐ (cf. LagBN 130\))—´ד abs. [Gn 1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11)\+ 13 t.—acc. cogn. תַּדְשֵׁא [Gn 1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11); cf. v:[12](https://ref.ly/Gen1:12) (in both, produced by earth); springing out of earth [2 S 23:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:4); of a second crop of grass [Pr 21:25](https://ref.ly/Prov21:25) (opp. חָצִיר); caused to spring forth by God לְהַצְמִיחַ מֹצָא דֶָשֶׁא [Jb 38:27](https://ref.ly/Job38:27); refreshed by rain [Dt 32:2](https://ref.ly/Deut32:2); ´נְאוֺת ד [Psalm 23:2](https://ref.ly/Ps23:2); as food of wild ass [Jb 6:5](https://ref.ly/Job6:5); as failing (withered) [Is 15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6); lacking for animals [Je 14:5](https://ref.ly/Jer14:5); וִירַק דֶּשֶׁא sim. of weakness [2 K 19:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:26) (‖עֵשֶב שָדֶה) \= [Is 37:27](https://ref.ly/Isa37:27); of transitoriness (withering) [Psalm 37:2](https://ref.ly/Ps37:2) (´יֶרֶק ד); of growth and prosperity, [Is 66:14](https://ref.ly/Isa66:14).

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 1:12; Psalms 37:2; Job 6:5; Isaiah 37:27; Proverbs 21:25; Isaiah 66:14; Psalms 23:2; Jeremiah 50:11; Genesis 1:11; 2 Samuel 23:4; Joel 2:22; Jeremiah 14:5; Deuteronomy 32:2; Isaiah 15:6; 2 Kings 19:26; Job 38:27


## דָּשֵׁן (id: BDB02237)

\[Str1878] † דָּשֵׁן **vb.** be fat, grow fat (Ar. دَسِمَ; whence also دَسَمٌ *grease, fat;* NH דִּשֵּׁן *make fat*, cf. NH דֶּשֶׁן *fat*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְדָשֵׁן consec. [Dt 31:20](https://ref.ly/Deut31:20) (JE) fig. of Isr.'s prosperity. **Pi.** *Pf.* דִּשַּׁנְתָּ [Psalm 23:5](https://ref.ly/Ps23:5); וְדִשְּׁנוּ consec. [Nu 4:13](https://ref.ly/Num4:13); *Impf.*: יְדַשְּׁנֶה־סֶּֽלָה [Psalm 20:4](https://ref.ly/Ps20:4) volunt. (cf. Ges§ 48, 3 De; but perh. rd. sf. נֶהָ\-, נָהּ\- v. Ki Geel. c.); 3 fs. תְּדַשֶּׁ־ן [Pr 15:30](https://ref.ly/Prov15:30); *Inf.* לְדַשְּׁנוֺ [Ex 27:3](https://ref.ly/Exod27:3)—causat. *make fat* בשׁמן ´ד ראשׁי i.e. anoint, symbol of festivity and joy [Psalm 23:5](https://ref.ly/Ps23:5); עָצֶם ´ד [Pr 15:30](https://ref.ly/Prov15:30) of bodily effect of good news; עולה ´ד *find* a burnt\-offering *fat* \= acceptable [Psalm 20:4](https://ref.ly/Ps20:4); elsewhere *denom.* fr. דֶּשֶׁן (fat ashes);—*take away, clear away the fat ashes* (acc. of altar cleared) [Nu 4:13](https://ref.ly/Num4:13) (P), so סירות לְדַשְּׁנוֺ [Ex 27:3](https://ref.ly/Exod27:3) (P). **Pu.** *Impf.* יְדֻשָּׁן [Pr 28:25](https://ref.ly/Prov28:25), [Is 34:7](https://ref.ly/Isa34:7); 3 fs. תְּדֻשָּׁן [Pr 11:25](https://ref.ly/Prov11:25), [13:4](https://ref.ly/Prov13:4)—pass. of causat. **Pi.** *be made fat*, of dust saturated מֵחַלֶב [Is 34:7](https://ref.ly/Isa34:7); fig. of prosperity of the liberal [Pr 11:25](https://ref.ly/Prov11:25), the diligent [13:4](https://ref.ly/Prov13:4), the trustful [28:25](https://ref.ly/Prov28:25). **Hothp.** *Pf.* 3 fs. הֻדַּ֫שְׁנָה (cf. O1:§ 271 Ges§ 54\. 3\) [Is 34:6](https://ref.ly/Isa34:6), of Yahweh's sword:—*it hath fattened itself* מֵחֶלֶב (‖מָֽלְאָה דָּם

> \[Str1880] † דֶּ֫שֶׁן **n.m.** fatness, fat ashes—abs. ´ד [Psalm 63:6](https://ref.ly/Ps63:6) \+ 8 t., דָּשֶׁן [Lv 1:16](https://ref.ly/Lev1:16) \+ 3 t.; cstr. דֶּשֶׁן [Psalm 36:9](https://ref.ly/Ps36:9); sf. דִּשְׁנִי Ju [9:9](https://ref.ly/Judg9:9)—**1\.** *fatness*, abundance, luxuriance, oil, Ju [9:9](https://ref.ly/Judg9:9) (of olive tree); abundance, fertility [Psalm 63:6](https://ref.ly/Ps63:6) (in simile ‖חֵלֶב), [65:12](https://ref.ly/Ps65:12), of food and drink, [Jb 36:16](https://ref.ly/Job36:16), [Je 31:14](https://ref.ly/Jer31:14); passing over into fig. of spiritual blessing [Psalm 36:9](https://ref.ly/Ps36:9) (ביתך ´ד), [Is 55:2](https://ref.ly/Isa55:2). **2\.** *fat ashes*, i.e. ashes of victims, mixed with the fat [Lv 1:16](https://ref.ly/Lev1:16), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Lev4:12), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Lev4:12), [6:3](https://ref.ly/Lev6:3), [6:4](https://ref.ly/Lev6:4) (all P) [Je 31:40](https://ref.ly/Jer31:40), [1 Ki 13:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:3), [13:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:5).

> \[Str1879] † דָּשֵׁן **adj.** fat, דָּשֵׁן [Is 30:23](https://ref.ly/Isa30:23) (‖שָׁמֵן), of לֶחֶם as product of ground; fig. of righteous as trees דְּשֵׁנִים [Psalm 92:15](https://ref.ly/Ps92:15) (‖רעננים) fat, full of oil (?) or sap (Che; cf. דֶּשֶׁן Ju [9:9](https://ref.ly/Judg9:9)); as subst. *vigorous, stalwart ones* (opp. יוֺרְדֵי עָפָר cf. Che) [Psalm 22:30](https://ref.ly/Ps22:30) דִּשְׁנֵ־י אֶרֶץ (Brüll שֹׁכְנֵי, RenanHist. iii. 134 ישְׁבֵי).

> \[Str1881] † דָּת **n.f.**:[Est 3:15](https://ref.ly/Esth3:15) decree, law, usage, only in Persian period (BAram. NH *id.*, Syr. ܕܰܬܳܐ or ܕܳܬܳܐ; Pers. loan\-w., Old Pers. *dâta, law*, SpiegAPK 225\)—´ד abs. [Est 1:8](https://ref.ly/Esth1:8) \+ 9 t.; cstr. דַּת [Est 2:12](https://ref.ly/Esth2:12), דָּת [9:13](https://ref.ly/Esth9:13); sf. דָּתוֺ [Est 2:8](https://ref.ly/Esth2:8) \+ 4 t.; pl. cstr. דָּתֵי [Ezr 8:36](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:36) \+ 2 t.; sf. דָּתַיהֶם [Est 3:8](https://ref.ly/Esth3:8); —**1\.** *decree, edict, commission* of Pers. king [Ezr 8:36](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:36) (word elsewhere only in Est) [Est 2:8](https://ref.ly/Esth2:8) (‖דְּבַר הַמֶּלֶךְ), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Esth4:3) (‖ *id.*) [8:17](https://ref.ly/Esth8:17) (‖ *id.*) [9:1](https://ref.ly/Esth9:1) (‖ *id.;* c. עשה *execute*) cf. also [9:13](https://ref.ly/Esth9:13); c. vb. נָתַן [3:14](https://ref.ly/Esth3:14), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Esth3:15), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Esth8:13), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Esth8:14), [9:14](https://ref.ly/Esth9:14); written כְּתָב־הַדָּת [4:8](https://ref.ly/Esth4:8) (cf. [3:14](https://ref.ly/Esth3:14), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Esth8:13)). **2\.** *law*, permanently valid and applicable, וְיִכָּתֵב בְּדָתֵי פָּרַסאוּמָדַי וְלאֹ יַעֲבוֺר [Est 1:19](https://ref.ly/Esth1:19) cf. v:[15](https://ref.ly/Esth1:15), [4:11](https://ref.ly/Esth4:11), [4:16](https://ref.ly/Esth4:16); יֹדְעֵי דָּת וָדִין [1:13](https://ref.ly/Esth1:13) *those that understood law and judgment;* דָּתֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ [3:8](https://ref.ly/Esth3:8); of laws of the Jews in Persia [3:8](https://ref.ly/Esth3:8); even of rules for drinking at a feast [1:8](https://ref.ly/Esth1:8); for purifying of women [2:12](https://ref.ly/Esth2:12).—The distinction between **1** \& **2** is not absolute. The king's will was law, and the royal edict (דְּבַר מַלְכוּת) concerning Vashti was reckoned among the laws (דָּתֵי) of the Persians \& Medes ([1:19](https://ref.ly/Esth1:19)).—In [Dt 33:2](https://ref.ly/Deut33:2) MT אֵשׁ דָּת לָמוֺ *fire was a law for them* is corrupt; many emend. proposed, cf. Di; Di suggests דֹת\[לַפִּ]אֵשׁ *fire of flames*, flaming fire, cf. הַלַּפִּידִם [Ex 20:18](https://ref.ly/Exod20:18) (but לַפִּדֹת pl. in ת—ֹ not in OT unless as n.pr.), or (better), דֶת\[יֹקֶ]אֵשׁ *a burning fire*, cf. [Is 65:5](https://ref.ly/Isa65:5) (diff. sense).

> \[Str1885] † דָּתָן **n.pr.m.** (cf.
